




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


naittippakaraṇapāḷi


1. saṅgahavārau

yaṃ  laukau pūjayatai, salaukapālau sadā namassati ca|

tassaita sāsanavaraṃ, vidūhi ñaiyyaṃ naravarassa||

dvādasa padāni suttaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ byañ‍janañ‍ca atthau ca|

taṃ viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ ubhayaṃ, kau atthau byañ‍janaṃ katamaṃ||

sauḷasahārā naitti naittī (ka॰), pañ‍canayā sāsanassa pariyaiṭṭhi|

aṭṭhārasamūlapadā, mahakac‍cānaina mahākac‍cānaina (sī॰) niddiṭṭhā||

hārā  byañ‍janavicayau, suttassa nayā tayau ca suttatthau|

ubhayaṃ pariggahītaṃ, vuc‍cati suttaṃ yathāsuttaṃ||

yā caiva daisanā yañ‍ca, daisitaṃ ubhayamaiva viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ|

tatrāyamānupubbī, navavidhasuttantapariyaiṭṭhīti||

saṅgahavārau|



2. uddaisavārau

1. tattha katamai sauḷasa hārā? daisanā vicayau yutti padaṭṭhānau lakkhaṇau catubyūhau āvaṭṭau vibhatti parivattanau vaivacanau pañ‍ñatti  autaraṇau saudhanau adhiṭṭhānau parikkhārau samāraupanau iti|

tassānugīti

daisanā  vicayau yutti, padaṭṭhānau ca lakkhaṇau|

catubyūhau ca āvaṭṭau, vibhatti parivattanau||

vaivacanau ca pañ‍ñatti, autaraṇau ca saudhanau|

adhiṭṭhānau parikkhārau, samāraupanau sauḷasau sauḷasa (sī॰)||

aitai sauḷasa hārā, pakittitā atthatau asaṃkiṇṇā|

aitaisañ‍caiva bhavati, vitthāratayā nayavibhattīti||

2. tattha katamai pañ‍ca nayā? nandiyāvaṭṭau tipukkhalau sīhavik‍kīḷitau disālaucanau aṅkusau iti|

tassānugīti

paṭhamau nandiyāvaṭṭau, dutiyau ca tipukkhalau|

sīhavik‍kīḷitau nāma, tatiyau nayalañ‍jakau nayalañchakau (sī॰)||

disālaucanamāhaṃsu, catutthaṃ nayamuttamaṃ|

pañ‍camau aṅkusau nāma, sabbai pañ‍ca nayā gatāti||

3. tattha katamāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni? nava padāni kusalāni nava padāni akusalāni| tattha katamāni nava padāni akusalāni, taṇhā avij‍jā laubhau dausau mauhau subhasañ‍ñā sukhasañ‍ñā nic‍casañ‍ñā attasañ‍ñāti, imāni nava padāni akusalāni, yattha sabbau akusalapakkhau saṅgahaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati|

tattha katamāni nava padāni kusalāni? samathau vipassanā alaubhau adausau amauhau asubhasañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā anic‍casañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñāti, imāni nava padāni kusalāni, yattha sabbau kusalapakkhau saṅgahaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati|

tatridaṃ uddānaṃ

taṇhā  ca avij‍jāpi ca, laubhau dausau tathaiva mauhau ca|

caturau ca vipal‍lāsā, kilaisabhūmī nava padāni||

samathau  ca vipassanā ca, kusalāni ca yāni tīṇi mūlāni|

caturau satipaṭṭhānā, indriyabhūmī nava padāni||

navahi ca padaihi kusalā, navahi ca yuj‍janti akusalapakkhā|

aitai khau mūlapadā, bhavanti aṭṭhārasa padānīti||

uddaisavārau|



3. niddaisavārau

4. tattha saṅkhaipatau naitti kittitā|

hārasaṅkhaipau

1.

 assādādīnavatā, nissaraṇampi ca phalaṃ upāyau ca|

āṇattī ca bhagavatau, yaugīnaṃ daisanāhārau||

2.

 yaṃ pucchitañ‍ca vissaj‍jitañ‍ca, suttassa yā ca anugīti|

suttassa yau pavicayau, hārau vicayauti niddiṭṭhau||

3.

 sabbaisaṃ hārānaṃ, yā bhūmī yau ca gaucarau taisaṃ|

yuttāyuttaparikkhā, hārau yuttīti niddiṭṭhau||

4.

 dhammaṃ daisaiti jinau, tassa ca dhammassa yaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

iti yāva sabbadhammā, aisau hārau padaṭṭhānau||

5.

 vuttamhi aikadhammai, yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā kaici|

vuttā bhavanti sabbai, sau hārau lakkhaṇau nāma||

6.

 nairuttamadhippāyau, byañ‍janamatha daisanānidānañ‍ca|

pubbāparānusandhī, aisau hārau catubyūhau||

7.

 aikamhi padaṭṭhānai, pariyaisati saisakaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

āvaṭṭati paṭipakkhai, āvaṭṭau nāma sau hārau||

8.

 dhammañ‍ca  padaṭṭhānaṃ, bhūmiñ‍ca vibhaj‍jatai ayaṃ hārau|

sādhāraṇai asādhāraṇai ca naiyyau vibhattīti||

9.

 kusalākusalai dhammai, niddiṭṭhai bhāvitai pahīnai ca|

parivattati paṭipakkhai, hārau parivattanau nāma||

10.

 vaivacanāni  bahūni tu, suttai vuttāni aikadhammassa|

yau jānāti suttavidū, vaivacanau nāma sau hārau||

11.

 aikaṃ  bhagavā dhammaṃ, pañ‍ñattīhi vividhāhi daisaiti|

sau ākārau ñaiyyau, pañ‍ñattī nāma hārauti||

12.

 yau ca paṭic‍cuppādau, indriyakhandhā ca dhātu āyatanā|

aitaihi autarati yau, autaraṇau nāma sau hārau||

13.

 vissaj‍jitamhi pañhai, gāthāyaṃ pucchitāyamārabbha|

suddhāsuddhaparikkhā, hārau sau saudhanau nāma||

14.

 aikattatāya dhammā, yaipi ca vaimattatāya niddiṭṭhā|

taina vikappayitabbā, aisau hārau adhiṭṭhānau||

15.

 yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ, janayantippac‍cayā paramparatau|

haitumavakaḍḍhayitvā, aisau hārau parikkhārau||

16.

 yai dhammā yaṃ mūlā, yai caikatthā pakāsitā muninā|

tai samaraupayitabbā, aisa samāraupanau hārau||

nayasaṅkhaipau

17.

 taṇhañ‍ca avij‍jampi ca, samathaina vipassanā yau naiti|

sac‍caihi yaujayitvā, ayaṃ nayau nandiyāvaṭṭau||

18.

 yau akusalai samūlaihi, naiti kusalai ca kusalamūlaihi|

bhūtaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ, tipukkhalaṃ taṃ nayaṃ āhu||

19.

 yau naiti vipal‍lāsaihi, kilaisai indriyaihi saddhammai|

aitaṃ nayaṃ nayavidū, sīhavik‍kīḷitaṃ āhu||

20.

 vaiyyākaraṇaisu  hi yai, kusalākusalā tahiṃ tahiṃ vuttā|

manasā vaulaukayatai, taṃ khu disālaucanaṃ āhu||

21.

 aulaukaitvā  disalaucanaina, ukkhipiya yaṃ samānaiti|

sabbai kusalākusalai, ayaṃ nayau aṅkusau nāma||

22.

 sauḷasa hārā paṭhamaṃ, disalaucanatau disalaucanaina (ka॰) disā vilaukaitvā|

saṅkhipiya aṅkusaina hi, nayaihi tīhi niddisai suttaṃ||

dvādasapada

23.

 akkharaṃ padaṃ byañ‍janaṃ, nirutti tathaiva niddaisau|

ākārachaṭṭhavacanaṃ, aittāva byañ‍janaṃ sabbaṃ||

24.

 saṅkāsanā  pakāsanā, vivaraṇā vibhajanuttānīkammapañ‍ñatti|

aitaihi chahi padaihi, atthau kammañ‍ca niddiṭṭhaṃ||

25.

 tīṇi ca nayā anūnā, atthassa ca chappadāni gaṇitāni|

navahi padaihi bhagavatau, vacanassatthau samāyuttau||

26.

 atthassa navappadāni, byañ‍janapariyaiṭṭhiyā catubbīsa|

ubhayaṃ saṅkalayitvā saṅkhaipayatau (ka॰), taittiṃsā aittikā naittīti||

niddaisavārau|



4. paṭiniddaisavārau

1. daisanāhāravibhaṅgau

5. tattha katamau daisanāhārau? ‘‘assādādīnavatā’’ti gāthā ayaṃ daisanāhārau| kiṃ daisayati? assādaṃ ādīnavaṃ nissaraṇaṃ phalaṃ upāyaṃ āṇattiṃ| dhammaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaissāmīti|

tattha  katamau assādau?

‘‘kāmaṃ kāmamādikā imā cha gāthā su॰ ni॰ 772 passitabbā kāmayamānassa, tassa caitaṃ samijjhati|

addhā pītimanau hauti, laddhā mac‍cau yadicchatī’’ti||

ayaṃ assādau|

tattha katamau ādīnavau?

‘‘tassa  cai kāmayānassa, chandajātassa jantunau|

tai kāmā parihāyanti, sal‍laviddhauva ruppatī’’ti||

ayaṃ ādīnavau|

tattha katamaṃ nissaraṇaṃ?

‘‘yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padā sirau|

saumaṃ visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattatī’’ti||

idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamau assādau?

‘‘khaittaṃ vatthuṃ hirañ‍ñaṃ vā, gavāssaṃ dāsapaurisaṃ|

thiyau bandhū puthū kāmai, yau narau anugijjhatī’’ti||

ayaṃ assādau|

tattha katamau ādīnavau?

‘‘abalā naṃ balīyanti, maddantai naṃ parissayā|

tatau naṃ dukkhamanvaiti, nāvaṃ bhin‍namivaudaka’’nti||

ayaṃ ādīnavau|

tattha katamaṃ nissaraṇaṃ?

‘‘tasmā jantu sadā satau, kāmāni parivaj‍jayai|

tai pahāya tarai aughaṃ, nāvaṃ sitvāva pāragū’’ti||

idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ phalaṃ?

‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, chattaṃ mahantaṃ yatha vassakālai|

aisānisaṃsau dhammai suciṇṇai, na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī’’ti||

idaṃ phalaṃ|

tattha katamau upāyau?

‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti…pai॰…

‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā passa dha॰ pa॰ 277 dukkhā’’ti…pai॰…

‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā’’ti||

ayaṃ upāyau|

tattha  katamā āṇatti?

‘‘cakkhumā passa udā॰ 43 visamānīva, vij‍jamānai parak‍kamai|

paṇḍitau jīvalaukasmiṃ, pāpāni parivaj‍jayai’’ti||

ayaṃ āṇatti|

‘‘‘suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu,

maugharājā’ti āṇatti, ‘sadā satau’ti upāyau|

‘attānudiṭṭhiṃ ūhac‍ca uhac‍ca (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1125, aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyā’’’||

idaṃ phalaṃ|

6. tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa nissaraṇaṃ daisayati, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa puggalassa ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca daisayati, naiyyassa puggalassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca daisayati|

tattha catassau paṭipadā, cattārau puggalā| taṇhācaritau mandau satindriyaina dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti satipaṭṭhānaihi nissayaihi| taṇhācaritau udattau udatthau (sī॰) u + ā + dā + ta samādhindriyaina dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya  niyyāti jhānaihi nissayaihi| diṭṭhicaritau mandau vīriyindriyaina sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti sammappadhānaihi nissayaihi| diṭṭhicaritau udattau pañ‍ñindriyaina sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti sac‍caihi nissayaihi|

ubhau taṇhācaritā samathapubbaṅgamāya vipassanā niyyanti rāgavirāgāya caitauvimuttiyā| ubhau diṭṭhicaritā vipassanāpubbaṅgamai samathaina niyyanti avij‍jāvirāgāya pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā|

tattha yai samathapubbaṅgamāhi paṭipadāhi niyyanti, tai nandiyāvaṭṭaina nayaina hātabbā, yai vipassanāpubbaṅgamāhi paṭipadāhi niyyanti, tai sīhavik‍kīḷitaina nayaina hātabbā|

7. svāyaṃ  hārau kattha sambhavati, yassa satthā vā dhammaṃ daisayati añ‍ñatarau vā garuṭṭhānīyau sabrahmacārī, sau taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā saddhaṃ paṭilabhati| tattha yā vīmaṃsā ussāhanā tulanā upaparikkhā, ayaṃ sutamayī pañ‍ñā| tathā sutaina nissayaina yā vīmaṃsā tulanā upaparikkhā manasānupaikkhaṇā, ayaṃ cintāmayī pañ‍ñā| imāhi dvīhi pañ‍ñāhi manasikārasampayuttassa yaṃ ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati dassanabhūmiyaṃ vā bhāvanābhūmiyaṃ vā, ayaṃ bhāvanāmayī pañ‍ñā|

8. parataughausā sutamayī pañ‍ñā| pac‍cattasamuṭṭhitā yaunisau manasikārā cintāmayī pañ‍ñā| yaṃ paratau ca ghausaina pac‍cattasamuṭṭhitaina ca yaunisaumanasikāraina ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍jati, ayaṃ bhāvanāmayī pañ‍ñā| yassa imā dvai pañ‍ñā atthi sutamayī cintāmayī ca, ayaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū| yassa sutamayī pañ‍ñā atthi, cintāmayī natthi, ayaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñū vipac‍citañ‍ñū (sī॰)| yassa naiva sutamayī pañ‍ñā atthi na cintāmayī, ayaṃ naiyyau|

9. sāyaṃ dhammadaisanā kiṃ daisayati? cattāri sac‍cāni dukkhaṃ samudayaṃ niraudhaṃ maggaṃ| ādīnavau ca phalañ‍ca dukkhaṃ, assādau samudayau, nissaraṇaṃ niraudhau, upāyau āṇatti ca maggau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| idaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ|

yathāha  bhagavā – ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti mai, bhikkhavai, bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ, sabbaṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ|

tattha  aparimāṇā padā, aparimāṇā akkharā, aparimāṇā byañ‍janā, aparimāṇā ākārā nairuttā niddaisā| aitassaiva atthassa saṅkāsanā pakāsanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ uttānikammaṃ (ka॰) pañ‍ñatti, itipidaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ|

‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai, bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ…pai॰…  ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti mai, bhikkhavai, bārāṇasiyaṃ isipatanai migadāyai anuttaraṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ appaṭivattiyaṃ samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ|

tattha aparimāṇā padā, aparimāṇā akkharā, aparimāṇā byañ‍janā, aparimāṇā ākārā nairuttā niddaisā| aitassaiva atthassa saṅkāsanā pakāsanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ pañ‍ñatti itipidaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ|

tattha bhagavā akkharaihi saṅkāsaiti, padaihi pakāsaiti, byañ‍janaihi vivarati, ākāraihi vibhajati, niruttīhi uttānīkarauti uttāniṃ karauti (ka॰), niddaisaihi pañ‍ñapaiti| tattha bhagavā akkharaihi ca padaihi ca ugghaṭaiti ugghāṭaiti (sī॰), byañ‍janaihi ca ākāraihi ca vipañ‍cayati, niruttīhi ca niddaisaihi ca vitthāraiti| tattha ugghaṭanā ugghāṭanā (sī॰) ādi, vipañ‍canā majjhai, vitthāraṇā pariyausānaṃ| sauyaṃ dhammavinayau ugghaṭīyantau ugghaṭitañ‍ñūpuggalaṃ vinaiti, taina naṃ āhu ‘‘ādikalyāṇau’’ti| vipañ‍cīyantau vipañ‍citañ‍ñūpuggalaṃ vinaiti, taina naṃ āhu ‘‘majjhaikalyāṇau’’ti| vitthārīyantau naiyyaṃ puggalaṃ vinaiti, taina naṃ āhu ‘‘pariyausānakalyāṇau’’ti|

10. tattha  chappadāni atthau saṅkāsanā pakāsanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammaṃ pañ‍ñatti, imāni chappadāni atthau| chappadāni byañ‍janaṃ akkharaṃ padaṃ byañ‍janaṃ ākārau nirutti niddaisau, imāni chappadāni byañ‍janaṃ| taināha bhagavā ‘‘dhammaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍jana’’nti|

kaivalanti  laukuttaraṃ, na missaṃ laukiyaihi dhammaihi| paripuṇṇanti paripūraṃ anūnaṃ anatiraikaṃ| parisuddhanti nimmalaṃ sabbamalāpagataṃ pariyaudātaṃ upaṭṭhitaṃ sabbavisaisānaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati  tathāgatapadaṃitipi tathāgatanisaivitaṃitipi tathāgatārañ‍jitaṃitipi, ataucaitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| taināha bhagavā ‘‘kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaissāmī’’ti|

kaisaṃ ayaṃ dhammadaisanā, yaugīnaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau –

‘‘assādādīnavatā, nissaraṇampi ca phalaṃ upāyau ca|

āṇattī ca bhagavatau, yaugīnaṃ daisanāhārau’’ti||

niyuttau daisanāhārau|

2. vicayahāravibhaṅgau

11. tattha katamau vicayau hārau? ‘‘yaṃ pucchitañ‍ca vissaj‍jitañ‍cā’’ti gāthā, ayaṃ vicayau hārau|

kiṃ vicinati? padaṃ vicinati, pañhaṃ vicinati, visaj‍janaṃ vissaj‍janaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) vicinati, pubbāparaṃ vicinati, assādaṃ vicinati, ādīnavaṃ vicinati, nissaraṇaṃ vicinati, phalaṃ vicinati, upāyaṃ vicinati, āṇattiṃ vicinati, anugītiṃ vicinati, sabbai nava suttantai vicinati| yathā kiṃ bhavai, yathā āyasmā ajitau pārāyanai bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati –

‘‘kainassu passa su॰ ni॰ 1038 nivutau laukau, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau,]

kainassu nappakāsati|

kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsi, kiṃ su tassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

imāni  cattāri padāni pucchitāni, sau aikau pañhau| kasmā? aikavatthu pariggahā, aivañhi  āha ‘‘kainassu nivutau laukau’’ti laukādhiṭṭhānaṃ pucchati, ‘‘kainassu nappakāsatī’’ti laukassa appakāsanaṃ pucchati, ‘‘kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsī’’ti laukassa abhilaipanaṃ pucchati, ‘‘kiṃsu tassa mahabbhaya’’nti tassaiva laukassa mahābhayaṃ pucchati| laukau tividhau kilaisalaukau bhavalaukau indriyalaukau|

tattha  visaj‍janā –

‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau laukau, [ajitāti bhagavā,]

vivicchā vaivicchā (su॰ ni॰ 1039) pamādā nappakāsati|

jappābhilaipanaṃ brūmi, dukkhamassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

imāni cattāri padāni imaihi catūhi padaihi visaj‍jitāni paṭhamaṃ paṭhamaina, dutiyaṃ dutiyaina, tatiyaṃ tatiyaina, catutthaṃ catutthaina|

‘‘kainassu nivutau laukau’’ti pañhai ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau laukau’’ti visaj‍janā| nīvaraṇaihi nivutau laukau, avij‍jānīvaraṇā hi sabbai sattā| yathāha bhagavā ‘‘sabbasattānaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbapāṇānaṃ sabbabhūtānaṃ pariyāyatau aikamaiva nīvaraṇaṃ vadāmi yadidaṃ avij‍jā, avij‍jānīvaraṇā hi sabbai sattā| sabbasauva, bhikkhavai, avij‍jāya niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā natthi sattānaṃ nīvaraṇanti vadāmī’’ti| taina ca paṭhamassa padassa visaj‍janā yuttā|

‘‘kainassu nappakāsatī’’ti pañhai ‘‘vivicchā pamādā nappakāsatī’’ti visaj‍janā| yau puggalau nīvaraṇaihi nivutau, sau vivicchati| vivicchā nāma vuc‍cati vicikicchā| sau vicikicchantau nābhisaddahati, na abhisaddahantau vīriyaṃ nārabhati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ sacchikiriyāya| sau idhappamādamanuyuttau viharati pamattau, suk‍kai dhammai na uppādiyati, tassa tai anuppādiyamānā nappakāsanti, yathāha bhagavā –

‘‘dūrai  santau pakāsanti pakāsainti dha॰ pa॰ 304, himavantauva pabbatau|

asantaittha na dissanti, rattiṃ khittā ratti khittā (sī॰), passa dha॰ pa॰ 304 yathā sarā|

tai guṇaihi pakāsanti, kittiyā ca yasaina cā’’ti||

taina  ca dutiyassa padassa visaj‍janā yuttā|

‘‘kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsī’’ti pañhai ‘‘jappābhilaipanaṃ brūmī’’ti visaj‍janā| jappā nāma vuc‍cati taṇhā| sā kathaṃ abhilimpati? yathāha bhagavā –

‘‘rattau  atthaṃ na jānāti, rattau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhantamaṃ andhatamaṃ (ka॰) tadā hauti, yaṃ rāgau sahatai nara’’nti||

sāyaṃ taṇhā āsattibahulassa puggalassa ‘‘aivaṃ abhijappā’’ti karitvā tattha laukau abhilittau nāma bhavati, taina ca tatiyassa padassa visaj‍janā yuttā|

‘‘kiṃ su tassa mahabbhaya’’nti pañhai ‘‘dukkhamassa mahabbhaya’’nti visaj‍janā| duvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ – kāyikañ‍ca caitasikañ‍ca| yaṃ kāyikaṃ idaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ caitasikaṃ idaṃ daumanassaṃ| sabbai sattā hi dukkhassa ubbij‍janti, natthi bhayaṃ dukkhaina samasamaṃ, kutau vā pana tassa uttaritaraṃ? tissau dukkhatā – dukkhadukkhatā saṅkhāradukkhatā vipariṇāmadukkhatā| tattha laukau audhasau kadāci karahaci dukkhadukkhatāya muc‍cati| tathā vipariṇāmadukkhatāya| taṃ kissa haitu? haunti laukai appābādhāpi dīghāyukāpi| saṅkhāradukkhatāya pana laukau anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā muc‍cati, tasmā saṅkhāradukkhatā dukkhaṃ laukassāti katvā dukkhamassa mahabbhayanti| taina ca catutthassa padassa visaj‍janā yuttā| taināha bhagavā ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau laukau’’ti|

savanti sabbadhi sautā, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau,]

sautānaṃ kiṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūhi, kaina sautā pidhīyarai pithīyarai (sī॰), pidhiyyarai (ka॰), passa su॰ ni॰ 1040||

imāni cattāri padāni pucchitāni| tai dvai pañhā| kasmā? imaihi batvādhivacanaina pucchitā| aivaṃ  samāpan‍nassa laukassa aivaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhassa kiṃ laukassa vaudānaṃ vuṭṭhānamiti, aivañhi āha|

savanti  sabbadhi sautāti| asamāhitassa savanti abhijjhābyāpādappamādabahulassa| tattha yā abhijjhā ayaṃ laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, yau byāpādau ayaṃ dausau akusalamūlaṃ, yau pamādau ayaṃ mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| tassaivaṃ asamāhitassa chasu āyatanaisu taṇhā savanti rūpataṇhā saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phauṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā, yathāha bhagavā –

‘‘savatī’’ti ca khau, bhikkhavai, chan‍naitaṃ ajjhattikānaṃ āyatanānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| cakkhu savati  manāpikaisu rūpaisu, amanāpikaisu amanāpiyaisu (ka॰) paṭihañ‍ñatīti| sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau savati manāpikaisu dhammaisu amanāpikaisu paṭihañ‍ñatīti| iti sabbā ca savati, sabbathā ca savati| taināha ‘‘savanti sabbadhi sautā’’ti|

‘‘sautānaṃ kiṃ nivāraṇa’’nti pariyuṭṭhānavighātaṃ pucchati, idaṃ vaudānaṃ| ‘‘sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūhi, kaina sautā pidhīyarai’’ti anusayasamugghātaṃ pucchati, idaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha visaj‍janā –

‘‘yāni sautāni laukasmiṃ, [ajitāti bhagavā,]

sati taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi, pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai’’ti||

kāyagatāya satiyā bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya cakkhu nāviñchati manāpikaisu rūpaisu, amanāpikaisu na paṭihañ‍ñati, sautaṃ…pai॰… ghānaṃ… jivhā… kāyau… manau nāviñchati manāpikaisu dhammaisu, amanāpikaisu na paṭihañ‍ñati| kaina kāraṇaina? saṃvutanivāritattā indriyānaṃ| kaina tai saṃvutanivāritā? satiārakkhaina| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘sati taisaṃ nivāraṇa’’nti|

pañ‍ñāya anusayā pahīyanti, anusayaisu pahīnaisu pariyuṭṭhānā pahīyanti| kissa tassa (sī॰), anusayassa pahīnattā? taṃ yathā khandhavantassa rukkhassa anavasaisamūluddharaṇai katai pupphaphalapal‍lavaṅkurasantati samucchin‍nā bhavati| aivaṃ  anusayaisu pahīnaisu pariyuṭṭhānasantati samucchin‍nā bhavati pidahitā paṭicchan‍nā| kaina? pañ‍ñāya| taināha bhagavā ‘‘pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍ñā caiva sati ca, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau,]

nāmarūpañ‍ca mārisa|

aitaṃ mai puṭṭhau pabrūhi, katthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘yamaitaṃ  pañhaṃ apucchi maṃ pucchi (ka॰), passa su॰ ni॰ 1043, ajita taṃ vadāmi tai|

yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, aitthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

ayaṃ pañhai pañhau (sī॰ ka॰) naittivibhāvanī passitabbā anusandhiṃ pucchati| anusandhiṃ pucchantau kiṃ pucchati? anupādisaisaṃ nibbānadhātuṃ| tīṇi ca sac‍cāni saṅkhatāni niraudhadhammāni dukkhaṃ samudayau maggau, niraudhau asaṅkhatau| tattha samudayau dvīsu bhūmīsu pahīyati dassanabhūmiyā ca bhāvanābhūmiyā ca| dassanaina tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau, bhāvanāya satta saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti kāmacchandau byāpādau rūparāgau arūparāgau mānau uddhac‍caṃ avij‍jāvasaisā avij‍jā ca niravasaisā (sī॰ ka॰)| taidhātukai imāni dasa saṃyaujanāni pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni|

12. tattha  tīṇi saṃyaujanāni sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ adhiṭṭhāya nirujjhanti| satta saṃyaujanāni kāmacchandau byāpādau rūparāgau arūparāgau mānau uddhac‍caṃ avij‍jāvasaisā añ‍ñindriyaṃ adhiṭṭhāya nirujjhanti| yaṃ pana aivaṃ jānāti ‘‘khīṇā mai jātī’’ti, idaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti pajānāti, idaṃ anuppādai ñāṇaṃ| imāni dvai ñāṇāni añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ| tattha yañ‍ca anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ yañ‍ca añ‍ñindriyaṃ, imāni aggaphalaṃ arahattaṃ pāpuṇantassa nirujjhanti, tattha yañ‍ca khayai ñāṇaṃ yañ‍ca anuppādai ñāṇaṃ, imāni dvai ñāṇāni aikapañ‍ñā|

api ca ārammaṇasaṅkaitaina dvai nāmāni labbhanti, ‘‘khīṇā mai jātī’’ti pajānantassa khayai ñāṇanti nāmaṃ labhati, ‘‘nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti pajānantassa  anuppādai ñāṇanti nāmaṃ labhati| sā pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā, yathādiṭṭhaṃ apilāpanaṭṭhaina sati|

13. tattha yai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, idaṃ nāmarūpaṃ| tattha yai phassapañ‍camakā dhammā, idaṃ nāmaṃ| yāni pañ‍cindriyāni rūpāni, idaṃ rūpaṃ| tadubhayaṃ nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasampayuttaṃ tassa niraudhaṃ bhagavantaṃ pucchantau āyasmā ajitau pārāyanai aivamāha –

‘‘pañ‍ñā  caiva sati ca, nāmarūpañ‍ca mārisa|

aitaṃ mai puṭṭhau pabrūhi, katthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

tattha sati ca pañ‍ñā ca cattāri indriyāni, sati dvai indriyāni satindriyañ‍ca samādhindriyañ‍ca, pañ‍ñā dvai indriyāni pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca vīriyindriyañ‍ca| yā imaisu catūsu indriyaisu saddahanā aukappanā, idaṃ saddhindriyaṃ| tattha yā saddhādhipataiyyā cittaikaggatā, ayaṃ chandasamādhi| samāhitai cittai kilaisānaṃ vikkhambhanatāya paṭisaṅkhānabalaina vā bhāvanābalaina vā, idaṃ pahānaṃ| tattha  yai assāsapassāsā vitak‍kavicārā sañ‍ñāvaidayitā sarasaṅkappā, imai saṅkhārā| iti purimakau ca chandasamādhi, kilaisavikkhambhanatāya ca pahānaṃ imai ca saṅkhārā, tadubhayaṃ chandasamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ| tattha yā vīriyādhipataiyyā cittaikaggatā, ayaṃ vīriyasamādhi…pai॰… tattha yā cittādhipataiyyā cittaikaggatā, ayaṃ cittasamādhi…pai॰… tattha yā vīmaṃsādhipataiyyā cittaikaggatā, ayaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhi| samāhitai cittai kilaisānaṃ vikkhambhanatāya paṭisaṅkhānabalaina vā bhāvanābalaina vā, idaṃ pahānaṃ| tattha yai assāsapassāsā vitak‍kavicārā sañ‍ñāvaidayitā sarasaṅkappā, imai saṅkhārā| iti purimakau ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi, kilaisavikkhambhanatāya ca pahānaṃ imai ca saṅkhārā, tadubhayaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhippadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti vivaikanissitaṃ virāganissitaṃ niraudhanissitaṃ vaussaggapariṇāmiṃ|

14. sabbau samādhi ñāṇamūlakau ñāṇapubbaṅgamau ñāṇānuparivatti|

yathā purai tathā pacchā, yathā pacchā tathā purai|

yathā divā tathā rattiṃ ratti (ka॰) ayaṃ gāthā thairagā॰ 397 dissati, yathā rattiṃ tathā divā||

iti  vivaṭaina caitasā apariyaunaddhaina sappabhāsaṃ cittaṃ bhāvaiti| pañ‍cindriyāni kusalāni cittasahabhūni cittai uppaj‍jamānai uppaj‍janti, cittai nirujjhamānai nirujjhanti| nāmarūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇahaitukaṃ viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nibbattaṃ, tassa maggaina haitu upacchin‍nau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anāhāraṃ anabhinanditaṃ appaṭisandhikaṃ taṃ nirujjhati| nāmarūpamapi ahaitu appac‍cayaṃ punabbhavaṃ na nibbattayati nibbattiyati (ka॰)| aivaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhā pañ‍ñā ca sati ca nāmarūpañ‍ca nirujjhati| taināha bhagavā –

‘‘yamaitaṃ  pañhaṃ apucchi, ajita taṃ vadāmi tai|

yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, aitthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

‘‘yai ca ayaṃ gāthā su॰ ni॰ 1044 añ‍ñathā dissati saṅkhātadhammāsai, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau]

yai ca saikkhā puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti||

15. imāni tīṇi padāni pucchitāni, tai tayau pañhā| kissa? saikhāsaikhavipassanāpubbaṅgamappahānayaugaina, aivañhi āha| ‘‘yai ca saṅkhātadhammāsai’’ti arahattaṃ pucchati, ‘‘yai ca saikhā puthū idhā’’ti saikhaṃ pucchati, ‘‘taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisā’’ti vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ pahānaṃ pucchati|

tattha visaj‍janā –

‘‘kāmaisu nābhigijjhaiyya, [ajitāti bhagavā]

manasānāvilau siyā|

kusalau sabbadhammānaṃ, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

bhagavatau sabbaṃ kāyakammaṃ ñāṇapubbaṅgamaṃ ñāṇānuparivatti, sabbaṃ vacīkammaṃ ñāṇapubbaṅgamaṃ ñāṇānuparivatti, sabbaṃ manaukammaṃ ñāṇapubbaṅgamaṃ ñāṇānuparivatti| atītai aṃsai appaṭihatañāṇadassanaṃ, anāgatai aṃsai appaṭihatañāṇadassanaṃ, pac‍cuppan‍nai aṃsai appaṭihatañāṇadassanaṃ|

kau  ca ñāṇadassanassa paṭighātau? yaṃ  anic‍cai dukkhai anattani ca añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ, ayaṃ ñāṇadassanassa paṭighātau| yathā idha purisau tārakarūpāni passaiyya, nau ca gaṇanasaṅkaitaina jānaiyya, ayaṃ ñāṇadassanassa paṭighātau|

bhagavatau pana appaṭihatañāṇadassanaṃ, anāvaraṇañāṇadassanā hi buddhā bhagavantau| tattha saikhaina dvīsu dhammaisu cittaṃ rakkhitabbaṃ gaidhā ca rajanīyaisu dhammaisu, dausā ca pariyuṭṭhānīyaisu| tattha  yā icchā mucchā patthanā piyāyanā kīḷanā, taṃ bhagavā nivāraintau aivamāha ‘‘kāmaisu nābhigijjhaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘manasānāvilau siyā’’ti pariyuṭṭhānavighātaṃ āha| tathā hi saikhau abhigijjhantau asamuppan‍nañ‍ca kilaisaṃ uppādaiti, uppan‍nañ‍ca kilaisaṃ phātiṃ karauti| yau pana anāvilasaṅkappau anabhigijjhantau vāyamati, sau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| sau uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| sau anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| sau uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati|

16. katamai katamai ca (aṭṭha॰) anuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā? kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, imai anuppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā| katamai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā? anusayā akusalamūlāni, imai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā| katamai anuppan‍nā kusalā dhammā? yāni sautāpan‍nassa indriyāni, imai anuppan‍nā kusalā dhammā| katamai  uppan‍nā kusalā dhammā? yāni aṭṭhamakassa indriyāni, imai uppan‍nā kusalā dhammā|

yaina kāmavitak‍kaṃ vāraiti, idaṃ satindriyaṃ| yaina byāpādavitak‍kaṃ vāraiti, idaṃ samādhindriyaṃ| yaina vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ vāraiti, idaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ|

yaina  uppan‍nuppan‍nai pāpakai akusalai dhammai pajahati vinaudaiti byantīkarauti anabhāvaṃ gamaiti nādhivāsaiti, idaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| yā imaisu catūsu indriyaisu saddahanā aukappanā, idaṃ saddhindriyaṃ|

tattha saddhindriyaṃ kattha daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu| vīriyindriyaṃ kattha daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu sammappadhānaisu| satindriyaṃ kattha daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu| samādhindriyaṃ kattha daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu jhānaisu| pañ‍ñindriyaṃ kattha daṭṭhabbaṃ? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| aivaṃ saikhau sabbaihi  kusalaihi dhammaihi appamattau vuttau bhagavatā anāvilatāya manasā| taināha bhagavā ‘‘manasānāvilausiyā’’ti|

17. ‘‘kusalau sabbadhammāna’’nti laukau nāma tividhau kilaisalaukau bhavalaukau indriyalaukau| tattha kilaisalaukaina bhavalaukau samudāgacchati, sau indriyāni nibbattaiti, indriyaisu bhāviyamānaisu naiyyassa pariñ‍ñā bhavati| sā duvidhaina upaparikkhitabbā dassanapariñ‍ñāya ca bhāvanāpariñ‍ñāya ca| yadā hi saikhau ñaiyyaṃ parijānāti, tadā nibbidāsahagataihi sañ‍ñāmanasikāraihi naiyyaṃ pariñ‍ñātaṃ bhavati| tassa dvai dhammā kausal‍laṃ gacchanti – dassanakausal‍lañ‍ca bhāvanākausal‍lañ‍ca|

taṃ ñāṇaṃ pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbaṃḥ abhiñ‍ñā pariñ‍ñā pahānaṃ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā| tattha  katamā abhiñ‍ñā? yaṃ dhammānaṃ salakkhaṇai ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā ca atthapaṭisambhidā ca, ayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā|

tattha katamā pariñ‍ñā? aivaṃ abhijānitvā yā parijānanā ‘‘idaṃ kusalaṃ, idaṃ akusalaṃ, idaṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, idaṃ anavaj‍jaṃ, idaṃ kaṇhaṃ, idaṃ suk‍kaṃ, idaṃ saivitabbaṃ, idaṃ na saivitabbaṃ, imai dhammā aivaṃgahitā, idaṃ phalaṃ nibbattainti nibbattāpainti (ka॰), taisaṃ aivaṃgahitānaṃ ayaṃ atthau’’ti, ayaṃ pariñ‍ñā|

aivaṃ parijānitvā tayau dhammā avasiṭṭhā bhavanti pahātabbā bhāvaitabbā sacchikātabbā ca| tattha katamai dhammā pahātabbā? yai akusalā| tattha katamai dhammā bhāvaitabbā? yai kusalā| tattha katamai dhammā sacchikātabbā? yaṃ asaṅkhataṃ| yau aivaṃ jānāti ayaṃ vuc‍cati atthakusalau dhammakusalau  kalyāṇatākusalau phalatākusalau, āyakusalau apāyakusalau upāyakusalau mahatā kausal‍laina saman‍nāgatauti, taināha bhagavā ‘‘kusalau sabbadhammāna’’nti|

‘‘satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti taina diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ abhik‍kantai paṭik‍kantai ālaukitai vilaukitai samiñ‍jitai sammiñ‍jitai (sī॰) pasāritai saṅghāṭipattacīvaradhāraṇai asitai pītai khāyitai sāyitai uc‍cārapassāvakammai gatai ṭhitai nisin‍nai suttai jāgaritai bhāsitai tuṇhibhāvai sataina sampajānaina vihātabbaṃ| imā dvai cariyā anuñ‍ñātā bhagavatā aikā visuddhānaṃ, aikā visujjhantānaṃ| kai visuddhā? arahantau| kai visujjhantā? saikkhā| katakic‍cāni hi arahatau  indriyāni| yaṃ baujjhaṃ, taṃ catubbidhaṃ dukkhassa pariñ‍ñābhisamayaina samudayassa pahānābhisamayaina maggassa bhāvanābhisamayaina niraudhassa sacchikiriyābhisamayaina, idaṃ catubbidhaṃ baujjhaṃ yau aivaṃ jānāti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati  satau abhik‍kamati satau paṭik‍kamati khayā rāgassa khayā dausassa khayā mauhassa| taināha bhagavā ‘‘satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti, taināha –

‘‘kāmaisu nābhigijjhaiyya, [ajitāti bhagavā]

manasānāvilau siyā|

kusalau sabbadhammānaṃ, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

aivaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, aivaṃ visaj‍jitabbaṃ| suttassa ca anugīti atthatau ca byañ‍janatau ca samānaitabbā samānayitabbā (sī॰ ka॰)| atthāpagataṃ hi byañ‍janaṃ samphappalāpaṃ bhavati| dun‍nikkhittassa padabyañ‍janassa atthaupi dun‍nayau bhavati, tasmā atthabyañ‍janūpaitaṃ saṅgāyitabbaṃ| suttañ‍ca pavicinitabbaṃ| kiṃ idaṃ suttaṃ āhac‍ca vacanaṃ anusandhivacanaṃ nītatthaṃ naiyyatthaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ? kuhiṃ imassa suttassa sabbāni sac‍cāni passitabbāni, ādimajjhapariyausānaiti? aivaṃ suttaṃ pavicaitabbaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau – ‘‘yaṃ pucchitañ‍ca vissaj‍jitañ‍ca, suttassa yā ca anugītī’’ti|

niyuttau vicayau hārau|

3. yuttihāravibhaṅgau

18. tattha  katamau yuttihārau? ‘‘sabbaisaṃ hārāna’’nti, ayaṃ yuttihārau| kiṃ yaujayati yaujaiti (sī॰)? cattārau mahāpadaisā buddhāpadaisau saṅghāpadaisau sambahulatthairāpadaisau sampahula… (ka॰) aikatthairāpadaisau| imai cattārau mahāpadaisā, tāni padabyañ‍janāni suttai autārayitabbāni, vinayai sandassayitabbāni, dhammatāyaṃ upanikkhipitabbāni|

katamasmiṃ  suttai autārayitabbāni? catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| katamasmiṃ vinayai sandassayitabbāni? rāgavinayai dausavinayai mauhavinayai| katamissaṃ katamiyaṃ (sī॰) dhammatāyaṃ upanikkhipitabbāni? paṭic‍casamuppādai| yadi catūsu ariyasac‍caisu avatarati, kilaisavinayai sandissati, dhammatañ‍ca na vilaumaiti, aivaṃ āsavai na janaiti| catūhi mahāpadaisaihi yaṃ yaṃ yuj‍jati, yaina yaina yuj‍jati, yathā yathā yuj‍jati, taṃ taṃ gahaitabbaṃ|

19. pañhaṃ pucchitaina kati padāni pañhaiti padasau pariyaugāhitabbaṃ vicaitabbaṃ? yadi sabbāni padāni aikaṃ atthaṃ abhivadanti, aikau pañhau| atha cattāri padāni aikaṃ atthaṃ abhivadanti, aikau pañhau| atha tīṇi padāni aikaṃ atthaṃ abhivadanti, aikau pañhau| atha dvai padāni aikaṃ atthaṃ abhivadanti, aikau pañhau| atha aikaṃ padaṃ aikaṃ atthaṃ abhivadati, aikau pañhau| taṃ upaparikkhamānaina añ‍ñātabbaṃ kiṃ imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā, udāhu imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ aikau atthau byañ‍janamaiva nānanti| yathā kiṃ bhavai? yathā sā daivatā bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati|

‘‘kainassubbhāhatau passa sa॰ ni॰ 1.66 laukau, kainassu parivāritau|

kaina sal‍laina autiṇṇau, kissa dhūpāyitau sadā’’ti||

imāni cattāri padāni pucchitāni| tai tayau pañhā kathaṃ ñāyati? bhagavā hi daivatāya visaj‍jaiti|

‘‘mac‍cunābbhāhatau mac‍cunabbhāhatau (ka॰) thairagā॰ 448; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.66 passitabbaṃ laukau, jarāya parivāritau|

taṇhāsal‍laina autiṇṇau, icchādhūpāyitau sadā’’ti||

20. tattha  jarā ca maraṇañ‍ca imāni dvai saṅkhatassa saṅkhatalakkhaṇāni| jarāyaṃ ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ, maraṇaṃ vayau| tattha jarāya ca maraṇassa ca atthatau nānattaṃ| kaina kāraṇaina, gabbhagatāpi  hi mīyanti, na ca tai jiṇṇā bhavanti| atthi ca daivānaṃ maraṇaṃ, na ca taisaṃ sarīrāni jīranti| sak‍kataiva jarāya paṭikammaṃ kātuṃ, na pana sak‍katai maraṇassa paṭikammaṃ kātuṃ añ‍ñatraiva iddhimantānaṃ iddhivisayā| yaṃ panāha taṇhāsal‍laina autiṇṇauti dissanti vītarāgā jīrantāpi mīyantāpi| yadi ca yathā jarāmaraṇaṃ, aivaṃ taṇhāpi siyā| aivaṃ santai sabbai yaubbanaṭṭhāpi vigatataṇhā siyuṃ| yathā ca taṇhā dukkhassa samudayau, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇampi siyā dukkhassa samudayau, na ca siyā taṇhā dukkhassa samudayau, na hi jarāmaraṇaṃ dukkhassa samudayau, taṇhā dukkhassa samudayau| yathā ca taṇhā maggavajjhā, aivaṃ jarāmaraṇampi  siyā maggavajjhaṃ| imāya yuttiyā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi kāraṇaihi gavaisitabbaṃ| yadi ca sandissati yuttisamārūḷhaṃ atthatau ca añ‍ñattaṃ, byañ‍janataupi gavaisitabbaṃ|

sal‍lauti vā dhūpāyananti vā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ atthatau aikattaṃ| na hi yuj‍jati icchāya ca taṇhāya ca atthatau añ‍ñattaṃ| taṇhāya adhippāyai aparipūramānai navasu āghātavatthūsu kaudhau ca upanāhau ca uppaj‍jati| imāya yuttiyā jarāya ca maraṇassa ca taṇhāya ca atthatau añ‍ñattaṃ|

yaṃ panidaṃ bhagavatā dvīhi nāmaihi abhilapitaṃ icchātipi taṇhātipi, idaṃ bhagavatā bāhirānaṃ vatthūnaṃ ārammaṇavasaina dvīhi nāmaihi abhilapitaṃ icchātipi taṇhātipi, sabbā hi taṇhā ajjhausānalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇā| yathā sabbau aggi uṇhattalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇau, api ca upādānavasaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñāni nāmāni labhati, kaṭṭhaggītipi tiṇaggītipi sakalikaggītipi gaumayaggītipi thusaggītipi saṅkāraggītipi, sabbau hi aggi uṇhattalakkhaṇauva| aivaṃ sabbā taṇhā ajjhausānalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇā, api tu ārammaṇaupādānavasaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi nāmaihi abhilapitā icchāitipi taṇhāitipi sal‍lauitipi dhūpāyanāitipi saritāitipi visattikāitipi sinaihauitipi kilamathauitipi latāitipi mañ‍ñanāitipi bandhauitipi āsāitipi pipāsāitipi abhinandanāitipi, iti sabbā taṇhā ajjhausānalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇā| yathā ca vaivacanai vuttā|

‘‘āsā  ca pihā abhinandanā ca, anaikadhātūsu sarā patiṭṭhitā|

añ‍ñāṇamūlappabhavā pajappitā, sabbā mayā byantikatā samūlakā’’ti samūlikā (sī॰)||

taṇhāyaitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| yathāha bhagavā – rūpai tissa avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa| aivaṃ vaidanāya sañ‍ñāya saṅkhāraisu viñ‍ñāṇai avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa sabbaṃ suttaṃ vitthāraitabbaṃ| taṇhāyaitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| aivaṃ yuj‍jati|

21. sabbau dukkhūpacārau kāmataṇhāsaṅkhāramūlakau, na pana yuj‍jati sabbau nibbidūpacārau kāmataṇhāparikkhāramūlakau| imāya yuttiyā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi kāraṇaihi gavaisitabbaṃ|

yathā  hi yathāha (sī॰) bhagavā rāgacaritassa puggalassa asubhaṃ daisayati, dausacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa maittaṃ daisayati| mauhacaritassa bhagavā puggalassa paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ daisayati| yadi hi bhagavā rāgacaritassa puggalassa maittaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ daisaiyya| sukhaṃ vā paṭipadaṃ dandhābhiñ‍ñaṃ sukhaṃ vā paṭipadaṃ khippābhiñ‍ñaṃ vipassanāpubbaṅgamaṃ vā pahānaṃ daisaiyya, na yuj‍jati daisanā| aivaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci rāgassa anulaumappahānaṃ dausassa anulaumappahānaṃ mauhassa  anulaumappahānaṃ| sabbaṃ taṃ vicayaina hāraina vicinitvā yuttihāraina yaujaitabbaṃ| yāvatikā ñāṇassa bhūmi|

maittāvihārissa satau byāpādau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, byāpādau pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā| karuṇāvihārissa satau vihaisā cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, vihaisā pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā| muditā vihārissa satau arati cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, arati pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā| upaikkhāvihārissa satau rāgau cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, rāgau pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā| animittavihārissa satau nimittānusārī taina tainaiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pavattatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, nimittaṃ pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā| asmīti vigataṃ ayamahamasmīti na samanupassāmi| atha ca pana mai kismīti kathasmīti vicikicchā kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya ṭhassatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, vicikicchā kathaṃkathāsal‍laṃ pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchatīti yuj‍jati daisanā|

yathā vā pana paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau kāmarāgabyāpādā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| vitak‍kasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau vitak‍kavicārasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| upaikkhāsukhasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau pītisukhasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā, upaikkhāsatipārisuddhisahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā|

ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā|

ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samāpan‍nassa satau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa satau sañ‍ñūpacārā visaisāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, hānāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhasahagatā vā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā hānāya saṃvattantīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, visaisāya saṃvattantīti yuj‍jati daisanā| kal‍latāparicitaṃ cittaṃ na ca abhinīhāraṃ khamatīti na yuj‍jati daisanā, kal‍latāparicitaṃ cittaṃ atha ca abhinīhāraṃ khamatīti yuj‍jati daisanā|

aivaṃ sabbai navasuttantā yathādhammaṃ yathāvinayaṃ yathāsatthusāsanaṃ  sabbatau vicayaina hāraina vicinitvā yuttihāraina yaujaitabbāti| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘sabbaisaṃ hārānaṃ yā bhūmi yau ca gaucarau taisa’’nti|

niyuttau yutti hārau|

4. padaṭṭhānahāravibhaṅgau

22. tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau hārau? ‘‘dhammaṃ daisaiti jinau’’ti, ayaṃ padaṭṭhānau hārau| kiṃ  daisaiti? sabbadhammayāthāvaasampaṭivaidhalakkhaṇā avij‍jā, tassā vipal‍lāsā padaṭṭhānaṃ| ajjhausānalakkhaṇā taṇhā, tassā piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| patthanalakkhaṇau laubhau, tassa adin‍nādānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| vaṇṇasaṇṭhānabyañ‍janaggahaṇalakkhaṇā subhasañ‍ñā, tassā indriyā saṃvarau padaṭṭhānaṃ| sāsavaphassaupagamanalakkhaṇā sukhasañ‍ñā, tassā assādau padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅkhatalakkhaṇānaṃ dhammānaṃ asamanupassanalakkhaṇā nic‍casañ‍ñā, tassā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| anic‍casañ‍ñādukkhasañ‍ñāasamanupassanalakkhaṇā attasañ‍ñā, tassā nāmakāyau padaṭṭhānaṃ| sabbadhammasampaṭivaidhalakkhaṇā vij‍jā, tassā sabbaṃ naiyyaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| cittavikkhaipapaṭisaṃharaṇalakkhaṇau samathau, tassa asubhā padaṭṭhānaṃ| icchāvacarapaṭisaṃharaṇalakkhaṇau alaubhau, tassa adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī vairamaṇi (ka॰) padaṭṭhānaṃ| abyāpaj‍jalakkhaṇau  adausau, tassa pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī padaṭṭhānaṃ| vatthuavippaṭipattilakkhaṇau vatthuavippaṭipādānalakkhaṇau (sī॰ ka॰) amauhau, tassa sammāpaṭipatti padaṭṭhānaṃ| vinīlakavipubbakagahaṇalakkhaṇā asubhasañ‍ñā, tassā nibbidā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sāsavaphassaparijānanalakkhaṇā dukkhasañ‍ñā, tassā vaidanā padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅkhatalakkhaṇānaṃ dhammānaṃ samanupassanalakkhaṇā anic‍casañ‍ñā, tassā uppādavayā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sabbadhammaabhinivaisalakkhaṇā anattasañ‍ñā, tassā dhammasañ‍ñā padaṭṭhānaṃ|

pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, pañ‍cindriyāni rūpīni rūparāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ bhavarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, nibbattabhavānupassitā pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇadassanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aukappanalakkhaṇā saddhā adhimuttipac‍cupaṭṭhānā ca, anāvilalakkhaṇau pasādau sampasīdanapac‍cupaṭṭhānau ca| abhipatthiyanalakkhaṇā saddhā, tassā avaic‍capasādau padaṭṭhānaṃ| anāvilalakkhaṇau pasādau, tassa saddhā padaṭṭhānaṃ| ārambhalakkhaṇaṃ vīriyaṃ, tassa sammappadhānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| apilāpanalakkhaṇā sati, tassā satipaṭṭhānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| aikaggalakkhaṇau samādhi, tassa jhānāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| pajānanalakkhaṇā pañ‍ñā, tassā sac‍cāni padaṭṭhānaṃ|

aparau nayau, assādamanasikāralakkhaṇau ayaunisaumanasikārau, tassa avij‍jā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sac‍casammauhanalakkhaṇā avij‍jā, sā saṅkhārānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| punabbhavavirauhaṇalakkhaṇā saṅkhārā, tai taṃ (ka॰) viñ‍ñāṇassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aupapac‍cayikanibbattilakkhaṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, taṃ nāmarūpassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nāmakāyarūpakāyasaṅghātalakkhaṇaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, taṃ chaḷāyatanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| indriyavavatthānalakkhaṇaṃ chaḷāyatanaṃ, taṃ phassassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| cakkhurūpaviñ‍ñāṇasan‍nipātalakkhaṇau phassau, sau vaidanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| iṭṭhāniṭṭhaanubhavanalakkhaṇā vaidanā, sā taṇhāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| ajjhausānalakkhaṇā  taṇhā, sā upādānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aupapac‍cayikaṃ upādānaṃ, taṃ bhavassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nāmakāyarūpakāyasambhavanalakkhaṇau bhavau, sau jātiyā  padaṭṭhānaṃ| khandhapātubhavanalakkhaṇā jāti, sā jarāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| upadhiparipākalakkhaṇā jarā, sā maraṇassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| jīvitindriyupacchaidalakkhaṇaṃ maraṇaṃ, taṃ saukassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ussuk‍kakārakau saukau, sau paridaivassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| lālappakārakau paridaivau, sau dukkhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyasaṃpīḷanaṃ dukkhaṃ, taṃ daumanassassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| cittasaṃpīḷanaṃ daumanassaṃ, taṃ upāyāsassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| audahanakārakau upāyāsau, sau bhavassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| imāni bhavaṅgāni yadā samaggāni nibbattāni bhavanti sau bhavau, taṃ saṃsārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| niyyānikalakkhaṇau maggau, sau niraudhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ|

titthañ‍ñutā pītañ‍ñutāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, pītañ‍ñutā pattañ‍ñutāya mattañ‍ñutāya (sī॰ ka॰) padaṭṭhānaṃ, pattañ‍ñutā attañ‍ñutāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, attañ‍ñutā pubbaikatapuñ‍ñatāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, pubbaikatapuñ‍ñatā patirūpadaisavāsassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, patirūpadaisavāsau sappurisūpanissayassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, sappurisūpanissayau attasammāpaṇidhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, attasammāpaṇidhānaṃ sīlānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sīlāni avippaṭisārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, avippaṭisārau pāmauj‍jassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, pāmauj‍jaṃ pītiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ, pīti passaddhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ, passaddhi sukhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, sukhaṃ samādhissa padaṭṭhānaṃ, samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, nibbidā virāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, virāgau vimuttiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| vimutti vimuttiñāṇadassanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aivaṃ yau kauci upanissayau yau kauci pac‍cayau, sabbau sau padaṭṭhānaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘dhammaṃ daisaiti jinau’’ti|

niyuttau padaṭṭhānau hārau|

5. lakkhaṇahāravibhaṅgau

23. tattha  katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? ‘‘vuttamhi aikadhammai’’ti, ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau| kiṃ lakkhayati? yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aikasmiṃ dhammai vuttai avasiṭṭhā dhammā vuttā bhavanti| yathā kiṃ bhavai? yathāha bhagavā –

‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anavaṭṭhitaṃ ittaraṃ parittaṃ pabhaṅgu paratau dukkhaṃ byasanaṃ calanaṃ calaṃ (sī॰) kuk‍kuḷaṃ saṅkhāraṃ sasaṅkhāraṃ (ka॰) vadhakaṃ amittamajjhai| imasmiṃ cakkhusmiṃ vuttai avasiṭṭhāni ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vuttāni bhavanti| kaina kāraṇaina? sabbāni hi cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni vadhakaṭṭhaina aikalakkhaṇāni| yathā cāha bhagavā –

‘‘atītai, rādha, rūpai anapaikkhau hauhi, anāgataṃ rūpaṃ mā abhinandi abhinanda (ka॰), pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya cāgāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipaj‍ja| imasmiṃ rūpakkhandhai vuttai avasiṭṭhā khandhā vuttā bhavanti| kaina kāraṇaina? sabbai hi pañ‍cakkhandhā yamakauvādasuttai passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.84 vadhakaṭṭhaina aikalakkhaṇā vuttā| yathā cāha bhagavā –

‘‘yaisañ‍ca passa dha॰ pa॰ 293 susamāraddhā, nic‍caṃ kāyagatāsati|

akic‍caṃ tai na saivanti, kic‍cai sātac‍cakārinau’’||

iti kāyagatāya satiyā vuttāya vuttā bhavanti vaidanāgatā sati cittagatā dhammagatā ca| tathā yaṃ kiñ‍ci diṭṭhaṃ vā sutaṃ vā mutaṃ vāti vuttai vuttaṃ bhavati viñ‍ñātaṃ| yathā cāha bhagavā –

tasmātiha  tvaṃ bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| ‘‘ātāpī’’ti vīriyindriyaṃ, ‘‘sampajānau’’ti pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, ‘‘satimā’’ti satindriyaṃ, ‘‘vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassa’’nti samādhindriyaṃ, aivaṃ kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| kaina kāraṇaina, aikalakkhaṇattā catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ|

24. catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, catūsu sammappadhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu cattārau iddhipādā bhāviyamānaisu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, catūsu iddhipādaisu bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍casu indriyaisu bhāviyamānaisu pañ‍ca balāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, pañ‍casu balaisu bhāviyamānaisu satta baujjhaṅgā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti, sattasu baujjhaṅgaisu bhāviyamānaisu ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchati, sabbaiva sabbai ca (sī॰ ka॰) baudhaṅgamā dhammā baudhipakkhiyā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| kaina kāraṇaina, sabbai hi baudhaṅgamā baudhipakkhiyā naiyyānikalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇā, tai aikalakkhaṇattā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

aivaṃ akusalāpi dhammā aikalakkhaṇattā pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu vipal‍lāsā pahīyanti, āhārā  cassa pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchanti, upādānaihi anupādānau bhavati, yaugaihi ca visaṃyuttau bhavati, ganthaihi ca vippayuttau bhavati, āsavaihi ca anāsavau bhavati, aughaihi ca nitthiṇṇau bhavati, sal‍laihi ca visal‍lau bhavati, viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau cassa pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchanti, agatigamanaihi na agatiṃ gacchati, aivaṃ  akusalāpi dhammā aikalakkhaṇattā pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchanti|

yattha vā pana rūpindriyaṃ daisitaṃ, daisitā tatthaiva rūpadhātu rūpakkhandhau rūpañ‍cāyatanaṃ| yattha vā pana sukhā vaidanā daisitā, daisitaṃ tattha sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ dukkhasamudayau ca ariyasac‍caṃ| yattha vā pana dukkhā vaidanā daisitā, daisitaṃ tattha dukkhindriyaṃ daumanassindriyaṃ dukkhañ‍ca ariyasac‍caṃ| yattha vā pana adukkhamasukhā vaidanā daisitā, daisitaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ sabbau ca paṭic‍casamuppādau| kaina kāraṇaina, adukkhamasukhāya hi vaidanāya avij‍jā anusaiti| avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| sau ca sarāgasadausasamauhasaṃkilaisapakkhaina hātabbau, vītarāgavītadausavītamauhaariyadhammaihi hātabbau|

aivaṃ yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā kic‍catau ca lakkhaṇatau ca sāmañ‍ñatau ca cutūpapātatau ca, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aikasmiṃ dhammai vuttai avasiṭṭhā dhammā vuttā bhavanti| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘vuttamhi aikadhammai’’ti|

niyuttau lakkhaṇau hārau|

6. catubyūhahāravibhaṅgau

25. tattha  katamau catubyūhau hārau? ‘‘nairuttamadhippāyau’’ti ayaṃ| byañ‍janaina suttassa nairuttañ‍ca adhippāyau ca nidānañ‍ca pubbāparasandhi ca gavaisitabbau| tattha  katamaṃ nairuttaṃ, yā niruttipadasaṃhitā, yaṃ dhammānaṃ nāmasau ñāṇaṃ| yadā hi bhikkhu atthassa ca nāmaṃ jānāti, dhammassa ca nāmaṃ jānāti, tathā tathā naṃ abhiniraupaiti| ayañ‍ca vuc‍cati atthakusalau dhammakusalau byañ‍janakusalau niruttikusalau pubbāparakusalau daisanākusalau atītādhivacanakusalau anāgatādhivacanakusalau pac‍cuppan‍nādhivacanakusalau itthādhivacanakusalau purisādhivacanakusalau napuṃsakādhivacanakusalau aikādhivacanakusalau anaikādhivacanakusalau, aivaṃ sabbāni kātabbāni janapadaniruttāni sabbā ca janapadaniruttiyau| ayaṃ niruttipadasaṃhitā|

26. tattha katamau adhippāyau?

‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, chattaṃ mahantaṃ yatha vassakālai viya vassakālai jā॰ 1.10.103|

aisānisaṃsau dhammai suciṇṇai, na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī’’ti||

idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai apāyaihi parimuc‍citukāmā bhavissanti, tai dhammacārinau bhavissantīti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau|

‘‘caurau yathā sandhimukhai gahītau, sakammunā hañ‍ñati hañ‍ñatai (sī॰) bajjhatai ca|

aivaṃ ayaṃ paic‍ca pajā parattha, sakammunā hañ‍ñati hañ‍ñatai (sī॰) bajjhatai cā’’ti||

idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? sañ‍caitanikānaṃ katānaṃ kammānaṃ upacitānaṃ dukkhavaidanīyānaṃ aniṭṭhaṃ asātaṃ vipākaṃ pac‍canubhavissatīti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau|

‘‘sukhakāmāni passa dha॰ pa॰ 131-132 bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau na labhatai sukha’’nti||

idha  bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai sukhaina atthikā bhavissanti, tai pāpakammaṃ pāpakaṃ kammaṃ (ka॰) na karissantīti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau|

‘‘middhī  passa dha॰ pa॰ 325 yadā hauti mahagghasau ca, niddāyitā samparivattasāyī|

mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti||

idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai jarāmaraṇaina aṭṭiyitukāmā bhavissanti, tai bhavissanti bhaujanai mattañ‍ñunau indriyaisu guttadvārā pubbarattāpararattaṃ jāgariyānuyaugamanuyuttā vipassakā kusalaisu dhammaisu sagāravā ca sabrahmacārīsu thairaisu navaisu majjhimaisūti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau|

‘‘appamādau amatapadaṃ amataṃ padaṃ (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 21, pamādau mac‍cunau padaṃ|

appamattā na mīyanti, yai pamattā yathā matā’’ti||

idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai amatapariyaisanaṃ pariyaisitukāmā bhavissanti, tai appamattā viharissantīti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau| ayaṃ adhippāyau|

27. tattha katamaṃ nidānaṃ? yathā sau dhaniyau gaupālakau bhagavantaṃ āha –

‘‘nandati puttaihi puttimā, gaumā gaumikau (sī॰), gaupikau (ka॰) su॰ ni॰ 33; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.144 passitabbaṃ gauhi tathaiva nandati|

upadhī hi narassa nandanā, na hi sau nandati yau nirūpadhī’’ti||

bhagavā āha –

‘‘saucati puttaihi puttimā, gaupikau gaumikau (sī॰) gauhi tathaiva saucati|

upadhī hi narassa saucanā, na hi sau saucati yau nirūpadhī’’ti||

iminā  vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā bāhiraṃ pariggahaṃ upadhi āhā’’ti| yathā ca mārau pāpimā gijjhakūṭā pabbatā puthusilaṃ pātaisi, bhagavā āha –

‘‘sacaipi  kaivalaṃ sabbaṃ, gijjhakūṭaṃ calaissasi calaiyyāsi (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.147|

naiva sammāvimuttānaṃ, buddhānaṃ atthi iñ‍jitaṃ||

nabhaṃ phalaiyyappathavī calaiyya, sabbaiva pāṇā uda santasaiyyuṃ|

sal‍lampi cai urasi kampayaiyyuṃ pakampayaiyyuṃ (sī॰), kappayaiyyuṃ (ka॰), upadhīsu tāṇaṃ na karaunti buddhā’’ti||

iminā vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā kāyaṃ upadhiṃ āhā’’ti| yathā cāha –

‘‘na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, yadāyasaṃ dārujapabbajañ‍ca dārujaṃ babbajañ‍ca (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 345|

sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā’’ti||

iminā vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā bāhiraisu vatthūsu taṇhaṃ āhā’’ti| yathā cāha –

‘‘aitaṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, auhārinaṃ sithilaṃ duppamuñ‍caṃ|

aitampi chaitvāna paribbajanti, anapaikkhinau kāmasukhaṃ pahāyā’’ti||

iminā vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā bāhiravatthukāya taṇhāya pahānaṃ āhā’’ti| yathā cāha –

‘‘āturaṃ asuciṃ pūtiṃ, duggandhaṃ daihanissitaṃ|

paggharantaṃ divā rattiṃ, bālānaṃ abhinandita’’nti||

iminā vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā ajjhattikavatthukāya taṇhāya pahānaṃ āhā’’ti| yathā cāha –

‘‘ucchinda  passa dha॰ pa॰ 285 sinaihamattanau, kumudaṃ sāradikaṃva pāṇinā|

santimaggamaiva brūhaya, nibbānaṃ sugataina daisita’’nti||

iminā  vatthunā iminā nidānaina aivaṃ ñāyati ‘‘idha bhagavā ajjhattikavatthukāya taṇhāya pahānaṃ āhā’’ti| idaṃ nidānaṃ|

tattha katamau pubbāparasandhi| yathāha –

‘‘kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā, taṇhāchadanachāditā|

pamattabandhanā pamattabandhunā udā॰ 64 baddhā bandhā (ka॰) passa udā॰ 64, macchāva kumināmukhai|

jarāmaraṇamanvainti, vacchau khīrapakauva mātara’’nti||

ayaṃ kāmataṇhā vuttā| sā katamaina pubbāparaina yuj‍jati? yathāha –

‘‘rattau atthaṃ na jānāti, rattau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhantamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ rāgau sahatai nara’’nti||

iti andhatāya ca sañchan‍natāya ca sāyaiva taṇhā abhilapitā| yañ‍cāha kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā, taṇhāchadanachāditāti| yañ‍cāha rattau atthaṃ na jānāti, rattau dhammaṃ na passatīti, imaihi padaihi pariyuṭṭhānaihi sāyaiva taṇhā abhilapitā| yaṃ andhakāraṃ, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau, yā ca taṇhā paunaubhavikā, yañ‍cāha kāmāti imai kilaisakāmā| yañ‍cāha jālasañchan‍nāti taisaṃ yaiva kāmānaṃ payaugaina pariyuṭṭhānaṃ dassaiti, tasmā kilaisavasaina ca pariyuṭṭhānavasaina ca taṇhābandhanaṃ vuttaṃ| yai aidisikā, tai jarāmaraṇaṃ anvainti, ayaṃ  bhagavatā yathānikkhittagāthābalaina dassitā jarāmaraṇamanvaintīti|

‘‘yassa papañ‍cā ṭhitī ca natthi, sandānaṃ palighañ‍ca paḷighañ‍ca (sī॰) passa udā॰ 67 vītivattau|

taṃ nittaṇhaṃ muniṃ carantaṃ, na vijānāti sadaivakaupi laukau’’ti||

papañ‍cā nāma taṇhādiṭṭhimānā, tadabhisaṅkhatā ca saṅkhārā| ṭhiti nāma anusayā| sandānaṃ nāma taṇhāya pariyuṭṭhānaṃ, yāni chattiṃsataṇhāya jāliniyā  vicaritāni| palighau nāma mauhau| yai ca papañ‍cā saṅkhārā yā ca ṭhiti yaṃ sandānañ‍ca yaṃ palighañ‍ca yau aitaṃ sabbaṃ samatik‍kantau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati nittaṇhau iti|

28. tattha  pariyuṭṭhānasaṅkhārā diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyā vā upapaj‍javaidanīyā vā aparāpariyavaidanīyā vā, aivaṃ taṇhā tividhaṃ phalaṃ daiti diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍jai vā aparai vā pariyāyai| aivaṃ bhagavā āha ‘‘yaṃ laubhapakataṃ kammaṃ karauti kāyaina vā vācāya vā manasā vā, tassa vipākaṃ anubhauti diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍jai vā aparai vā pariyāyai’’ti| idaṃ bhagavatau pubbāparaina yuj‍jati| tattha pariyuṭṭhānaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ vā kammaṃ upapaj‍javaidanīyaṃ vā kammaṃ aparāpariyāyavaidanīyaṃ aparāpariyavaidanīyaṃ (sī॰) vā kammaṃ, aivaṃ kammaṃ tidhā vipac‍cati diṭṭhai vā dhammai upapaj‍jai vā aparai vā pariyāyai| yathāha –

‘‘yañ‍cai bālau idha pāṇātipātī hauti…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhi hauti, tassa diṭṭhai vā dhammai vipākaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti upapaj‍jai vā aparai vā pariyāyai’’ti| idaṃ  bhagavatau pubbāparaina yuj‍jati| tattha pariyuṭṭhānaṃ paṭisaṅkhānabalaina pahātabbaṃ, saṅkhārā dassanabalaina, chattiṃsa taṇhāvicaritāni bhāvanābalaina pahātabbānīti aivaṃ taṇhāpi tidhā pahīyati| yā nittaṇhātā ayaṃ saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| bhaidā kāyassa ayaṃ anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu|

papañ‍cau nāma vuc‍cati anubandhau| yañ‍cāha bhagavā ‘‘papañ‍caiti atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ rūpaṃ ārabbhā’’ti| yañ‍cāha bhagavā – ‘‘atītai, rādha, rūpai anapaikkhau hauhi, anāgataṃ rūpaṃ mā abhinandi, pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭinissaggāya paṭipaj‍jā’’ti| idaṃ bhagavatau pubbāparaina yuj‍jati| yau cāpi papañ‍cau yai ca saṅkhārā yā ca atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nassa abhinandanā, idaṃ aikatthaṃ| api ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi padaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi akkharaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi byañ‍janaihi aparimāṇā dhammadaisanā vuttā bhagavatā| aivaṃ suttaina suttaṃ saṃsandayitvā pubbāparaina saddhiṃ yaujayitvā suttaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ bhavati|

sau cāyaṃ sa cāyaṃ (sī॰) pubbāparau sandhi catubbidhau atthasandhi byañ‍janasandhi daisanāsandhi niddaisasandhīti|

tattha  atthasandhi chappadāni saṅkāsanā pakāsanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkammatā pañ‍ñattīti|

byañ‍janasandhi chappadāni akkharaṃ padaṃ byañ‍janaṃ ākārau nirutti niddaisauti|

daisanāsandhi  na ca pathaviṃ nissāya jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca| na ca āpaṃ nissāya jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca, na ca taijaṃ nissāya jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca, na  ca vāyuṃ nissāya jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca| na ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ nissāya…pai॰… na ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ nissāya…pai॰… na ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ nissāya…pai॰… na ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ nissāya…pai॰… na ca imaṃ laukaṃ nissāya…pai॰… na ca paralaukaṃ nissāya jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca| yamidaṃ ubhayamantaraina diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ vitak‍kitaṃ vicāritaṃ manasānucintitaṃ, tampi nissāya na jhāyati jhāyī jhāyati ca| ayaṃ sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anissitaina cittaina na ñāyati jhāyantau|

yathā mārau pāpimā gaudhikassa kulaputtassa passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.159 viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanvaisantau na jānāti na passati| sau hi papañ‍cātītau taṇhāpahānaina diṭṭhinissayaupissa natthi| yathā ca gaudhikassa, aivaṃ vak‍kalissa sadaivakaina laukaina samārakaina sabrahmakaina sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anissitacittā na ñāyanti jhāyamānā| ayaṃ daisanāsandhi|

tattha katamā niddaisasandhi? nissitacittā akusalapakkhaina niddisitabbā, anissitacittā kusalapakkhaina niddisitabbā| nissitacittā kilaisaina niddisitabbā, anissitacittā vaudānaina niddisitabbā| nissitacittā saṃsārappavattiyā niddisitabbā, anissitacittā saṃsāranivattiyā niddisitabbā| nissitacittā taṇhāya ca avij‍jāya ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā samathaina ca vipassanāya ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā ahirikaina ca anauttappaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā hiriyā ca auttappaina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā asatiyā ca asampajañ‍ñaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā satiyā ca sampajañ‍ñaina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā ayauniyā  ca ayaunisaumanasikāraina  ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā yauniyā ca yaunisaumanasikāraina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā kausaj‍jaina ca dauvacassaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā vīriyārambhaina ca sauvacassaina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā assaddhiyaina ca pamādaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā saddhāya ca appamādaina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā asaddhammassavanaina ca asaṃvaraṇaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā saddhammassavanaina ca saṃvaraina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā abhijjhāya ca byāpādaina ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā anabhijjhāya ca abyāpādaina ca niddisitabbā| nissitacittā  nīvaraṇaihi ca saṃyaujaniyaihi ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā rāgavirāgāya ca caitauvimuttiyā avij‍jāvirāgāya ca pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā niddisitabbā| nissitacittā ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā ca sassatadiṭṭhiyā ca niddisitabbā, anissitacittā saupādisaisāya ca anupādisaisāya ca nibbānadhātuyā niddisitabbā| ayaṃ niddaisasandhi| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘nairuttamadhippāyau’’ti|

niyuttau catubyūhau hārau|

7. āvaṭṭahāravibhaṅgau

29. tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau? ‘‘aikamhi padaṭṭhānai’’ti ayaṃ|

‘‘ārambhatha ārabbhatha (sī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.185; thairagā॰ 256 passitabbaṃ nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau’’ti||

‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamathā’’ti vīriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai’’ti samādhissa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau’’ti pañ‍ñāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamathā’’ti vīriyindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai’’ti samādhindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau’’ti pañ‍ñindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| imāni  padaṭṭhānāni daisanā|

ayuñ‍jantānaṃ vā sattānaṃ yaugai, yuñ‍jantānaṃ vā ārambhau|

tattha yai na yuñ‍janti, tai pamādamūlakā na yuñ‍janti| sau pamādau duvidhau taṇhāmūlakau avij‍jāmūlakau ca| tattha avij‍jāmūlakau yaina  añ‍ñāṇaina nivutau ñaiyyaṭṭhānaṃ nappajānāti pañ‍cakkhandhā uppādavayadhammāti, ayaṃ avij‍jāmūlakau| yau taṇhāmūlakau, sau tividhau anuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ uppādāya pariyaisantau pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati, uppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ ārakkhanimittaṃ paribhauganimittañ‍ca pamādaṃ āpaj‍jati ayaṃ laukai catubbidhau pamādau aikavidhau avij‍jāya tividhau taṇhāya| tattha avij‍jāya nāmakāyau padaṭṭhānaṃ| taṇhāya rūpakāyau padaṭṭhānaṃ| taṃ kissa haitu, rūpīsu bhavaisu ajjhausānaṃ, arūpīsu sammauhau? tattha rūpakāyau  rūpakkhandhau nāmakāyau cattārau arūpinau khandhā| imai pañ‍cakkhandhā katamaina upādānaina saupādānā, taṇhāya ca avij‍jāya ca? tattha taṇhā dvai upādānāni kāmupādānañ‍ca sīlabbatupādānañ‍ca| avij‍jā dvai upādānāni diṭṭhupādānañ‍ca attavādupādānañ‍ca| imaihi catūhi upādānaihi yai saupādānā khandhā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| cattāri upādānāni, ayaṃ samudayau| pañ‍cakkhandhā dukkhaṃ| taisaṃ bhagavā pariñ‍ñāya pahānāya ca dhammaṃ daisaiti dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya samudayassa pahānāya|

30. tattha yau tividhau taṇhāmūlakau pamādau anuppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ uppādāya pariyaisati, uppan‍nānaṃ bhaugānaṃ ārakkhaṇañ‍ca karauti paribhauganimittañ‍ca, tassa sampaṭivaidhaina rakkhaṇā paṭisaṃharaṇā, ayaṃ samathau|

sau kathaṃ bhavati? yadā jānāti kāmānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau  nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau aukārañ‍ca saṃkilaisañ‍ca vaudānañ‍ca naikkhammai ca ānisaṃsaṃ| tattha yā vīmaṃsā upaparikkhā ayaṃ vipassanā| imai dvai dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti samathau ca vipassanā ca| imaisu dvīsu dhammaisu bhāviyamānaisu dvai dhammā pahīyanti taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca, imaisu dvīsu dhammaisu pahīnaisu cattāri upādānāni nirujjhanti| upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| iti purimakāni ca dvai sac‍cāni dukkhaṃ samudayau ca, samathau ca vipassanā ca maggau| bhavaniraudhau nibbānaṃ imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha bhagavā ‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamathā’’ti|

yathāpi  mūlai anupaddavai daḷhai, chin‍naupi rukkhau punaraiva punadaiva (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 338 rūhati|

aivampi taṇhānusayai anūhatai, nibbattatī dukkhamidaṃ punappunaṃ||

ayaṃ taṇhānusayau| katamassā taṇhāya? bhavataṇhāya| yau aitassa dhammassa pac‍cayau ayaṃ avij‍jā| avij‍jāpac‍cayā hi bhavataṇhā| imai dvai kilaisā taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca| tāni cattāri upādānāni taihi catūhi upādānaihi yai saupādānā khandhā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| cattāri upādānāni ayaṃ samudayau| pañ‍cakkhandhā dukkhaṃ| taisaṃ bhagavā pariñ‍ñāya ca pahānāya ca dhammaṃ daisaiti dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya samudayassa pahānāya|

yaina  taṇhānusayaṃ samūhanati samūhanti (sī॰), ayaṃ samathau| yaina taṇhānusayassa pac‍cayaṃ avij‍jaṃ vārayati, ayaṃ vipassanā| imai dvai dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti samathau ca vipassanā ca| tattha samathassa phalaṃ rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti, vipassanāya phalaṃ avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti| iti purimakāni ca dvai sac‍cāni dukkhaṃ samudayau ca, samathau vipassanā ca maggau, dvai ca vimuttiyau niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha bhagavā ‘‘yathāpi mūlai’’ti|

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudāpanaṃ pariyaudapanaṃ (sī॰) dha॰ pa॰ 183; dī॰ ni॰ 2.90 passitabbaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

sabbapāpaṃ nāma tīṇi duc‍caritāni kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ, tai dasa akusalakammapathā pāṇātipātau adin‍nādānaṃ kāmaisumicchācārau musāvādau pisuṇā vācā pharusā vācā samphappalāpau abhijjhā byāpādau micchādiṭṭhi, tāni dvai kammāni caitanā caitasikañ‍ca| tattha yau ca pāṇātipātau yā ca pisuṇā vācā yā ca pharusā vācā, idaṃ dausasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yañ‍ca adin‍nādānaṃ yau ca kāmaisumicchācārau yau ca musāvādau, idaṃ laubhasamuṭṭhānaṃ, yau samphappalāpau, idaṃ mauhasamuṭṭhānaṃ| imāni satta kāraṇāni caitanākammaṃ| yā abhijjhā, ayaṃ laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yau byāpādau, ayaṃ dausau akusalamūlaṃ| yā  micchādiṭṭhi, ayaṃ micchāmaggau| imāni tīṇi kāraṇāni caitasikakammaṃ| taināha ‘‘caitanākammaṃ caitasikakamma’’nti|

akusalamūlaṃ payaugaṃ gacchantaṃ catubbidhaṃ agatiṃ gacchati chandā dausā bhayā mauhā| tattha  yaṃ chandā agatiṃ gacchati, idaṃ laubhasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaṃ dausā agatiṃ gacchati, idaṃ dausasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaṃ bhayā ca mauhā ca agatiṃ gacchati, idaṃ mauhasamuṭṭhānaṃ| tattha laubhau asubhāya pahīyati| dausau maittāya| mauhau pañ‍ñāya| tathā laubhau upaikkhāya pahīyati| dausau maittāya ca karuṇāya ca| mauhau muditāya pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchati| taināha bhagavā ‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇa’’nti|

31. sabbapāpaṃ nāma aṭṭha micchattāni micchādiṭṭhi micchāsaṅkappau micchāvācā micchākammantau micchāājīvau micchāvāyāmau micchāsati micchāsamādhi, idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbapāpaṃ|  imaisaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ micchattānaṃ yā akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhācārau, idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ|

aṭṭhasu micchattaisu pahīnaisu aṭṭha sammattāni sampaj‍janti| aṭṭhan‍naṃ sammattānaṃ yā kiriyā karaṇaṃ sampādanaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati kusalassa upasampadā| sacittapariyaudāpananti atītassa maggassa bhāvanākiriyaṃ dassayati, cittai pariyaudāpitai pariyaudapitai (sī॰ ka॰) pañ‍cakkhandhā pariyaudāpitā bhavanti, aivañhi bhagavā āha ‘‘caitauvisuddhatthaṃ, bhikkhavai, tathāgatai brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti| duvidhā hi pariyaudāpanā nīvaraṇappahānañ‍ca anusayasamugghātau ca| dvai pariyaudāpanabhūmiyau dassanabhūmi ca, bhāvanābhūmi ca, tattha yaṃ paṭivaidhaina pariyaudāpaiti, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yatau pariyaudāpaiti, ayaṃ samudayau| yaina pariyaudāpaiti, ayaṃ maggau| yaṃ pariyaudāpitaṃ, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha bhagavā ‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇa’’nti|

‘‘dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, chattaṃ mahantaṃ yatha vassakālai|

aisānisaṃsau  dhammai suciṇṇai, na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī’’ti||

dhammau  nāma duvidhau indriyasaṃvarau maggau ca| duggati nāma duvidhā daivamanussai vā upanidhāya apāyā duggati, nibbānaṃ vā upanidhāya sabbā upapattiyau duggati| tattha yā saṃvarasīlai akhaṇḍakāritā, ayaṃ dhammau suciṇṇau apāyaihi rakkhati| aivaṃ bhagavā āha – dvaimā, bhikkhavai, sīlavatau gatiyau daivā ca manussā ca| aivañ‍ca nāḷandāyaṃ nigamai asibandhakaputtau gāmaṇi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘brāhmaṇā, bhantai, pacchābhūmakā kāmaṇḍalukā saivālamālikā udakaurauhakā aggiparicārakā, tai mataṃ kālaṅkataṃ uyyāpainti nāma, sañ‍ñāpainti nāma, saggaṃ nāma auk‍kāmainti uggamainti (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.358| bhagavā pana, bhantai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau pahauti tathā kātuṃ, yathā sabbau laukau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, gāmaṇi, tañ‍ñaivaittha paṭipucchissāmi, yathā tai khamaiyya, tathā naṃ byākaraiyyāsīti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, idhassa purisau pāṇātipātī adin‍nādāyī kāmaisumicchācārī  musāvādī pisuṇavācau pharusavācau samphappalāpī abhijjhālu byāpan‍nacittau micchādiṭṭhikau, tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu sau purisau mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaituvā pañ‍jalikaṃ pañ‍jalikā saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.358 anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, purisau mahatiṃ puthusilaṃ gambhīrai udakarahadai udakadahai (ka॰) pakkhipaiyya, tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma  samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ummuj‍ja, bhau, puthusilai, up‍lava bhau puthusilai, thalamup‍lava, bhau puthusilai’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi gāmaṇi, api nu sā mahatī puthusilā mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikaṃ anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā ummuj‍jaiyya vā up‍lavaiyya vā thalaṃ vā up‍lavaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau pāṇātipātī…pai॰… micchādiṭṭhikau, kiñ‍cāpi naṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti| atha khau sau purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, idhassa purisau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratau musāvādā paṭiviratau pisuṇāya vācāya paṭiviratau pharusāya vācāya paṭiviratau samphappalāpā paṭiviratau anabhijjhālu abyāpan‍nacittau sammādiṭṭhikau, tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatū’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, gāmaṇi, api nu sau purisau mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikaṃ anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyā’’ti| ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, gāmaṇi, purisau sappikumbhaṃ vā tailakumbhaṃ vā gambhīrai gambhīraṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.358 udakarahadai augāhaitvā bhindaiyya| tatra yāssa sakkharā vā kaṭhalā kathalā (ka॰), sā adhaugāmī assa| yañ‍ca khvassa tatra sappi vā tailaṃ vā, taṃ uddhaṃgāmi assa| tamainaṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma  āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ausīda, bhau sappitaila, saṃsīda, bhau sappitaila, adhau gaccha avaṃgaccha (sī॰ ka॰)‘bhau sappitailā’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi gāmaṇi, api nu taṃ  sappitailaṃ mahatau janakāyassa āyācanahaitu vā thaumanahaitu vā pañ‍jalikaṃ anuparisak‍kanahaitu vā ‘ausīdaiyya vā saṃsīdaiyya vā adhau vā gacchaiyyā’ti| ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, gāmaṇi, yau sau purisau pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau…pai॰… sammādiṭṭhikau, kiñ‍cāpi naṃ mahājanakāyau saṅgamma samāgamma āyācaiyya thaumaiyya pañ‍jalikau anuparisak‍kaiyya ‘ayaṃ purisau kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jatū’’’ti| atha khau sau purisau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya| iti dhammau suciṇṇau apāyaihi rakkhati| tattha yā maggassa tikkhatā adhimattatā, ayaṃ dhammau suciṇṇau sabbāhi upapattīhi rakkhati| aivaṃ bhagavā āha –

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa passa udā॰ 32, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti||

32. tattha duggatīnaṃ haitu taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca, tāni cattāri upādānāni, taihi catūhi upādānaihi yai saupādānā khandhā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| cattāri upādānāni, ayaṃ samudayau| pañ‍cakkhandhā dukkhaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavā pariñ‍ñāya ca pahānāya ca dhammaṃ daisaiti dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya samudayassa pahānāya| tattha taṇhāya pañ‍cindriyāni rūpīni padaṭṭhānaṃ| avij‍jāya manindriyaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍cindriyāni rūpīni rakkhantau samādhiṃ bhāvayati, taṇhañ‍ca niggaṇhāti| manindriyaṃ rakkhantau vipassanaṃ bhāvayati, avij‍jañ‍ca niggaṇhāti| taṇhāniggahaina dvai upādānāni pahīyanti kāmupādānañ‍ca sīlabbatupādānañ‍ca| avij‍jāniggahaina dvai upādānāni pahīyanti diṭṭhupādānañ‍ca attavādupādānañ‍ca| catūsu  upādānaisu pahīnaisu dvai dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti samathau ca vipassanā ca| idaṃ vuc‍cati brahmacariyanti|

tattha brahmacariyassa phalaṃ cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni sautāpattiphalaṃ sakadāgāmiphalaṃ anāgāmiphalaṃ arahattaṃ arahattaphalaṃ (ka॰) aggaphalaṃ| imāni cattāri brahmacariyassa phalāni brahmacariyaphalānīti (sī॰)| iti purimakāni ca dvai sac‍cāni dukkhaṃ samudayau ca| samathau ca vipassanā ca brahmacariyañ‍ca maggau, brahmacariyassa  phalāni ca tadārammaṇā ca asaṅkhatādhātu niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha bhagavā ‘‘dhammau havai rakkhatī’’ti|

tattha yaṃ paṭivaidhaina rakkhati, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yatau rakkhati, ayaṃ samudayau| yaina rakkhati, ayaṃ maggau| yaṃ rakkhati, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘aikamhi padaṭṭhānai’’ti|

niyuttau āvaṭṭau hārau|

8. vibhattihāravibhaṅgau

33. tattha  katamau vibhattihārau? ‘‘dhammañ‍ca padaṭṭhānaṃ bhūmiñ‍cā’’ti|

dvai suttāni vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca| dvai paṭipadā puñ‍ñabhāgiyā ca phalabhāgiyā ca| dvai sīlāni saṃvarasīlañ‍ca pahānasīlañ‍ca, tattha bhagavā vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ puñ‍ñabhāgiyāya paṭipadāya daisayati, sau saṃvarasīlai ṭhitau taina brahmacariyaina brahmacārī bhavati, tattha  bhagavā nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ phalabhāgiyāya paṭipadāya daisayati, sau pahānasīlai ṭhitau taina brahmacariyaina brahmacārī bhavati|

tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ? vāsanābhāgiyaṃ nāma suttaṃ dānakathā sīlakathā saggakathā kāmānaṃ ādīnavau naikkhammai ānisaṃsauti|

tattha katamaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ? nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ nāma suttaṃ yā catusac‍cappakāsanā, vāsanābhāgiyai suttai natthi pajānanā, natthi maggau, natthi phalaṃ| nibbaidhabhāgiyai suttai atthi pajānanā, atthi maggau, atthi phalaṃ| imāni cattāri suttāni| imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ suttānaṃ daisanāya phalaina sīlaina brahmacariyaina sabbatau vicayaina hāraina vicinitvā yuttihāraina yaujayitabbā yāvatikā ñāṇassa bhūmi|

34. tattha katamai dhammā sādhāraṇā? dvai dhammā sādhāraṇā nāmasādhāraṇā vatthusādhāraṇā ca| yaṃ vā pana kiñ‍ci añ‍ñampi aivaṃ jātiyaṃ, micchattaniyatānaṃ sattānaṃ aniyatānañ‍ca sattānaṃ  dassanappahātabbā kilaisā sādhāraṇā, puthuj‍janassa sautāpan‍nassa ca kāmarāgabyāpādā sādhāraṇā, puthuj‍janassa anāgāmissa ca uddhaṃbhāgiyā saṃyaujanā sādhāraṇā, yaṃ kiñ‍ci ariyasāvakau laukiyaṃ samāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jati, sabbā sā avītarāgaihi avigatarāgaihi (ka॰) sādhāraṇā, sādhāraṇā hi dhammā aivaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ paraṃ paraṃ sakaṃ sakaṃ visayaṃ nātivattanti| yaupi imaihi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau na sau taṃ dhammaṃ upātivattati| imai dhammā sādhāraṇā|

tattha katamai dhammā asādhāraṇā? yāva daisanaṃ upādāya gavaisitabbā saikkhāsaikkhā bhabbābhabbāti, aṭṭhamakassa sautāpan‍nassa ca kāmarāgabyāpādā sādhāraṇā  dhammatā asādhāraṇā, aṭṭhamakassa anāgāmissa ca uddhambhāgiyā  saṃyaujanā sādhāraṇā dhammatā asādhāraṇā| sabbaisaṃ saikkhānaṃ nāmaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ dhammatā asādhāraṇā| sabbaisaṃ paṭipan‍nakānaṃ nāmaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ dhammatā asādhāraṇā| sabbaisaṃ saikkhānaṃ saikkhasīlaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ dhammatā asādhāraṇā| aivaṃ visaisānupassinā hīnuk‍kaṭṭhamajjhimaṃ upādāya gavaisitabbaṃ|

dassanabhūmi niyāmāvak‍kantiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ, bhāvanābhūmi uttarikānaṃ phalānaṃ pattiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā samathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā vipassanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu paratau ghausassa sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu cintāmayiyā pañ‍ñāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu bhāvanāmayiyā pañ‍ñāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| dānamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu paratau ca ghausassa sutamayiyā ca pañ‍ñāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ sīlamayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu cintāmayiyā ca pañ‍ñāya yaunisau ca manasikārassa sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakiriyavatthu bhāvanāmayiyā ca pañ‍ñāya sammādiṭṭhiyā ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| patirūpadaisavāsau vivaikassa ca samādhissa ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sappurisūpanissayau tiṇṇañ‍ca avaic‍cappasādānaṃ samathassa ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, attasammāpaṇidhānaṃ hiriyā ca vipassanāya ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, akusalaparic‍cāgau kusalavīmaṃsāya ca samādhindriyassa ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, dhammasvākkhātatā kusalamūlaraupanāya ca phalasamāpattiyā ca sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, saṅghasuppaṭipan‍natā saṅghasuṭṭhutāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, satthusampadā appasan‍nānañ‍ca pasādāya pasan‍nānañ‍ca bhiyyaubhāvāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, appaṭihatapātimaukkhatā dummaṅkūnañ‍ca  puggalānaṃ niggahāya paisalānañ‍ca puggalānaṃ phāsuvihārāya sādhāraṇaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘dhammañ‍ca padaṭṭhāna’’nti|

niyuttau vibhatti hārau|

9. parivattanahāravibhaṅgau

35. tattha  katamau parivattanau hārau? ‘‘kusalākusalai dhammai’’ti| sammādiṭṭhissa purisapuggalassa micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā bhavati| yai cassa micchādiṭṭhipac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ anaikai anaikā (ka॰) pāpakā akusalā dhammā, tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammādiṭṭhipac‍cayā cassa anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti, tai cassa bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| sammāsaṅkappassa purisapuggalassa micchāsaṅkappau nij‍jiṇṇau bhavati| yai cassa micchāsaṅkappapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā, tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammāsaṅkappapac‍cayā cassa anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti| tai cassa bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ sammāvācassa sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsatissa sammāsamādhissa sammāvimuttassa sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanassa purisapuggalassa micchāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ nij‍jiṇṇaṃ bhavati| yai cassa micchāvimuttiñāṇadassanapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā, tai cassa nij‍jiṇṇā haunti| sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanapac‍cayā cassa anaikai kusalā dhammā sambhavanti, tai cassa bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti|

36. yassa vā pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa pāṇātipātau pahīnau hauti| adin‍nādānā paṭiviratassa adin‍nādānaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti| brahmacārissa abrahmacariyaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti| sac‍cavādissa musāvādau pahīnau hauti| apisuṇavācassa pisuṇā vācā pahīnā hauti| saṇhavācassa pharusā vācā pahīnā hauti| kālavādissa samphappalāpau pahīnau hauti| anabhijjhālussa anabhijjhāmanassa (ka॰) abhijjhā pahīnā  hauti| abyāpan‍nacittassa byāpādau pahīnau hauti| sammādiṭṭhissa micchādiṭṭhi pahīnā hauti|

yai ca khau kaici ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ garahanti, naisaṃ sandiṭṭhikā sahadhammikā gārayhā vādānuvādā āgacchanti| sammādiṭṭhiñ‍ca tai bhavantau dhammaṃ garahanti| taina hi yai micchādiṭṭhikā, taisaṃ bhavantānaṃ puj‍jā ca pāsaṃsā ca| aivaṃ sammāsaṅkappaṃ sammāvācaṃ sammākammantaṃ sammāājīvaṃ sammāvāyāmaṃ sammāsatiṃ sammāsamādhiṃ sammāvimuttiṃ sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanañ‍ca tai bhavantau dhammaṃ garahanti| taina hi yai micchāvimuttiñāṇadassanā, taisaṃ bhavantānaṃ puj‍jā ca pāsaṃsā ca|

yai  ca khau kaici aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘bhuñ‍jitabbā kāmā, paribhuñ‍jitabbā kāmā, āsaivitabbā kāmā, nisaivitabbā kāmā, bhāvayitabbā kāmā, bahulīkātabbā kāmā’’ti| kāmaihi vairamaṇī taisaṃ adhammau|

yai vā pana kaici aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘attakilamathānuyaugau dhammau’’ti| niyyānikau taisaṃ dhammau adhammau| yai ca khau kaici aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘dukkhau dhammau’’ti| sukhau taisaṃ dhammau adhammau| yathā vā pana bhikkhunau sabbasaṅkhāraisu asubhānupassinau viharatau subhasañ‍ñā pahīyanti| dukkhānupassinau viharatau sukhasañ‍ñā pahīyanti| anic‍cānupassinau viharatau nic‍casañ‍ñā pahīyanti| anattānupassinau viharatau attasañ‍ñā pahīyanti| yaṃ yaṃ vā pana dhammaṃ raucayati vā upagacchati vā, tassa tassa dhammassa yau paṭipakkhau, svassa aniṭṭhatau ajjhāpan‍nau bhavati| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘kusalākusaladhammai’’ti|

niyuttau parivattanau hārau|

10. vaivacanahāravibhaṅgau

37. tattha  katamau vaivacanau hārau? ‘‘vaivacanāni bahūnī’’ti| yathā aikaṃ bhagavā dhammaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaihi vaivacanaihi niddisati| yathāha bhagavā –

‘‘āsā ca pihā abhinandanā ca, anaikadhātūsu sarā patiṭṭhitā|

añ‍ñāṇamūlappabhavā pajappitā, sabbā mayā byantikatā samūlikā’’ti||

āsā nāma vuc‍cati yā bhavissassa atthassa āsīsanā āsiṃsanā (sī॰) avassaṃ āgamissatīti āsāssa uppaj‍jati| pihā nāma yā vattamānassa vattamānakassa (sī॰) atthassa patthanā, saiyyataraṃ vā disvā ‘‘aidisau bhavaiyya’’nti pihāssa uppaj‍jati| atthanipphattipaṭipālanā abhinandanā nāma, piyaṃ vā ñātiṃ abhinandati, piyaṃ vā dhammaṃ abhinandati, appaṭikūlatau vā abhinandati|

anaikadhātūti  cakkhudhātu rūpadhātu cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu saddadhātu sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu gandhadhātu ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu rasadhātu jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu phauṭṭhabbadhātu kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu dhammadhātu manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

sarāti kaici rūpādhimuttā kaici saddādhimuttā kaici gandhādhimuttā kaici rasādhimuttā kaici phauṭṭhabbādhimuttā kaici dhammādhimuttā| tattha yāni cha gaihasitāni daumanassāni yāni ca cha gaihasitāni saumanassāni yāni ca cha naikkhammasitāni daumanassāni yāni ca cha naikkhammasitāni saumanassāni, imāni catuvīsapadāni taṇhāpakkhau, taṇhāya aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| yā cha upaikkhā gaihasitā, ayaṃ diṭṭhipakkhau|

38. sāyaiva patthanākāraina dhammanandī dhammapaimaṃ dhammajjhausānanti taṇhāya aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| cittaṃ manau viñ‍ñāṇanti  cittassa aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| manindriyaṃ manaudhātu manāyatanaṃ vijānanāti manassaitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ adhipañ‍ñā sikkhā pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñākkhandhau dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau ñāṇaṃ sammādiṭṭhi tīraṇā vipassanā dhammai ñāṇaṃ atthai ñāṇaṃ anvayai ñāṇaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ anuppādai ñāṇaṃ anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ cakkhu vij‍jā buddhi bhūri maidhā ālaukau, yaṃ vā pana yaṃ kiñ‍ci añ‍ñaṃpi aivaṃ jātiyaṃ, pañ‍ñāya aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| pañ‍cindriyāni laukuttarāni, sabbā pañ‍ñā| api ca ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina saddhā, ārambhaṭṭhaina vīriyaṃ, apilāpanaṭṭhaina sati, avikkhaipaṭṭhaina samādhi, pajānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā|

yathā ca buddhānussatiyaṃ vuttaṃ itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| balanipphattigatau vaisāraj‍jappattau adhigatappaṭisambhidau catuyaugavippahīnau agatigamanavītivattau uddhaṭasal‍lau nirūḷhavaṇau madditakaṇḍakau nibbāpitapariyuṭṭhānau nibbāhita … (ka॰) bandhanātītau ganthavinivaiṭhanau ajjhāsayavītivattau bhin‍nandhakārau cakkhumā laukadhammasamatik‍kantau anuraudhaviraudhavippayuttau iṭṭhāniṭṭhaisu dhammaisu asaṅkhaipagatau bandhanātivattau ṭhapitasaṅgāmau abhik‍kantatarau uk‍kādharau ālaukakarau paj‍jautakarau tamaunudau raṇañ‍jahau aparimāṇavaṇṇau appamaiyyavaṇṇau asaṅkhaiyyavaṇṇau ābhaṃkarau pabhaṃkarau dhammaubhāsapaj‍jautakarauti  ca buddhā bhagavantauti ca buddhānussatiyā aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

yathā ca dhammānussatiyaṃ vuttaṃ svākkhātau  bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau  aupanaiyyikau aupanayikau (sī॰) pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhi| yadidaṃ madanimmadanau pipāsavinayau ālayasamugghāṭau vaṭṭūpacchaidau suñ‍ñatau atidul‍labhau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ|

‘‘asaṅkhataṃ anataṃ asaṅkhataṃ nanta … (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.409 anāsavañ‍ca, sac‍cañ‍ca pāraṃ nipuṇaṃ sududdasaṃ|
