ajaj‍jaraṃ dhuvaṃ apalaukitaṃ apalaukiyaṃ (sī॰ ka॰), anidassanaṃ nippapañ‍ca santaṃ||

‘‘amataṃ paṇītañ‍ca sivañ‍ca khaimaṃ, taṇhākkhayau acchariyañ‍ca abbhutaṃ|

anītikaṃ anītikadhammaṃ nītikadhammamaiva vā (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.409, nibbānamaitaṃ sugataina daisitaṃ||

‘‘ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ anupaddavañ‍ca, akataṃ asaukañ‍ca athau visaukaṃ|

anūpasaggaṃnupasaggadhammaṃ, nibbānamaitaṃ sugataina daisitaṃ||

‘‘gambhīrañ‍caiva duppassaṃ, uttarañ‍ca anuttaraṃ|

asamaṃ appaṭisamaṃ, jaiṭṭhaṃ saiṭṭhanti vuc‍cati||

‘‘laiṇañ‍ca tāṇaṃ araṇaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ, akāca maitaṃ vimalanti vuc‍cati|

dīpau sukhaṃ appamāṇaṃ patiṭṭhā, akiñ‍canaṃ appapañ‍canti vutta’’nti||

dhammānussatiyā aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

yathā  ca saṅghānussatiyaṃ vuttaṃ suppaṭipan‍nau ujuppaṭipan‍nau ñāyappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa, sīlasampan‍nau  samādhisampan‍nau pañ‍ñāsampan‍nau vimuttisampan‍nau vimuttiñāṇadassanasampan‍nau sattānaṃ sārau sattānaṃ maṇḍau sattānaṃ uddhārau sattānaṃ aisikā aisikau (ka॰) sattānaṃ surabhipasūnaṃ puj‍jau daivānañ‍ca manussānañ‍cāti saṅghānussatiyā aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

yathā ca sīlānussatiyaṃ vuttaṃ yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni ariyāni ariyakantāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñuppasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, alaṅkārau ca sīlaṃ uttamaṅgaupasaubhaṇatāya, nidhānañ‍ca sīlaṃ sabbadaubhaggasamatik‍kamanaṭṭhaina, sippañ‍ca sīlaṃ akkhaṇavaidhitāya, vailā ca sīlaṃ anatik‍kamanaṭṭhaina, dhañ‍ñañ‍ca sīlaṃ daliddaupacchaidanaṭṭhaina daḷiddau… (sī॰), ādāsau ca sīlaṃ dhammavaulaukanatāya, pāsādau ca sīlaṃ vaulaukanaṭṭhaina, sabbabhūmānuparivatti ca sīlaṃ amatapariyausānanti sīlānussatiyā aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

yathā ca cāgānussatiyaṃ vuttaṃ yasmiṃ samayai ariyasāvakau agāraṃ ajjhāvasati muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratauti cāgānussatiyā aitaṃ vaivacanaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘vaivacanāni bahūnī’’ti|

niyuttau vaivacanau hārau|

11. pañ‍ñattihāravibhaṅgau

39. tattha katamau pañ‍ñattihārau? ‘‘aikaṃ bhagavā dhammaṃ pañ‍ñattīhi vividhāhi daisaitī’’ti|

yā  pakatikathāya daisanā| ayaṃ nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti| kā ca pakatikathāya daisanā, cattāri sac‍cāni| yathā bhagavā āha ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti ayaṃ pañ‍ñatti pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ chan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ dhātūnaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ dasan‍naṃ indriyānaṃ nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti|

kabaḷīkārai  cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī nandi (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.64) atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi buddhi (ka॰)| yattha atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi|

phassai cai…pai॰… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai| viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai atthi rāgau atthi nandī atthi taṇhā, patiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ| yattha patiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ virūḷhaṃ, atthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha atthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, atthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha atthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, atthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha atthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, atthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha atthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, sasaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaraṃ saupāyāsanti vadāmi| ayaṃ pabhavapañ‍ñatti dukkhassa ca samudayassa ca|

kabaḷīkārai cai, bhikkhavai passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.64, āhārai natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā, appatiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ| yattha appatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ, natthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha natthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, natthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha natthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, natthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha natthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, natthi tattha  āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha natthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, asaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, adaraṃ anupāyāsanti vadāmi|

phassai cai…pai॰… manausañ‍caitanāya cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai| viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai natthi rāgau natthi nandī natthi taṇhā, appatiṭṭhitaṃ tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ| yattha appatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ, natthi tattha nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti| yattha natthi nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti, natthi tattha saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi| yattha natthi saṅkhārānaṃ vuddhi, natthi tattha āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti| yattha natthi āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti, natthi tattha āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ| yattha natthi āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ, asaukaṃ taṃ, bhikkhavai, adaraṃ anupāyāsanti vadāmi|

ayaṃ  pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti dukkhassa, pahānapañ‍ñatti samudayassa, bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti niraudhassa|

40. samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha| appamattau nipakau satau, samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? ‘‘cakkhu cakkhuṃ (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.99 anic‍ca’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘rūpā anic‍cā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti ‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘cakkhusamphassau anic‍cau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampidaṃ yamidaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) cakkhusamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

sautaṃ  …pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhā…pai॰… kāyau…pai॰… ‘‘manau anic‍cau’’ti anic‍ca’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.100) yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘dhammā anic‍cā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘manausamphassau anic‍cau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| yampidaṃ manausamphassapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati vaidayitaṃ sukhaṃ vā dukkhaṃ vā adukkhamasukhaṃ vā, tampi anic‍canti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

ayaṃ bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti dukkhassa, pahānapañ‍ñatti samudayassa, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti niraudhassa|

rūpaṃ, rādha, vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ vikīḷanikaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.169 karautha, pañ‍ñāya taṇhakkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| taṇhakkhayā dukkhakkhayau, dukkhakkhayā nibbānaṃ| vaidanaṃ…pai॰…| sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ karautha, pañ‍ñāya taṇhakkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| taṇhakkhayā dukkhakkhayau, dukkhakkhayā nibbānaṃ|

ayaṃ niraudhapañ‍ñatti niraudhassa, nibbidāpañ‍ñatti assādassa, pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti dukkhassa, pahānapañ‍ñatti samudayassa, bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti niraudhassa|

‘‘sau idaṃ dukkha’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

ayaṃ  paṭivaidhapañ‍ñatti sac‍cānaṃ, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti dassanabhūmiyā, bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti sautāpattiphalassa| ‘‘sau imai āsavā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhau’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘imai āsavā asaisaṃ nirujjhantī’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

ayaṃ uppādapañ‍ñatti khayai ñāṇassa, aukāsapañ‍ñatti anuppādai ñāṇassa, bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti dukkhassa, pahānapañ‍ñatti samudayassa, ārambhapañ‍ñatti  vīriyindriyassa, āsāṭanapañ‍ñatti āsāṭikānaṃ, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti bhāvanābhūmiyā, abhinighātapañ‍ñatti pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ|

41. idaṃ ‘‘dukkha’’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ayaṃ ‘‘dukkhasamudayau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ayaṃ ‘‘dukkhaniraudhau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰…| ayaṃ ‘‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai  ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

ayaṃ daisanāpañ‍ñatti sac‍cānaṃ, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti sutamayiyā pañ‍ñāya sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyassa, pavattanāpañ‍ñatti dhammacak‍kassa|

‘‘taṃ khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyya’’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘‘sau khau panāyaṃ dukkhasamudayau pahātabbau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sau khau panāyaṃ dukkhaniraudhau sacchikātabbau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sā khau panāyaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā bhāvaitabbā’’ti mai, bhikkhavai pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi|

ayaṃ bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti cintāmayiyā pañ‍ñāya, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti añ‍ñindriyassa|

‘‘taṃ  khau panidaṃ dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñāta’’nti mai, bhikkhavai, pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ‘‘sau khau panāyaṃ dukkhasamudayau pahīnau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sau khau panāyaṃ dukkhaniraudhau sacchikatau’’ti mai, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ‘‘sā khau panāyaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā bhāvitā’’ti mai, bhikkhavai pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu cakkhuṃ udapādi, ñāṇaṃ udapādi, pañ‍ñā udapādi, vij‍jā udapādi, ālaukau udapādi| ayaṃ bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti bhāvanāmayiyā pañ‍ñāya, sacchikiriyāpañ‍ñatti añ‍ñātāvinau indriyassa, pavattanāpañ‍ñatti dhammacak‍kassa|

‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca  sambhavaṃ, bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji muni|

ajjhattaratau samāhitau, abhindi abhidā (sī॰ ka॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 2.169 kavacamivattasambhava’’nti||

‘‘tula’’nti  saṅkhāradhātu| ‘‘atula’’nti nibbānadhātu, ‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca sambhava’’nti abhiñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti sabbadhammānaṃ| nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti dhammapaṭisambhidāya| ‘‘bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji munī’’ti paric‍cāgapañ‍ñatti samudayassa| pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti dukkhassa| ‘‘ajjhattaratau samāhitau’’ti bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti kāyagatāya satiyā| ṭhitipañ‍ñatti cittaikaggatāya| ‘‘abhindi kavacamivattasambhava’’nti abhinibbidāpañ‍ñatti cittassa, upādānapañ‍ñatti sabbañ‍ñutāya, padālanāpañ‍ñatti avij‍jaṇḍakausānaṃ| taināha bhagavā ‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca sambhava’’nti|

yau dukkhamaddakkhi yataunidānaṃ, kāmaisu sau jantu kathaṃ namaiyya|

kāmā hi laukai saṅgauti ñatvā, taisaṃ satīmā vinayāya sikkhaiti||

‘‘yau dukkha’’nti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti ca dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāpañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘yataunidāna’’nti pabhavapañ‍ñatti ca samudayassa pahānapañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘addakkhī’’ti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti ca ñāṇacakkhussa paṭivaidhapañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘kāmaisu sau jantukathaṃ namaiyyā’’ti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti ca kāmataṇhāya abhinivaisapañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘kāmā hi laukai saṅgauti ñatvā’’ti pac‍catthikatau dassanapañ‍ñatti kāmānaṃ| kāmā hi aṅgārakāsūpamā maṃsapaisūpamā pāvakakappā papātauragaupamā ca| ‘‘taisaṃ satīmā’’ti apacayapañ‍ñatti pahānāya, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti kāyagatāya  satiyā, bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa| ‘‘vinayāya sikkhai’’ti paṭivaidhapañ‍ñatti rāgavinayassa dausavinayassa mauhavinayassa| ‘‘jantū’’ti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti yaugissa| yadā hi yaugī kāmā saṅgauti pajānāti| sau kāmānaṃ anuppādāya kusalai dhammai uppādayati, sau anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya vāyamati| ayaṃ  vāyāmapañ‍ñatti appattassa pattiyā| nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti auramattikāya asantuṭṭhiyā| tattha sau uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā vāyamatīti ayaṃ appamādapañ‍ñatti bhāvanāya, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti vīriyindriyassa, ārakkhapañ‍ñatti kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, ṭhitipañ‍ñatti adhicittasikkhāya| taināha bhagavā ‘‘yau dukkhamaddakkhi yataunidāna’’nti|

‘‘mauhasambandhanau  laukau, bhabbarūpauva dissati|

upadhibandhanau upadhisambandhanau (sī॰) passa udā॰ 70 bālau, tamasā parivāritau|

assirī viya sassatauriva (udā॰ 70) khāyati, passatau natthi kiñ‍cana’’nti||

‘‘mauhasambandhanau laukau’’ti daisanāpañ‍ñatti vipal‍lāsānaṃ| ‘‘bhabbarūpauva dissatī’’ti viparītapañ‍ñatti laukassa| ‘‘upadhibandhanau bālau’’ti pabhavapañ‍ñatti pāpakānaṃ icchāvacarānaṃ, kic‍capañ‍ñatti pariyuṭṭhānānaṃ| balavapañ‍ñatti kilaisānaṃ| virūhanāpañ‍ñatti saṅkhārānaṃ| ‘‘tamasā parivāritau’’ti daisanāpañ‍ñatti avij‍jandhakārassa vaivacanapañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘assirī viya khāyatī’’ti dassanapañ‍ñatti dibbacakkhussa, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti pañ‍ñācakkhussa| ‘‘passatau natthi kiñ‍cana’’nti paṭivaidhapañ‍ñatti sattānaṃ, rāgau kiñ‍canaṃ dausau kiñ‍canaṃ mauhau kiñ‍canaṃ| taināha bhagavā ‘‘mauhasambandhanau laukau’’ti|

‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ akataṃ asaṅkhataṃ, nau caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhavissa ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ akataṃ asaṅkhataṃ| nayidha na idha (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 73 jātassa bhūtassa katassa saṅkhatassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaitha| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ akataṃ asaṅkhataṃ, tasmā jātassa bhūtassa katassa saṅkhatassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti|

‘‘nau  caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhavissa ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ akataṃ asaṅkhata’’nti daisanāpañ‍ñatti nibbānassa vaivacanapañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘nayidha jātassa bhūtassa katassa saṅkhatassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyaithā’’ti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti saṅkhatassa upanayanapañ‍ñatti  ca| ‘‘yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi ajātaṃ abhūtaṃ akataṃ asaṅkhata’’nti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti nibbānassa jautanāpañ‍ñatti ca| ‘‘tasmā jātassa bhūtassa katassa saṅkhatassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti ayaṃ vaivacanapañ‍ñatti nibbānassa, niyyānikapañ‍ñatti maggassa, nissaraṇapañ‍ñatti saṃsāratau| taināha bhagavā ‘‘nau caitaṃ, bhikkhavai, abhavissā’’ti| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘aikaṃ bhagavā dhammaṃ, pañ‍ñattīhi vividhāhi daisaitī’’ti|

niyuttau pañ‍ñatti hārau|

12. autaraṇahāravibhaṅgau

42. tattha  katamau autaraṇau hārau? ‘‘yau ca paṭic‍cuppādau’’ti|

‘‘uddhaṃ adhau sabbadhi vippamuttau, ayaṃ ahasmīti ayamahamasmīti (sī॰) passa udā॰ 61 anānupassī|

aivaṃ vimuttau udatāri aughaṃ, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti||

‘‘uddha’’nti rūpadhātu ca arūpadhātu ca| ‘‘adhau’’ti kāmadhātu| ‘‘sabbadhi vippamuttau’’ti taidhātukai ayaṃ asaikkhāvimutti| tāniyaiva asaikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni, ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva asaikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni vij‍jā, vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau, saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau, phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau, vaidanāniraudhā  taṇhāniraudhau, taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau, jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva  asaikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni tīhi khandhaihi saṅgahitāni – sīlakkhandhaina samādhikkhandhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina, ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva asaikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nāni yai saṅkhārā anāsavā, nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ, nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

‘‘ayaṃ  ahasmīti anānupassī’’ti ayaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā samugghātau, sā saikkhāvimutti, tāniyaiva saikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva saikkhāni pañ‍cindriyāni vij‍jā, vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbau paṭic‍casamuppādau| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vij‍jā pañ‍ñākkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vij‍jā saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nā, yai saṅkhārā anāsavā, nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā, ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ, nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ, ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

saikkhāya ca vimuttiyā asaikkhāya ca vimuttiyā vimuttau udatāri aughaṃ atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāya| taināha  bhagavā ‘‘uddhaṃ adhau’’ti|

43. ‘‘nissitassa passa udā॰ 74 calitaṃ, anissitassa calitaṃ natthi, calitai asati passaddhi, passaddhiyā sati nati na hauti, natiyā asati āgatigati na hauti, āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti, cutūpapātai asati naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti|

‘‘nissitassa calita’’nti nissayau nāma duvidhau taṇhānissayau ca diṭṭhinissayau ca| tattha yā rattassa caitanā, ayaṃ taṇhānissayau; yā mūḷhassa caitanā, ayaṃ diṭṭhinissayau| caitanā pana saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ, aivaṃ sabbau paṭic‍casamuppādau| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

tattha yā rattassa vaidanā, ayaṃ sukhā vaidanā| yā sammūḷhassa vaidanā, ayaṃ adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, imā dvai vaidanā vaidanākkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

tattha  sukhā vaidanā dvai indriyāni sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca, adukkhamasukhā vaidanā upaikkhindriyaṃ| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva indriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nāni, yai saṅkhārā sāsavā bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ sāsavaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ, ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

‘‘anissitassa calitaṃ natthī’’ti samathavasaina vā taṇhāya anissitau vipassanāvasai vā diṭṭhiyā anissitau| yā vipassanā ayaṃ vij‍jā, vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbau paṭic‍casamuppādau| ayaṃ  paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vipassanā pañ‍ñākkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vipassanā dvai indriyāni – vīriyindriyañ‍ca pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vipassanā saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nā, yai saṅkhārā anāsavā, nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ, nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

‘‘passaddhiyā satī’’ti duvidhā passaddhi kāyikā ca caitasikā ca| yaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ, ayaṃ kāyapassaddhi| yaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ, ayaṃ caitasikā passaddhi| passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati vaidayati (ka॰), sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau nibbindati, nibbindantau  viraj‍jati, virāgā vimuc‍cati, vimuttasmiṃ ‘‘vimutta’’miti vimuttamhīti (sī॰ ka॰) ñāṇaṃ hauti, ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti pajānāti| sau  na namati rūpaisu, na saddaisu, na gandhaisu, na rasaisu, na phauṭṭhabbaisu, na dhammaisu khayā rāgassa khayā dausassa khayā mauhassa yaina rūpaina tathāgataṃ tiṭṭhantaṃ carantaṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya, tassa rūpassa khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā rūpasaṅkhayai vimuttau, tathāgatau atthītipi na upaiti, natthītipi na upaiti, atthi natthītipi na upaiti, naivatthi nau natthītipi na upaiti| atha khau gambhīrau appamaiyyau asaṅkhaiyyau nibbutautiyaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati khayā rāgassa, khayā dausassa, khayā mauhassa|

yāya vaidanāya…pai॰… yāya sañ‍ñāya| yaihi saṅkhāraihi| yaina viñ‍ñāṇaina tathāgataṃ  tiṭṭhantaṃ carantaṃ pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiyya, tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā viñ‍ñāṇasaṅkhayai vimuttau, tathāgatau atthītipi na upaiti, natthītipi na upaiti, atthi natthītipi na upaiti, naivatthi nau natthītipi na upaiti| atha khau gambhīrau appamaiyyau asaṅkhaiyyau nibbutautiyaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati khayā rāgassa, khayā dausassa, khayā mauhassa| ‘‘āgatī’’ti idhāgati| ‘‘gatī’’ti paic‍cabhavau| āgatigatīpi na bhavanti, ‘‘naividhā’’ti chasu ajjhattikaisu āyatanaisu| ‘‘na hura’’nti chasu bāhiraisu āyatanaisu| ‘‘na ubhayamantarainā’’ti phassasamuditaisu dhammaisu attānaṃ na passati| ‘‘aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti paṭic‍casamuppādau| sau duvidhau laukiyau ca laukuttarau ca| tattha laukiyau avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, yāva jarāmaraṇā| laukuttarau sīlavatau avippaṭisārau jāyati, yāva nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| taināha bhagavā ‘‘nissitassa calitaṃ anissitassa calitaṃ natthi…pai॰… aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti|

44.

‘‘yai kaici saukā paridaivitā vā, dukkhā dukkhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 78 ca laukasmimanaikarūpā|

piyaṃ paṭic‍cappabhavanti aitai, piyai asantai na bhavanti aitai||

tasmā  hi tai sukhinau vītasaukā, yaisaṃ piyaṃ natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai|

tasmā asaukaṃ virajaṃ patthayānau, piyaṃ na kayirātha kuhiñ‍ci laukai’’ti||

‘‘yai kaici saukā paridaivitā vā, dukkhā ca laukasmimanaikarūpā piyaṃ paṭic‍cappabhavanti aitai’’ti – ayaṃ dukkhā vaidanā| ‘‘piyai asantai na bhavanti aitai’’ti – ayaṃ sukhā vaidanā| vaidanā  vaidanākkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

vaidanāpac‍cayā  taṇhā, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ, aivaṃ sabbaṃ| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

tattha sukhā vaidanā dvai indriyāni – sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca| dukkhā vaidanā dvai indriyāni – dukkhindriyaṃ daumanassindriyañ‍ca| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva indriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nāni, yai saṅkhārā sāsavā bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ sāsavaṃ bhavaṅgaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

tasmā hi tai sukhinau vītasaukā, yaisaṃ piyaṃ natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai|

tasmā asaukaṃ virajaṃ patthayānau, piyaṃ na kayirātha kuhiñ‍ci laukaiti||

idaṃ taṇhāpahānaṃ| taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbaṃ| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

taṃyaiva taṇhāpahānaṃ samathau| sau samathau dvai indriyāni satindriyaṃ samādhindriyañ‍ca| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

sauyaiva samathau samādhikkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

sauyaiva samathau saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nau, yai saṅkhārā anāsavā, nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā  dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ, nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā| taināha bhagavā ‘‘yai kaici saukā’’ti|

kāmaṃ  kāmayamānassa, tassa cai taṃ samijjhati|

addhā pītimanau hauti, laddhā mac‍cau yadicchati||

tassa cai kāmayānassa, chandajātassa jantunau|

tai kāmā parihāyanti, sal‍laviddhauva ruppati||

yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva sabbassaiva (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 774 padā sirau|

saumaṃ visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattatīti||

tattha yā pītimanatā, ayaṃ anunayau| yadāha sal‍laviddhauva ruppatīti, idaṃ paṭighaṃ| anunayaṃ paṭighañ‍ca pana taṇhāpakkhau, taṇhāya ca pana dasarūpīni āyatanāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva dasa rūpīni rūpakāyau nāmasampayuttau, tadubhayaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ, saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau, phassapac‍cayā vaidanā, vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā, aivaṃ sabbaṃ| ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

tadaiva nāmarūpaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

tadaiva nāmarūpaṃ aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

tattha yau rūpakāyau imāni pañ‍ca rūpīni indriyāni, yau nāmakāyau imāni pañ‍ca arūpīni indriyāni, imāni dasa indriyāni| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tattha yadāha –

‘‘yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padā sirau|

saumaṃ visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattatī’’ti||

ayaṃ saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu, ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva  saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu vij‍jā, vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbaṃ| ayaṃ  paṭic‍casamuppādaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva  vij‍jā pañ‍ñākkhandhau| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vij‍jā dvai indriyāni – vīriyindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca| ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

sāyaiva vij‍jā saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nā, yai saṅkhārā anāsavā, nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā| ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ, nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ| ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā| taināha bhagavā ‘‘kāmaṃ kāmayamānassā’’ti|

aittāvatā paṭic‍ca indriyakhandhadhātuāyatanāni samausaraṇautaraṇāni bhavanti| aivaṃ paṭic‍ca indriyakhandhadhātuāyatanāni autāraitabbāni| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘yau ca paṭic‍cuppādau’’ti|

niyuttau autaraṇau hārau|

13. saudhanahāravibhaṅgau

45. tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? ‘‘vissaj‍jitamhi pañhai’’tigāthā| yathā āyasmā ajitau pārāyanai bhagavantaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati –

‘‘kainassu nivutau laukau, kainassu nappakāsati|

kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsi, kiṃsu tassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

‘‘avij‍jāya  nivutau laukau, [ajitāti bhagavā]

vivicchā pamādā nappakāsati|

jappābhilaipanaṃ brūmi, dukkhamassa mahabbhaya’’nti||

‘‘kainassu nivutau laukau’’ti pañhai ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau laukau’’ti bhagavā padaṃ saudhaiti, nau ca ārambhaṃ| ‘‘kainassu nappakāsatī’’ti  pañhai ‘‘vivicchā pamādā nappakāsatī’’ti bhagavā padaṃ saudhaiti, nau ca ārambhaṃ| ‘‘kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsī’’ti pañhai ‘‘jappābhilaipanaṃ brūmī’’ti bhagavā padaṃ saudhaiti, nau ca ārambhaṃ| ‘‘kiṃsu tassa mahabbhaya’’nti pañhai ‘‘dukkhamassa mahabbhaya’’nti suddhau ārambhau| taināha bhagavā ‘‘avij‍jāya nivutau laukau’’ti|

‘‘savanti  sabbadhi sautā, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau]

sautānaṃ kiṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūhi, kaina sautā pidhīyarai’’ti||

‘‘yāni sautāni laukasmiṃ, [ajitāti bhagavā]

sati taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi, pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai’’ti||

‘‘savanti sabbadhi sautā, sautānaṃ kiṃ nivāraṇa’’nti pañhai ‘‘yāni sautāni laukasmiṃ, sati taisaṃ nivāraṇa’’nti bhagavā padaṃ saudhaiti, nau ca ārambhaṃ| ‘‘sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūhi, kaina sautā pidhīyarai’’ti pañhai ‘‘sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi, pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai’’ti suddhau ārambhau| taināha bhagavā ‘‘yāni sautāni laukasmi’’nti|

‘‘pañ‍ñā caiva sati ca, [ic‍cāyasmā ajitau]

nāmarūpañ‍ca nāmaṃ rūpañ‍ca (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1042 mārisa|

aitaṃ mai puṭṭhau pabrūhi, katthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

pañhai  –

‘‘yamaitaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi, ajita taṃ vadāmi tai|

yattha nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, asaisaṃ uparujjhati|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, aitthaitaṃ uparujjhatī’’ti||

suddhau ārambhau| taināha bhagavā ‘‘yamaitaṃ pañhaṃ apucchī’’ti| yattha  aivaṃ suddhau ārambhau, sau pañhau visaj‍jitau bhavati| yattha pana ārambhau asuddhau, na tāva sau pañhau visaj‍jitau bhavati| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘vissaj‍jitamhi pañhai’’ti|

niyuttau saudhanau hārau|

14. adhiṭṭhānahāravibhaṅgau

46. tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau hārau? ‘‘aikattatāya dhammā, yaipi ca vaimattatāya niddiṭṭhā’’ti|

yai tattha niddiṭṭhā, tathā tai dhārayitabbā|

‘‘dukkha’’nti  aikattatā| tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? jāti dukkhā, jarā dukkhā, byādhi dukkhau, maraṇaṃ dukkhaṃ, appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhau, piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhau, yampicchaṃ na labhati tampi dukkhaṃ, saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā, rūpā dukkhā, vaidanā dukkhā, sañ‍ñā dukkhā, saṅkhārā dukkhā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘dukkhasamudayau’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau dukkhasamudayau? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā paunaubbhavikā (ka॰) nandīrāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī| saiyyathidaṃ, kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā vibhavataṇhā| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘dukkhaniraudhau’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau dukkhaniraudhau? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā’’ti aikattatā| tattha  katamā dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ, sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘maggau’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau maggau? nirayagāmī maggau tiracchānayaunigāmī maggau paittivisayagāmī maggau asurayauniyau asurayaunigāmiyau (sī॰), asurayaunigāmīniyau (ka॰) maggau saggagāmiyau maggau manussagāmī maggau nibbānagāmī maggau| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘niraudhau’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau niraudhau? paṭisaṅkhāniraudhau appaṭisaṅkhāniraudhau anunayaniraudhau paṭighaniraudhau mānaniraudhau makkhaniraudhau paḷāsaniraudhau issāniraudhau macchariyaniraudhau sabbakilaisaniraudhau| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘rūpa’’nti aikattatā| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cātumahābhūtikaṃ cātummahābhūtikaṃ (sī॰) rūpaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpassa pañ‍ñatti| tattha katamāni cattāri mahābhūtāni? pathavīdhātu paṭhavīdhātu (sī॰) āpaudhātu taijaudhātu vāyaudhātu|

47. dvīhi ākāraihi dhātuyau pariggaṇhāti saṅkhaipaina ca vitthāraina ca| kathaṃ vitthāraina dhātuyau pariggaṇhāti? vīsatiyā ākāraihi pathavīdhātuṃ  vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti, dvādasahi ākāraihi āpaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti, catūhi ākāraihi taijaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti, chahi ākāraihi vāyaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti|

katamaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pathavīdhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti? atthi  imasmiṃ kāyai kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau, maṃsaṃ nhāru aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ aṭṭhimiñ‍jā (sī॰) vak‍kaṃ, hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ, antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ matthakai matthaluṅganti imaihi vīsatiyā ākāraihi pathavīdhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti|

katamaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi āpaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti? atthi imasmiṃ kāyai pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttanti imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi āpaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti|

katamaihi  catūhi ākāraihi taijaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti? yaina ca santappati, yaina ca jīrīyati jīrati (sī॰), jīrayati (ka॰), passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.351, yaina ca pariḍayhati, yaina ca asitapītakhāyitasāyitaṃ sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchati, imaihi catūhi ākāraihi taijaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti|

katamaihi chahi ākāraihi vāyaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti? uddhaṅgamā vātā, adhaugamā vātā, kucchisayā vātā, kauṭṭhāsayā kauṭṭhasayā (sī॰) vātā, aṅgamaṅgānusārinau vātā, assāsau passāsau iti, imaihi chahi ākāraihi vāyaudhātuṃ vitthāraina pariggaṇhāti|

aivaṃ imaihi dvācattālīsāya ākāraihi vitthāraina dhātuyau sabhāvatau upalakkhayantau tulayantau parivīmaṃsantau pariyaugāhantau pac‍cavaikkhantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passati kāyaṃ vā kāyapadaisaṃ vā, yathā candanikaṃ pavicinantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passaiyya, yathā saṅkāraṭṭhānaṃ pavicinantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passaiyya, yathā vac‍cakuṭiṃ pavicinantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passaiyya, yathā sivathikaṃ sīvathikaṃ (sī॰) pavicinantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passaiyya| aivamaiva imaihi dvācattālīsāya ākāraihi aivaṃ vitthāraina  dhātuyau sabhāvatau  upalakkhayantau tulayantau parivīmaṃsantau pariyaugāhantau pac‍cavaikkhantau na kiñ‍ci gayhūpagaṃ passati kāyaṃ vā kāyapadaisaṃ vā| taināha bhagavā yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā pathavīdhātu naivaisāhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.349, yā ca bāhirā pathavīdhātu, pathavīdhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā pathavīdhātuyā nibbindati, pathavīdhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti| yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā āpaudhātu, yā ca bāhirā āpaudhātu…pai॰… yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā taijaudhātu, yā ca bāhirā taijaudhātu…pai॰… yā caiva khau pana ajjhattikā vāyaudhātu, yā ca bāhirā vāyaudhātu, vāyaudhāturaivaisā| taṃ ‘‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā vāyaudhātuyā nibbindati, vāyaudhātuyā cittaṃ virājaiti| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

48. ‘‘avij‍jā’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamā avij‍jā? dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, aparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantāparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu añ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ aivarūpaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayau ananubaudhau asambaudhau appaṭivaidhau asal‍lakkhaṇā anupalakkhaṇā apac‍cupalakkhaṇā asamavaikkhaṇaṃ  asamavaikkhanaṃ (ka॰) apac‍cakkhakammaṃ dummaijjhaṃ  bālyaṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ mauhau pamauhau sammauhau avij‍jā avij‍jaughau avij‍jāyaugau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘vij‍jā’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamā vij‍jā? dukkhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ, pubbantai ñāṇaṃ, aparantai ñāṇaṃ, pubbantāparantai ñāṇaṃ, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ, yā aivarūpā pañ‍ñā pajānanā vicayau pavicayau dhammavicayau saṃlakkhaṇā upalakkhaṇā pac‍cupalakkhaṇā paṇḍic‍caṃ kausal‍laṃ naipuñ‍ñaṃ vaibhabyā vaibhavyā (sī॰) cintā upaparikkhā bhūrī maidhā pariṇāyikā vipassanā sampajañ‍ñaṃ pataudau pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñāpāsādau pañ‍ñāālaukau  pañ‍ñāaubhāsau pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau maggaṅgaṃ maggapariyāpan‍naṃ| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘samāpattī’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamā samāpatti? sañ‍ñāsamāpatti asañ‍ñāsamāpatti, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāsamāpatti| vibhūtasañ‍ñāsamāpatti niraudhasamāpattīti| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘jhāyī’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau jhāyī? atthi saikkhau jhāyī, atthi asaikkhau jhāyī, naivasaikkhanāsaikkhau jhāyī, ājāniyau jhāyī, assakhaluṅkau jhāyī, diṭṭhuttarau jhāyī, taṇhuttarau jhāyī, pañ‍ñuttarau jhāyī| ayaṃ  vaimattatā|

‘‘samādhī’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau samādhi? saraṇau samādhi, araṇau samādhi, savairau samādhi, avairau samādhi, sabyāpaj‍jau sabyāpajjhau (sī॰) samādhi, abyāpaj‍jau samādhi, sappītikau samādhi, nippītikau samādhi, sāmisau samādhi, nirāmisau samādhi, sasaṅkhārau samādhi, asaṅkhārau samādhi, aikaṃsabhāvitau samādhi, ubhayaṃsabhāvitau samādhi, ubhayatau bhāvitabhāvanau samādhi, savitak‍kasavicārau samādhi, avitak‍kavicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kaavicārau samādhi, hānabhāgiyau samādhi, ṭhitibhāgiyau samādhi, visaisabhāgiyau samādhi, nibbaidhabhāgiyau samādhi, laukiyau samādhi, laukuttarau samādhi, micchāsamādhi, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘paṭipadā’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamā paṭipadā? āgāḷhapaṭipadā āgaḷhā paṭipadā (sī॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā, nijjhāmapaṭipadā, majjhimapaṭipadā, akkhamā paṭipadā, khamā paṭipadā, samā paṭipadā, damā paṭipadā, dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñāti| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

‘‘kāyau’’ti aikattatā| tattha katamau kāyau? nāmakāyau rūpakāyau ca| tattha katamau rūpakāyau? kaisā laumā nakhā dantā tacau maṃsaṃ nhāru nahāru (sī॰) aṭṭhi aṭṭhimiñ‍jaṃ vak‍kaṃ hadayaṃ yakanaṃ kilaumakaṃ pihakaṃ papphāsaṃ antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariyaṃ karīsaṃ pittaṃ saimhaṃ pubbau lauhitaṃ saidau maidau  assu vasā khaiḷau siṅghāṇikā lasikā muttaṃ matthaluṅganti – ayaṃ  rūpakāyau| nāmakāyau nāma vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā cittaṃ phassau manasikārauti – ayaṃ nāmakāyauti| ayaṃ vaimattatā|

aivaṃ yau dhammau yassa dhammassa samānabhāvau, sau dhammau tassa dhammassa aikattatāya aikī bhavati| yaina yaina vā pana vilakkhaṇau, taina taina vaimattaṃ gacchati| aivaṃ suttai vā vaiyyākaraṇai vā gāthāyaṃ vā pucchitaina vīmaṃsayitabbaṃ, kiṃ aikattatāya pucchati, udāhu vaimattatāyāti| yadi aikattatāya pucchitaṃ, aikattatāya visaj‍jayitabbaṃ| yadi vaimattatāya pucchitaṃ, vaimattatāya visaj‍jayitabbaṃ| yadi sattādhiṭṭhānaina pucchitaṃ, sattādhiṭṭhānaina visaj‍jayitabbaṃ| yadi dhammādhiṭṭhānaina pucchitaṃ, dhammādhiṭṭhānaina visaj‍jayitabbaṃ| yathā yathā vā pana pucchitaṃ, tathā tathā visaj‍jayitabbaṃ| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘aikattatāya dhammā’’ti|

niyuttau adhiṭṭhānau hārau|

15. parikkhārahāravibhaṅgau

49. tattha katamau parikkhārau hārau? ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ janayantī’’ti|

yau dhammau yaṃ dhammaṃ janayati, tassa sau parikkhārau| kiṃlakkhaṇau parikkhārau? janakalakkhaṇau parikkhārau| dvai dhammā janayanti haitu ca pac‍cayau ca| tattha kiṃlakkhaṇau haitu, kiṃlakkhaṇau pac‍cayau? asādhāraṇalakkhaṇau haitu, sādhāraṇalakkhaṇau pac‍cayau| yathā kiṃ bhavai? yathā aṅkurassa nibbattiyā bījaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ, pathavī āpau  ca sādhāraṇā| aṅkurassa hi pathavī āpau ca pac‍cayau sabhāvau haitu| yathā vā pana ghaṭai duddhaṃ pakkhittaṃ dadhi bhavati, na  catthi aikakālasamavadhānaṃ duddhassa ca dadhissa ca| aivamaivaṃ natthi aikakālasamavadhānaṃ haitussa ca pac‍cayassa ca|

ayañhi saṃsārau sahaitu sappac‍cayau nibbattau| vuttaṃ hi avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, aivaṃ sabbau paṭic‍casamuppādau| iti avij‍jā avij‍jāya haitu ayaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau| purimikā avij‍jā pacchimikāya avij‍jāya haitu| tattha purimikā avij‍jā avij‍jānusayau  pacchimikā avij‍jā avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ, purimikau avij‍jānusayau pacchimikassa avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa haitubhūtau paribrūhanāya, bījaṅkurau viya samanantarahaitutāya| yaṃ pana yattha phalaṃ nibbattati, idamassa paramparahaitutāya haitubhūtaṃ| duvidhau hi haitu samanantarahaitu paramparahaitu ca, aivaṃ avij‍jāyapi duvidhau haitu samanantarahaitu paramparahaitu ca|

yathā vā pana thālakañ‍ca vaṭṭi ca tailañ‍ca padīpassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ na sabhāvahaitu, na hi sak‍kā thālakañ‍ca vaṭṭiñ‍ca tailañ‍ca anaggikaṃ dīpaituṃ padīpassa pac‍cayabhūtaṃ| padīpau viya sabhāvau haitu hauti| iti sabhāvau haitu, parabhāvau pac‍cayau| ajjhattikau haitu, bāhirau pac‍cayau| janakau haitu, pariggāhakau pac‍cayau| asādhāraṇau haitu, sādhāraṇau pac‍cayau|

avupacchaidatthau santati atthau, nibbatti atthau phalatthau, paṭisandhi atthau punabbhavatthau, palibaudhatthau pariyuṭṭhānatthau, asamugghātatthau anusayatthau, asampaṭivaidhatthau avij‍jatthau, apariñ‍ñātatthau viñ‍ñāṇassa bījatthau| yattha avupacchaidau tattha santati, yattha santati tattha nibbatti, yattha nibbatti tattha phalaṃ, yattha phalaṃ tattha paṭisandhi, yattha paṭisandhi tattha punabbhavau, yattha punabbhavau tattha palibaudhau, yattha palibaudhau tattha pariyuṭṭhānaṃ, yattha pariyuṭṭhānaṃ tattha asamugghātau| yattha asamugghātau tattha anusayau, yattha anusayau tattha asampaṭivaidhau, yattha asampaṭivaidhau tattha avij‍jā, yattha avij‍jā tattha sāsavaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ apariñ‍ñātaṃ, yattha sāsavaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ apariñ‍ñātaṃ tattha bījatthau|

sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhassa pac‍cayau, samādhikkhandhau pañ‍ñākkhandhassa pac‍cayau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau vimuttikkhandhassa pac‍cayau, vimuttikkhandhau vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhassa pac‍cayau| titthañ‍ñutā pītañ‍ñutāya pac‍cayau, pītañ‍ñutā pattañ‍ñutāya pac‍cayau, pattañ‍ñutā attañ‍ñutāya pac‍cayau|

yathā  vā pana cakkhuñ‍ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tattha cakkhu ādhipataiyyapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau, rūpā ārammaṇapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| ālaukau san‍nissayatāya pac‍cayau, manasikārau sabhāvau haitu| saṅkhārā viñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| nāmarūpaṃ saḷāyatanassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| saḷāyatanaṃ phassassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| phassau vaidanāya pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| vaidanā taṇhāya pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| taṇhā upādānassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| upādānaṃ bhavassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| bhavau jātiyā pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| jāti jarāmaraṇassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| jarāmaraṇaṃ saukassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| saukau paridaivassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| paridaivau dukkhassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| dukkhaṃ daumanassassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| daumanassaṃ upāyāsassa pac‍cayau, sabhāvau haitu| aivaṃ yau kauci upanissayau sabbau sau parikkhārau| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ dhammaṃ janayantī’’ti|

niyuttau parikkhārau hārau|

16. samāraupanahāravibhaṅgau

50. tattha  katamau samāraupanau hārau? ‘‘yai dhammā yaṃmūlā, yai caikatthā pakāsitā muninā’’ti|

aikasmiṃ padaṭṭhānai yattakāni padaṭṭhānāni autaranti, sabbāni tāni samāraupayitabbāni| yathā āvaṭṭai hārai bahukāni padaṭṭhānāni autarantīti| tattha samāraupanā catubbidhā padaṭṭhānaṃ, vaivacanaṃ, bhāvanā, pahānamiti|

tattha katamā padaṭṭhānaina samāraupanā?

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

tassa kiṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ? tīṇi sucaritāni – kāyasucaritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manausucaritaṃ – idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ; tattha yaṃ kāyikañ‍ca vācasikañ‍ca sucaritaṃ, ayaṃ sīlakkhandhau| manausucaritai yā  anabhijjhā abyāpādau ca, ayaṃ samādhikkhandhau| yā sammādiṭṭhi, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, tattha sīlakkhandhau  ca samādhikkhandhau ca samathau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau vipassanā| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, tattha samathassa phalaṃ rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti, vipassanā phalaṃ avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

vanaṃ vanathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kiñ‍ca vanaṃ? kau ca vanathau? vanaṃ nāma pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā, taṇhā vanathau| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| vanaṃ  nāma nimittaggāhau ‘‘itthī’’ti vā ‘‘purisau’’ti vā| vanathau nāma taisaṃ taisaṃ aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ anubyañ‍janaggāhau ‘‘ahau cakkhu, ahau sautaṃ, ahau ghānaṃ, ahau jivhā, ahau kāyau, iti| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| vanaṃ nāma cha ajjhattikabāhirāni āyatanāni apariñ‍ñātāni| yaṃ tadubhayaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati saṃyaujanaṃ, ayaṃ vanathau| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| vanaṃ nāma anusayau| vanathau nāma pariyuṭṭhānaṃ| idaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| taināha bhagavā ‘‘chaitvā vanañ‍ca vanathañ‍cā’’ti| ayaṃ padaṭṭhānaina samāraupanā|

51. tattha katamā vaivacanaina samāraupanā? rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti saikkhaphalaṃ; avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti asaikkhaphalaṃ| idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti anāgāmiphalaṃ; avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti aggaphalaṃ arahattaṃ| idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti kāmadhātusamatik‍kamanaṃ; avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti taidhātusamatik‍kamanaṃ| idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, pañ‍ñābalaṃ, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā, pañ‍ñākkhandhau, dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau, upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau, ñāṇaṃ, sammādiṭṭhi, tīraṇā, santīraṇā, hirī, vipassanā, dhammai ñāṇaṃ, sabbaṃ, idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| ayaṃ vaivacanaina samāraupanā|

tattha katamā bhāvanāya samāraupanā? yathāha bhagavā ‘‘tasmātiha tvaṃ bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharāhi, ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ’’| ātāpīti  vīriyindriyaṃ| sampajānauti pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| satimāti satindriyaṃ| vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassanti samādhindriyaṃ| aivaṃ kāyai kāyānupassinau viharatau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| kaina kāraṇaina? aikalakkhaṇattā catun‍naṃ indriyānaṃ| catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| catūsu sammappadhānaisu bhāviyamānaisu cattārau iddhipādā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| catūsu iddhipādaisu bhāviyamānaisu pañ‍cindriyāni bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ sabbai| kaina kāraṇaina? sabbai hi  baudhaṅgamā dhammā baudhipakkhiyā niyyānikalakkhaṇaina aikalakkhaṇā, tai aikalakkhaṇattā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ bhāvanāya samāraupanā|

tattha katamā pahānaina samāraupanā? kāyai kāyānupassī viharantau ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahati, kabaḷīkārau cassa āhārau pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, kāmupādānaina ca anupādānau bhavati, kāmayaugaina ca visaṃyuttau bhavati, abhijjhākāyaganthaina ca vippayuj‍jati, kāmāsavaina ca anāsavau bhavati, kāmaughañ‍ca uttiṇṇau bhavati, rāgasal‍laina ca visal‍lau bhavati, rūpūpikā rūpupikā (ka॰) aivamuparipi cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, rūpadhātuyaṃ cassa rāgau pahīnau bhavati, na ca chandāgatiṃ gacchati|

vaidanāsu vaidanānupassī viharantau ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahati, phassau cassa āhārau pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, bhavūpādānaina ca anupādānau bhavati, bhavayaugaina ca visaṃyuttau bhavati, byāpādakāyaganthaina ca vippayuj‍jati, bhavāsavaina ca anāsavau bhavati, bhavaughañ‍ca  uttiṇṇau bhavati, dausasal‍laina ca visal‍lau bhavati, vaidanūpikā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, vaidanādhātuyaṃ cassa rāgau pahīnau bhavati, na ca dausāgatiṃ gacchati|

cittai cittānupassī viharantau ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cassa āhārau pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, diṭṭhupādānaina ca anupādānau bhavati, diṭṭhiyaugaina ca visaṃyuttau bhavati, sīlabbataparāmāsakāyaganthaina ca vippayuj‍jati, diṭṭhāsavaina ca anāsavau bhavati, diṭṭhaughañ‍ca uttiṇṇau bhavati, mānasal‍laina ca visal‍lau bhavati, sañ‍ñūpikā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, sañ‍ñādhātuyaṃ cassa rāgau pahīnau bhavati, na ca bhayāgatiṃ gacchati|

dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharantau ‘‘anattani anattaniyai (sī॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.49 attā’’ti vipal‍lāsaṃ pajahati, manausañ‍caitanā cassa āhārau pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, attavādupādānaina ca anupādānau bhavati, avij‍jāyaugaina ca visaṃyuttau bhavati, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthaina ca vippayuj‍jati, avij‍jāsavaina ca anāsavau bhavati, avij‍jaughañ‍ca uttiṇṇau bhavati, mauhasal‍laina ca visal‍lau bhavati, saṅkhārūpikā cassa viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchati, saṅkhāradhātuyaṃ cassa rāgau pahīnau bhavati, na ca mauhāgatiṃ gacchati| ayaṃ pahānaina samāraupanā|

taināha  āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau –

‘‘yai dhammā yaṃ mūlā, yai caikatthā pakāsitā muninā|

tai samāraupayitabbā, aisa samāraupanau hārau’’ti||

niyuttau samāraupanau hārau|

niṭṭhitau ca hāravibhaṅgau|

1. daisanāhārasampātau

52.

 ‘‘sauḷasa  hārā paṭhamaṃ, disalaucanatau disā vilaukaitvā|

saṅkhipiya aṅkusaina hi, nayaihi tīhi niddisai sutta’’nti||

vuttā, tassā niddaisau kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbau? hārasampātai| tattha katamau daisanāhārasampātau?

‘‘arakkhitaina cittaina kāyaina (udā॰ 32), micchādiṭṭhihataina ca|

thinamiddhābhibhūtaina, vasaṃ mārassa gacchatī’’ti||

arakkhitaina cittaināti kiṃ daisayati, pamādaṃ taṃ mac‍cunau padaṃ| micchādiṭṭhihataina cāti micchādiṭṭhihataṃ nāma vuc‍cati yadā ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti passati, sau vipal‍lāsau| sau pana vipal‍lāsau kiṃlakkhaṇau? viparītaggāhalakkhaṇau vipal‍lāsau| sau kiṃ vipal‍lāsayati? tayau dhammai sañ‍ñaṃ cittaṃ diṭṭhimiti| sau kuhiṃ vipal‍lāsayati? catūsu attabhāvavatthūsu, rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ|

tattha  rūpaṃ paṭhamaṃ vipal‍lāsavatthu ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti| vaidanā dutiyaṃ vipal‍lāsavatthu ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti| sañ‍ñā saṅkhārā ca tatiyaṃ vipal‍lāsavatthu ‘‘anattani attā’’ti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ vipal‍lāsavatthu ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti| dvai  dhammā cittassa saṃkilaisā – taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca| taṇhānivutaṃ cittaṃ dvīhi vipal‍lāsaihi vipal‍lāsīyati ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti| diṭṭhinivutaṃ cittaṃ dvīhi vipal‍lāsaihi vipal‍lāsīyati ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti ‘‘anattani attā’’ti|

tattha yau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau, sau atītaṃ rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, atītaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… atītaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, atītai saṅkhārai…pai॰… atītaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati| tattha yau taṇhāvipal‍lāsau, sau anāgataṃ rūpaṃ abhinandati, anāgataṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… anāgataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ, anāgatai saṅkhārai, anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandati| dvai dhammā cittassa  upak‍kilaisā – taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca| tāhi visujjhantaṃ cittaṃ visujjhati| taisaṃ avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati sandhāvantānaṃ saṃsarantānaṃ sakiṃ nirayaṃ sakiṃ tiracchānayauniṃ sakiṃ paittivisayaṃ sakiṃ asurakāyaṃ sakiṃ daivai sakiṃ manussai|

thinamiddhābhibhūtaināti| thinaṃ thīnaṃ (sī॰) nāma yā cittassa akal‍latā akammaniyatā; middhaṃ nāma yaṃ kāyassa līnattaṃ| vasaṃ mārassa gacchatīti kilaisamārassa ca sattamārassa ca vasaṃ gacchati, sau hi nivutau saṃsārābhimukhau hauti| imāni bhagavatā dvai sac‍cāni daisitāni dukkhaṃ samudayau ca| taisaṃ bhagavā pariñ‍ñāya ca pahānāya ca dhammaṃ daisaiti dukkhassa pariñ‍ñāya samudayassa pahānāya| yaina ca parijānāti yaina ca pajahati, ayaṃ maggau| yaṃ taṇhāya avij‍jāya ca pahānaṃ, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| taināha bhagavā ‘‘arakkhitaina cittainā’’ti| taināhāyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau ‘‘assādādīnavatā’’ti|

niyuttau daisanā hārasampātau|

2. vicayahārasampātau

53. tattha  katamau vicayau hārasampātau? tattha taṇhā duvidhā kusalāpi akusalāpi| akusalā saṃsāragāminī, kusalā apacayagāminī pahānataṇhā| mānaupi duvidhau kusalaupi akusalaupi| yaṃ mānaṃ nissāya mānaṃ pajahati, ayaṃ mānau kusalau| yau pana mānau dukkhaṃ nibbattayati, ayaṃ mānau akusalau| tattha yaṃ naikkhammasitaṃ daumanassaṃ kudāssunāmāhaṃ taṃ āyatanaṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissaṃ yaṃ ariyā santaṃ āyatanaṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantīti tassa uppaj‍jati pihā, pihāpac‍cayā daumanassaṃ, ayaṃ taṇhā kusalā rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti, tadārammaṇā kusalā avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti|

tassā kau pavicayau? aṭṭha maggaṅgāni sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| sau kattha daṭṭhabbau? catutthai jhānai pāramitāya| catutthai hi jhānai aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ cittaṃ bhāvayati parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ mudu kammaniyaṃ ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ| sau tattha aṭṭhavidhaṃ adhigacchati cha abhiñ‍ñā dvai ca visaisai, taṃ cittaṃ yatau parisuddhaṃ, tatau pariyaudātaṃ, yatau pariyaudātaṃ, tatau anaṅgaṇaṃ, yatau anaṅgaṇaṃ, tatau vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ, yatau vigatūpak‍kilaisaṃ, tatau mudu, yatau mudu, tatau kammaniyaṃ, yatau kammaniyaṃ, tatau ṭhitaṃ, yatau ṭhitaṃ, tatau ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ| tattha  aṅgaṇā ca upak‍kilaisā ca tadubhayaṃ taṇhāpakkhau| yā ca iñ‍janā yā ca cittassa aṭṭhiti, ayaṃ diṭṭhipakkhau|

cattāri indriyāni dukkhindriyaṃ daumanassindriyaṃ sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca catutthajjhānai nirujjhanti, tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ avasiṭṭhaṃ bhavati| sau uparimaṃ samāpattiṃ santatau manasikarauti, tassa uparimaṃ samāpattiṃ santatau manasikarautau catutthajjhānai auḷārikā sañ‍ñā saṇṭhahati uk‍kaṇṭhā ca paṭighasañ‍ñā, sau sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘‘anantaṃ ākāsa’’nti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| abhiñ‍ñābhinīhārau rūpasañ‍ñā vaukārau nānattasañ‍ñā samatik‍kamati paṭighasañ‍ñā cassa abbhatthaṃ gacchati, aivaṃ samādhi tassa samāhitassa aubhāsau antaradhāyati dassanañ‍ca rūpānaṃ, sau samādhi chaḷaṅgasaman‍nāgatau pac‍cavaikkhitabbau| anabhijjhāsahagataṃ mai mānasaṃ sabbalaukai, abyāpan‍naṃ mai cittaṃ sabbasattaisu, āraddhaṃ mai vīriyaṃ paggahitaṃ, passaddhau mai kāyau asāraddhau, samāhitaṃ mai cittaṃ avikkhittaṃ, upaṭṭhitā mai sati asammuṭṭhā appammuṭṭhā (sī॰), tattha yañ‍ca anabhijjhāsahagataṃ mānasaṃ sabbalaukai yañ‍ca abyāpan‍naṃ cittaṃ sabbasattaisu yañ‍ca āraddhaṃ vīriyaṃ paggahitaṃ yañ‍ca samāhitaṃ cittaṃ avikkhittaṃ, ayaṃ samathau| yau passaddhau kāyau asāraddhau, ayaṃ samādhiparikkhārau| yā upaṭṭhitā sati asammuṭṭhā ayaṃ vipassanā|

54. sau samādhi pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau| ayaṃ samādhi ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau’’ti itissa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati, ayaṃ samādhi ‘‘āyatiṃ sukhavipākau’’ti itissa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati, ayaṃ samādhi ‘‘ariyau nirāmisau’’ti itissa  pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati, ayaṃ samādhi ‘‘akāpurisasaivitau’’ti itissa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati, ayaṃ samādhi ‘‘santau caiva paṇītau ca paṭippassaddhiladdhau ca aikaudibhāvādhigatau ca na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavatau (sī॰), sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvaṭau (ka॰) cā’’ti itissa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati| taṃ khau panimaṃ samādhiṃ ‘‘satau samāpaj‍jāmi satau vuṭṭhahāmī’’ti itissa pac‍cattamaiva ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ bhavati| tattha yau ca samādhi pac‍cuppan‍nasukhau yau ca samādhi āyatiṃ sukhavipākau ayaṃ samathau| yau ca samādhi ariyau nirāmisau, yau ca samādhi akāpurisasaivitau, yau ca samādhi santau caiva paṇītau paṭippassaddhiladdhau  ca aikaudibhāvādhigatau ca na sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau ca yañ‍cāhaṃ taṃ khau panimaṃ samādhiṃ satau samāpaj‍jāmi satau vuṭṭhahāmīti, ayaṃ vipassanā|

sau samādhi pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau pītipharaṇatā sukhapharaṇatā caitaupharaṇatā ālaukapharaṇatā pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ| tattha yau ca pītipharaṇau yau ca sukhapharaṇau yau ca caitaupharaṇau, ayaṃ samathau| yau ca ālaukapharaṇau yañ‍ca pac‍cavaikkhaṇānimittaṃ| ayaṃ vipassanā|

55. dasa  kasiṇāyatanāni pathavīkasiṇaṃ āpaukasiṇaṃ taijaukasiṇaṃ vāyaukasiṇaṃ nīlakasiṇaṃ pītakasiṇaṃ lauhitakasiṇaṃ audātakasiṇaṃ ākāsakasiṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇaṃ| tattha yañ‍ca pathavīkasiṇaṃ yañ‍ca āpaukasiṇaṃ aivaṃ sabbaṃ, yañ‍ca audātakasiṇaṃ| imāni aṭṭha kasiṇāni samathau| yañ‍ca ākāsakasiṇaṃ yañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇakasiṇaṃ, ayaṃ vipassanā| aivaṃ sabbau ariyau maggau yaina yaina ākāraina vuttau, taina taina samathavipassanaina yaujayitabbau| tai  tīhi dhammaihi saṅgahitā anic‍catāya dukkhatāya anattatāya| sau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayamānau tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni bhāvayati| tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni bhāvayantau tayau khandhai bhāvayati| tayau khandhai bhāvayantau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayati|

rāgacaritau puggalau animittaina vimaukkhamukhaina niyyāti nīyāti (sī॰) adhicittasikkhāya sikkhantau laubhaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pajahantau sukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ anupagacchantau sukhaṃ vaidanaṃ parijānantau rāgamalaṃ pavāhaintau rāgarajaṃ niddhunantau rāgavisaṃ vamaintau rāgaggiṃ nibbāpaintau rāgasal‍laṃ uppāṭaintau rāgajaṭaṃ vijaṭaintau| dausacaritau puggalau appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina niyyāti adhisīlasikkhāya sikkhantau dausaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pajahantau dukkhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ anupagacchantau dukkhavaidanaṃ parijānantau dausamalaṃ pavāhaintau dausarajaṃ niddhunantau dausavisaṃ vamaintau dausaggiṃ nibbāpaintau dausasal‍laṃ uppāṭaintau dausajaṭaṃ vijaṭaintau| mauhacaritau puggalau suñ‍ñatavimaukkhamukhaina niyyāti adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya sikkhantau mauhaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pajahantau adukkhamasukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ anupagacchantau adukkhamasukhaṃ vaidanaṃ parijānantau mauhamalaṃ pavāhaintau mauharajaṃ niddhunantau mauhavisaṃ vamaintau mauhaggiṃ nibbāpaintau mauhasal‍laṃ uppāṭaintau mauhajaṭaṃ vijaṭaintau|

tattha suñ‍ñatavimaukkhamukhaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau, animittavimaukkhamukhaṃ samādhikkhandhau, appaṇihitavimaukkhamukhaṃ  sīlakkhandhau| sau tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni bhāvayantau tayau khandhai bhāvayati, tayau khandhai bhāvayantau ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvayati| tattha  yā ca sammāvācā yau ca sammākammantau yau ca sammāājīvau, ayaṃ sīlakkhandhau, yau ca sammāvāyāmau  yā ca sammāsati yau ca sammāsamādhi, ayaṃ samādhikkhandhau, yā ca sammādiṭṭhi yau ca sammāsaṅkappau, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau|

tattha sīlakkhandhau ca samādhikkhandhau ca samathau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau vipassanā| yau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvaiti, tassa dvai bhavaṅgāni bhāvanaṃ gacchanti kāyau cittañ‍ca, bhavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā dvai padāni sīlaṃ samādhi ca| sau hauti bhikkhu bhāvitakāyau bhāvitasīlau bhāvitacittau bhāvitapañ‍ñau| kāyai bhāviyamānai dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti sammākammantau sammāvāyāmau ca, sīlai bhāviyamānai dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti sammāvācā sammāājīvau ca, cittai bhāviyamānai dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti sammāsati sammāsamādhi ca, pañ‍ñāya bhāviyamānāya dvai dhammā bhāvanaṃ gacchanti sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau ca|

tattha yau ca sammākammantau yau ca sammāvāyāmau siyā kāyikau siyā caitasikau, tattha yau kāyasaṅgahau, sau kāyai bhāvitai bhāvanaṃ gacchati, yau cittasaṅgahau, sau cittai bhāvitai bhāvanaṃ gacchati| sau samathavipassanaṃ bhāvayantau pañ‍cavidhaṃ adhigamaṃ gacchati adhigacchati (sī॰) khippādhigamau ca hauti, vimuttādhigamau ca hauti, mahādhigamau ca hauti, vipulādhigamau ca hauti, anavasaisādhigamau ca hauti| tattha samathaina khippādhigamau ca mahādhigamau ca vipulādhigamau ca hauti, vipassanāya vimuttādhigamau ca anavasaisādhigamau ca hauti|

56. tattha yau daisayati, sau dasabalasaman‍nāgatau satthā auvādaina sāvakai na visaṃvādayati| sau tividhaṃ idaṃ karautha  iminā upāyaina karautha idaṃ vau kurumānānaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati, sau tathā auvaditau tathānusiṭṭhau tathākarauntau tathāpaṭipaj‍jantau taṃ bhūmiṃ na pāpuṇissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau tathā auvaditau tathānusiṭṭhau sīlakkhandhaṃ aparipūrayantau taṃ bhūmiṃ anupāpuṇissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sau tathā auvaditau tathānusiṭṭhau sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūrayantau taṃ bhūmiṃ anupāpuṇissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

sammāsambuddhassa tai satau imai dhammā anabhisambuddhāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sabbāsavaparikkhīṇassa tai satau imai āsavā aparikkhīṇāti naitaṃ  ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yassa tai  atthāya dhammau daisitau, sau na niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sāvakau khau pana tai dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau anudhammacārī sau pubbaina aparaṃ uḷāraṃ visaisādhigamaṃ na sacchikarissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

yai khau pana dhammā antarāyikā, tai paṭisaivatau nālaṃ antarāyāyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yai khau pana dhammā aniyyānikā, tai niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yai khau pana dhammā niyyānikā, tai niyyanti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| sāvakau khau pana tai saupādisaisau anupādisaisaṃ nibbānadhātuṃ anupāpuṇissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

diṭṭhisampan‍nau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya hatthaihi vā pādaihi vā suhataṃ karaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, puthuj‍janau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya hatthaihi vā pādaihi vā suhataṃ karaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| aivaṃ pitaraṃ, arahantaṃ, bhikkhuṃ| diṭṭhisampan‍nau puggalau saṅghaṃ bhindaiyya saṅghai vā saṅgharājiṃ janaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, puthuj‍janau saṅghaṃ  bhindaiyya saṅghai vā saṅgharājiṃ janaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, diṭṭhisampan‍nau tathāgatassa duṭṭhacittau lauhitaṃ uppādaiyya, parinibbutassa vā tathāgatassa duṭṭhacittau thūpaṃ bhindaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| puthuj‍janau tathāgatassa duṭṭhacittau lauhitaṃ uppādaiyya, parinibbutassa vā tathāgatassa duṭṭhacittau thūpaṃ bhindaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| diṭṭhisampan‍nau añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ apadisaiyya api jīvitahaitūti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, puthuj‍janau añ‍ñaṃ satthāraṃ apadisaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| diṭṭhisampan‍nau itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ pariyaisaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, puthuj‍janau itau bahiddhā añ‍ñaṃ dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ pariyaisaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, diṭṭhisampan‍nau kutūhalamaṅgalaina suddhiṃ pac‍caiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| puthuj‍janau kutūhalamaṅgalaina suddhiṃ pac‍caiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

57. itthī rājā cak‍kavattī siyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau rājā cak‍kavattī siyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; itthī sak‍kau daivānamindau siyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau sak‍kau daivānamindau siyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; itthī mārau pāpimā siyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau mārau pāpimā  siyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; itthī mahābrahmā siyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau mahābrahmā siyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati; itthī tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau  siyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau siyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

dvai tathāgatā arahantau sammāsambuddhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ aikissā laukadhātuyā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ vā dhammaṃ vā daisaiyyunti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, aikauva tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau aikissā laukadhātuyā uppaj‍jissati vā dhammaṃ vā daisaissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

tiṇṇaṃ duc‍caritānaṃ iṭṭhau kantau piyau manāpau vipākau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, tiṇṇaṃ duc‍caritānaṃ aniṭṭhau akantau appiyau amanāpau vipākau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ aniṭṭhau akantau appiyau amanāpau vipākau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ iṭṭhau kantau piyau manāpau vipākau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati|

añ‍ñatarau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā kuhakau lapakau naimittakau kuhanalapananaimittakattaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ katvā pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai appahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu anupaṭṭhitassati anupaṭṭhitasati (sī॰) viharantau satta baujjhaṅgai abhāvayitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, añ‍ñatarau samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā sabbadausāpagatau pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu upaṭṭhitassati viharantau satta baujjhaṅgai bhāvayitvā anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambujjhissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau idaṃ vuc‍cati ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

iti ṭhānāṭṭhānagatā sabbai khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā kaici saggūpagā kaici apāyūpagā kaici nibbānūpagā, aivaṃ bhagavā āha –

58.

 sabbai  sattā passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.133 marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ|

yathākammaṃ gamissanti, puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagā|

nirayaṃ pāpakammantā, puñ‍ñakammā ca suggatiṃ|

aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavāti parinibbanti anāsavāti (sī॰ ka॰)||

sabbai  sattāti ariyā ca anariyā ca sak‍kāyapariyāpan‍nā ca sak‍kāyavītivattā ca| marissantīti dvīhi maraṇaihi dandhamaraṇaina ca adandhamaraṇaina ca, sak‍kāyapariyāpan‍nānaṃ adandhamaraṇaṃ sak‍kāyavītivattānaṃ dandhamaraṇaṃ| maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitanti khayā āyussa indriyānaṃ uparaudhā jīvitapariyantau maraṇapariyantau| yathākammaṃ gamissantīti kammassakatā| puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagāti kammānaṃ phaladassāvitā ca avippavāsau ca|

nirayaṃ pāpakammantāti apuñ‍ñasaṅkhārā| puñ‍ñakammā ca suggatinti puñ‍ñasaṅkhārā  sugatiṃ gamissanti| aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavāti sabbasaṅkhārānaṃ samatik‍kamanaṃ| taināha bhagavā – ‘‘sabbai…pai॰… nāsavā’’ti|

‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ| yathākammaṃ gamissanti, puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagā| nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti āgāḷhā ca nijjhāmā ca paṭipadā| ‘‘aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavā’’ti majjhimā paṭipadā| ‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ, yathākammaṃ gamissanti, puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagā, nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti ayaṃ saṃkilaisau| aivaṃ saṃsāraṃ nibbattayati| ‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti…pai॰… nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti imai tayau vaṭṭā dukkhavaṭṭau kammavaṭṭau kilaisavaṭṭau| ‘‘aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavā’’ti tiṇṇaṃ vaṭṭānaṃ vivaṭṭanā| ‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti…pai॰… nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti ādīnavau, ‘‘puñ‍ñakammā ca suggati’’nti assādau, ‘‘aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavā’’ti nissaraṇaṃ| ‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti…pai॰… nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti haitu ca phalañ‍ca, pañ‍cakkhandhā phalaṃ, taṇhā haitu, ‘‘aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavā’’ti maggau ca phalañ‍ca| ‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ| yathākammaṃ gamissanti, puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagā, nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, sau saṃkilaisau tividhau taṇhāsaṃkilaisau diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau duc‍caritasaṃkilaisauti|

59. tattha  taṇhāsaṃkilaisau tīhi taṇhāhi niddisitabbau – kāmataṇhāya bhavataṇhāya vibhavataṇhāya| yaina yaina vā pana vatthunā ajjhausitau, taina tainaiva niddisitabbau, tassā vitthārau chattiṃsāya taṇhāya jāliniyā vicaritāni| tattha diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau ucchaidasassataina niddisitabbau, yaina yaina vā pana vatthunā diṭṭhivasaina abhinivisati, ‘‘idamaiva  sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti taina tainaiva niddisitabbau, tassā vitthārau dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni| tattha duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau caitanā caitasikakammaina niddisitabbau, tīhi duc‍caritaihi kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina  manauduc‍caritaina, tassā vitthārau dasa akusalakammapathā| aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavāti idaṃ vaudānaṃ|

tayidaṃ vaudānaṃ tividhaṃ; taṇhāsaṃkilaisau samathaina visujjhati, sau samathau samādhikkhandhau, diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau vipassanāya visujjhati, sā vipassanā pañ‍ñākkhandhau, duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau sucaritaina visujjhati, taṃ sucaritaṃ sīlakkhandhau|

‘‘sabbai sattā marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ, yathākammaṃ gamissanti, puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagā, nirayaṃ pāpakammantā’’ti apuñ‍ñappaṭipadā, ‘‘puñ‍ñakammā ca suggati’’nti puñ‍ñappaṭipadā, ‘‘aparai ca maggaṃ bhāvaitvā, parinibbantināsavā’’ti puñ‍ñapāpasamatik‍kamappaṭipadā, tattha yā ca puñ‍ñappaṭipadā yā ca apuñ‍ñappaṭipadā, ayaṃ aikā paṭipadā sabbatthagāminī aikā apāyaisu, aikā daivaisu, yā ca puñ‍ñapāpasamatik‍kamā paṭipadā ayaṃ tattha tattha gāminī paṭipadā|

tayau rāsī – micchattaniyatau rāsi, sammattaniyatau rāsi, aniyatau rāsi, tattha yau ca micchattaniyatau rāsi yau ca sammattaniyatau rāsi aikā paṭipadā tattha tattha gāminī, tattha yau aniyatau rāsi, ayaṃ sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā| kaina kāraṇaina? pac‍cayaṃ labhantau nirayai upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau tiracchānayaunīsu upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau paittivisayaisu  upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau asuraisu upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau daivaisu upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau manussaisu upapaj‍jaiyya, pac‍cayaṃ labhantau parinibbāyaiyya, tasmāyaṃ sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā, yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau, idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā ñāṇaṃ dutiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

iti  sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā anaikadhātulaukau, tattha tattha gāminī paṭipadā nānādhātulaukau| tattha katamau anaikadhātulaukau? cakkhudhātu rūpadhātu cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, sautadhātu saddadhātu sautaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, ghānadhātu gandhadhātu ghānaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, jivhādhātu rasadhātu jivhāviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāyadhātu phauṭṭhabbadhātu kāyaviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, manaudhātu dhammadhātu manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, pathavīdhātu, āpaudhātu, taijaudhātu, vāyaudhātu, ākāsadhātu, viñ‍ñāṇadhātu, kāmadhātu, byāpādadhātu, vihiṃsādhātu, naikkhammadhātu, abyāpādadhātu, avihiṃsādhātu, dukkhadhātu, daumanassadhātu, avij‍jādhātu, sukhadhātu, saumanassadhātu, upaikkhādhātu, rūpadhātu, arūpadhātu, niraudhadhātu, saṅkhāradhātu, nibbānadhātu, ayaṃ anaikadhātulaukau|

tattha katamau nānādhātulaukau? añ‍ñā cakkhudhātu, añ‍ñā rūpadhātu, añ‍ñā cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aivaṃ sabbā| añ‍ñā nibbānadhātu| yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau, idaṃ vuc‍cati anaikadhātu nānādhātu ñāṇaṃ tatiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

60. iti anaikadhātu nānādhātukassa laukassa yaṃ yadaiva dhātuṃ sattā adhimuc‍canti, taṃ tadaiva adhiṭṭhahanti abhinivisanti, kaici rūpādhimuttā, kaici saddādhimuttā, kaici gandhādhimuttā, kaici rasādhimuttā, kaici phauṭṭhabbādhimuttā, kaici dhammādhimuttā, kaici itthādhimuttā, kaici purisādhimuttā, kaici cāgādhimuttā, kaici hīnādhimuttā, kaici paṇītādhimuttā, kaici daivādhimuttā, kaici manussādhimuttā, kaici nibbānādhimuttā| yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau, ayaṃ vainaiyyau, ayaṃ na vainaiyyau, ayaṃ saggagāmī, ayaṃ duggatigāmīti, idaṃ vuc‍cati sattānaṃ nānādhimuttikatā ñāṇaṃ catutthaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

iti tai yathādhimuttā ca bhavanti, taṃ taṃ kammasamādānaṃ samādiyanti| tai chabbidhaṃ kammaṃ samādiyanti – kaici laubhavasaina, kaici dausavasaina, kaici mauhavasaina, kaici saddhāvasaina, kaici vīriyavasaina, kaici pañ‍ñāvasaina| taṃ vibhaj‍jamānaṃ duvidhaṃ – saṃsāragāmi ca nibbānagāmi ca|

tattha yaṃ laubhavasaina dausavasaina mauhavasaina ca kammaṃ karauti, idaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ| tattha yaṃ saddhāvasaina kammaṃ karauti, idaṃ kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ  suk‍kavipākaṃ| tattha yaṃ laubhavasaina dausavasaina mauhavasaina saddhāvasaina ca kammaṃ karauti, idaṃ kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ| tattha yaṃ vīriyavasaina pañ‍ñāvasaina ca kammaṃ karauti, idaṃ kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammuttamaṃ kammasaiṭṭhaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati|

cattāri kammasamādānāni| atthi kammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ dukkhavipākaṃ, atthi kammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhaṃ āyatiṃ sukhavipākaṃ, atthi kammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nadukkhañ‍caiva āyatiṃ ca dukkhavipākaṃ, atthi kammasamādānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhañ‍caiva āyatiṃ ca sukhavipākaṃ| yaṃ aivaṃ jātiyaṃ kammasamādānaṃ, iminā puggalaina akusalakammasamādānaṃ  upacitaṃ avipak‍kaṃ vipākāya pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ na ca bhabbau abhinibbidhā gantunti taṃ  bhagavā na auvadati| yathā daivadattaṃ kaukālikaṃ sunakkhattaṃ licchaviputtaṃ, yai vā panañ‍ñaipi sattā micchattaniyatā imaisañ‍ca puggalānaṃ upacitaṃ akusalaṃ na ca tāva pāripūriṃ gataṃ, purā pāripūriṃ gacchati| purā phalaṃ nibbattayati, purā maggamāvārayati, purā vainaiyyattaṃ samatik‍kamatīti tai bhagavā asamattai auvadati| yathā puṇṇañ‍ca gauvatikaṃ acailañ‍ca kuk‍kuravatikaṃ|

61. imassa ca puggalassa akusalakammasamādānaṃ paripūramānaṃ maggaṃ āvārayissati purā pāripūriṃ gacchati, purā phalaṃ nibbattayati, purā maggamāvārayati, purā vainaiyyattaṃ samatik‍kamatīti taṃ bhagavā asamattaṃ auvadati| yathā āyasmantaṃ aṅgulimālaṃ|

sabbaisaṃ mudumajjhādhimattatā| tattha mudu ānaiñ‍jābhisaṅkhārā majjhaṃ avasaisakusalasaṅkhārā, adhimattaṃ akusalasaṅkhārā, yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau, idaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ upapaj‍javaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ aparāpariyavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ nirayavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ tiracchānavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ paittivisayavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ asuravaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ daivavaidanīyaṃ, idaṃ manussavaidanīyanti, idaṃ vuc‍cati atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau vipākavaimattatā ñāṇaṃ pañ‍camaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

62. iti tathā samādin‍nānaṃ kammānaṃ samādin‍nānaṃ jhānānaṃ vimaukkhānaṃ samādhīnaṃ samāpattīnaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, idaṃ vaudānaṃ, idaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ, aivaṃ saṃkilissati, aivaṃ vaudāyati, aivaṃ vuṭṭhahatīti ñāṇaṃ anāvaraṇaṃ|

tattha  kati jhānāni? cattāri  jhānāni| kati vimaukkhā? aikādasa ca aṭṭha ca satta ca tayau ca dvai ca| kati samādhī? tayau samādhī – savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi, avitak‍kau vicāramattau samādhi, avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| kati samāpattiyau? pañ‍ca samāpattiyau – sañ‍ñāsamāpatti asañ‍ñāsamāpatti naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāsamāpatti vibhūtasañ‍ñāsamāpatti vibhūtasamāpatti (sī॰ ka॰) niraudhasamāpatti|

tattha katamau saṃkilaisau? paṭhamajjhānassa kāmarāgabyāpādā saṃkilaisau| yai ca kuk‍kuṭajhāyī dvai paṭhamakā yau vā pana kauci hānabhāgiyau samādhi, ayaṃ saṃkilaisau| tattha katamaṃ  vaudānaṃ, nīvaraṇapārisuddhi, paṭhamassa jhānassa yai ca kuk‍kuṭajhāyī dvai pacchimakā yau vā pana kauci visaisabhāgiyau samādhi, idaṃ vaudānaṃ| tattha katamaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ? yaṃ samāpattivuṭṭhānakausal‍laṃ, idaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ| yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau, idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbaisaṃ jhānavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ saṃkilaisavaudānavuṭṭhānañāṇaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

63. iti tassaiva samādhissa tayau dhammā parivārā indriyāni balāni vīriyamiti, tāniyaiva indriyāni vīriyavasaina balāni bhavanti, ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina indriyāni, akampiyaṭṭhaina balāni, iti taisaṃ mudumajjhādhimattatā ayaṃ mudindriyau ayaṃ majjhindriyau ayaṃ tikkhindriyauti| tattha bhagavā tikkhindriyaṃ saṃkhittaina auvādaina auvadati, majjhindriyaṃ bhagavā saṃkhittavitthāraina auvadati, mudindriyaṃ bhagavā vitthāraina auvadati| tattha bhagavā tikkhindriyassa mudukaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ upadisati, majjhindriyassa bhagavā mudutikkhadhammadaisanaṃ  upadisati, mudindriyassa bhagavā tikkhaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ upadisati| tattha bhagavā tikkhindriyassa samathaṃ upadisati, majjhindriyassa bhagavā samathavipassanaṃ upadisati, mudindriyassa bhagavā vipassanaṃ upadisati| tattha bhagavā tikkhindriyassa nissaraṇaṃ upadisati, majjhindriyassa bhagavā ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca upadisati, mudindriyassa bhagavā assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca upadisati| tattha bhagavā tikkhindriyassa adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya pañ‍ñāpayati, majjhindriyassa bhagavā adhicittasikkhāya pañ‍ñāpayati, mudindriyassa bhagavā adhisīlasikkhāya pañ‍ñāpayati|

yaṃ  aittha ñāṇaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau anaudhisau ayaṃ imaṃ bhūmiṃ bhāvanañ‍ca gatau, imāya vailāya imāya anusāsaniyā aivaṃ dhātukau cāyaṃ ayaṃ cassa āsayau ayañ‍ca anusayau iti, idaṃ vuc‍cati parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyaparaupariyattavaimattatā ñāṇaṃ sattamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

iti tattha yaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ, aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattārīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikānipi jātisatāni anaikānipi jātisahassāni anaikānipi jātisatasahassāni anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai| amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ| tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau  aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau  tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nauti, iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

64. tattha saggūpagaisu ca sattaisu manussūpagaisu ca sattaisu apāyūpagaisu ca sattaisu imassa puggalassa laubhādayau ussan‍nā alaubhādayau mandā, imassa puggalassa alaubhādayau ussan‍nā laubhādayau mandā, yai vā pana ussan‍nā yai vā pana mandā imassa puggalassa imāni indriyāni upacitāni imassa puggalassa imāni indriyāni anupacitāni amukāya vā kappakauṭiyaṃ kappasatasahassai vā kappasahassai vā kappasatai vā kappai vā antarakappai vā upaḍḍhakappai vā saṃvaccharai vā upaḍḍhasaṃvaccharai vā māsai vā pakkhai vā divasai vā muhuttai vā iminā pamādaina vā pasādaina vāti| taṃ taṃ bhavaṃ bhagavā anussarantau asaisaṃ jānāti, tattha yaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā  micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā|

imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā, tattha saggūpagaisu ca sattaisu manussūpagaisu ca sattaisu apāyūpagaisu ca sattaisu iminā puggalaina aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ amukāya kappakauṭiyaṃ upacitaṃ kappasatasahassai vā kappasahassai vā  kappasatai vā kappai vā antarakappai vā upaḍḍhakappai vā saṃvaccharai vā upaḍḍhasaṃvaccharai vā māsai vā pakkhai vā divasai vā muhuttai vā iminā pamādaina vā pasādaina vāti| imāni bhagavatau dvai ñāṇāni – pubbainivāsānussatiñāṇañ‍ca dibbacakkhu ca aṭṭhamaṃ navamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

iti tattha yaṃ sabbañ‍ñutā pattā viditā sabbadhammā virajaṃ vītamalaṃ uppan‍naṃ sabbañ‍ñutañāṇaṃ nihatau mārau baudhimūlai, idaṃ bhagavatau dasamaṃ balaṃ sabbāsavaparikkhayaṃ ñāṇaṃ| dasabalasaman‍nāgatā hi buddhā bhagavantauti|

niyuttau vicayau hārasampātau|

3. yuttihārasampātau

65.

 tattha  katamau yuttihārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti||

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti rakkhitacittassa sammāsaṅkappagaucarau bhavissatīti yuj‍jati, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau sammādiṭṭhi bhavissatīti yuj‍jati, sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau viharantau udayabbayaṃ paṭivijjhissatīti yuj‍jati, udayabbayaṃ paṭivijjhantau sabbā duggatiyau jahissatīti yuj‍jati| sabbā duggatiyau jahantau sabbāni duggativinipātabhayāni samatik‍kamissatīti yuj‍jatīti|

niyuttau yuttihārasampātau|

4. padaṭṭhānahārasampātau

66. tattha  katamau padaṭṭhānau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti samathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti vipassanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti dassanabhūmiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti vīriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti bhāvanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ|

niyuttau padaṭṭhānau hārasampātau|

5. lakkhaṇahārasampātau

67. tattha  katamau lakkhaṇau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti idaṃ satindriyaṃ, satindriyai gahitai gahitāni bhavanti pañ‍cindriyāni| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya gahitau bhavati ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| taṃ kissa haitu? sammādiṭṭhitau hi sammāsaṅkappau pabhavati, sammāsaṅkappatau sammāvācā pabhavati, sammāvācātau sammākammantau pabhavati, sammākammantatau sammāājīvau pabhavati, sammāājīvatau sammāvāyāmau pabhavati, sammāvāyāmatau sammāsati pabhavati, sammāsatitau sammāsamādhi pabhavati, sammāsamādhitau sammāvimutti pabhavati, sammāvimuttitau sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ pabhavati|

niyuttau lakkhaṇau hārasampātau|

6. catubyūhahārasampātau

68. tattha  katamau catubyūhau hārasampātau|

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti rakkhitaṃ paripālīyatīti aisā nirutti| idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai duggatīhi parimuc‍citukāmā bhavissanti, tai dhammacārinau bhavissantīti ayaṃ aittha bhagavatau adhippāyau| kaukālikau hi sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu  thairaisu cittaṃ padausayitvā mahāpadumanirayai upapan‍nau| bhagavā ca satiārakkhaina caitasā saman‍nāgatau, suttamhi vuttaṃ ‘‘satiyā cittaṃ rakkhitabba’’nti|

niyuttau catubyūhau hārasampātau|

7. āvaṭṭahārasampātau

69. tattha  katamau āvaṭṭau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti samathau ayaṃ samathau (sī॰ ka॰)| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti vipassanā| ‘‘ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti dukkhapariñ‍ñā| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti samudayapahānaṃ| ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti niraudhau ayaṃ niraudhau (sī॰ ka॰)| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni|

niyuttau āvaṭṭau hārasampātau|

8. vibhattihārasampātau

70. tattha katamau vibhattihārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| kusalapakkhau kusalapakkhaina niddisitabbau| akusalapakkhau akusalapakkhaina niddisitabbau|

niyuttau vibhattihārasampātau|

9. parivattanahārasampātau

71. tattha  katamau parivattanau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| samathavipassanāya bhāvitāya niraudhau phalaṃ, pariñ‍ñātaṃ dukkhaṃ, samudayau pahīnau, maggau bhāvitau paṭipakkhaina|

niyuttau parivattanau hārasampātau|

10. vaivacanahārasampātau

72. tattha  katamau vaivacanau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti cittaṃ manau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ manindriyaṃ manāyatanaṃ vijānanā vijānitattaṃ, idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti naikkhammasaṅkappau abyāpādasaṅkappau avihiṃsāsaṅkappau, idaṃ vaivacanaṃ| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti sammādiṭṭhi nāma pañ‍ñāsatthaṃ pañ‍ñākhaggau pañ‍ñāratanaṃ pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautau pañ‍ñāpataudau pañ‍ñāpāsādau, idaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

niyuttau vaivacanau hārasampātau|

11. pañ‍ñattihārasampātau

73. tattha katamau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassā’’ti padaṭṭhānapañ‍ñatti satiyā| ‘‘sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti samathassa| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbaya’’nti dassanabhūmiyā nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti| ‘‘thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhū’’ti samudayassa anavasaisappahānapañ‍ñatti, ‘‘sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti bhāvanāpañ‍ñatti maggassa|

niyuttau pañ‍ñattihārasampātau|

12. autaraṇahārasampātau

74. tattha  katamau autaraṇau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’| ‘‘sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau’’ti sammādiṭṭhiyā gahitāya gahitāni bhavanti pañ‍cindriyāni, ayaṃ indriyaihi autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva indriyāni vij‍jā, vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau, avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbaṃ, ayaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaina autaraṇā|

tāniyaiva pañ‍cindriyāni tīhi khandhaihi saṅgahitāni – sīlakkhandhaina samādhikkhandhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina| ayaṃ khandhaihi autaraṇā|

tāni  yaiva pañ‍cindriyāni saṅkhārapariyāpan‍nāni| yai saṅkhārā anāsavā nau ca bhavaṅgā, tai saṅkhārā dhammadhātusaṅgahitā, ayaṃ dhātūhi autaraṇā|

sā dhammadhātu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā, yaṃ āyatanaṃ anāsavaṃ nau ca bhavaṅgaṃ, ayaṃ āyatanaihi autaraṇā|

niyuttau autaraṇau hārasampātau|

13. saudhanahārasampātau

75. tattha katamau saudhanau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| yattha ārambhau suddhau, sau pañhau visaj‍jitau bhavati| yattha pana ārambhau na suddhau, na tāva sau pañhau visaj‍jitau bhavati|

niyuttau saudhanau hārasampātau|

14. adhiṭṭhānahārasampātau

76. tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau hārasampātau?

tasmā  rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarauti gāthā| tasmā rakkhitacittassāti aikattatā| cittaṃ manau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ayaṃ vaimattatā| sammāsaṅkappagaucarauti aikattatā| naikkhammasaṅkappau abyāpādasaṅkappau avihiṃsāsaṅkappau  ayaṃ vaimattatā| sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārauti aikattatā| sammādiṭṭhi nāma yaṃ dukkhai ñāṇaṃ dukkhasamudayai ñāṇaṃ dukkhaniraudhai ñāṇaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṃ maggai ñāṇaṃ haitumhi ñāṇaṃ haitusamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ pac‍cayai ñāṇaṃ pac‍cayasamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ tattha tattha yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ abhisamayau sampaṭivaidhau sac‍cāgamanaṃ, ayaṃ vaimattatā| ñatvāna udayabbayanti aikattatā, udayaina avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, aivaṃ sabbaṃ samudayau bhavati| vayaina avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, aivaṃ sabbaṃ niraudhau hauti, ayaṃ vaimattatā| thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhūti aikattatā, thinaṃ nāma yā cittassa akal‍latā akammaniyatā, middhaṃ nāma yaṃ kāyassa līnattaṃ, ayaṃ vaimattatā| sabbā duggatiyau jahaiti aikattatā, daivamanussai vā upanidhāya  apāyā duggati, nibbānaṃ vā upanidhāya sabbā upapattiyau duggati, ayaṃ vaimattatā|

niyuttau adhiṭṭhānau hārasampātau|

15. parikkhārahārasampātau

77. tattha katamau parikkhārau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau’’ti gāthā| ayaṃ samathavipassanāya parikkhārau|

niyuttau parikkhārau hārasampātau|

16. samāraupanahārasampātau

78. tattha katamau samāraupanau hārasampātau?

‘‘tasmā  rakkhitacittassa, sammāsaṅkappagaucarau|

sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārau, ñatvāna udayabbayaṃ|

thinamiddhābhibhū bhikkhu, sabbā duggatiyau jahai’’ti||
