tasmā  rakkhitacittassāti tiṇṇaṃ sucaritānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, cittai rakkhitai taṃ rakkhitaṃ bhavati kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ| sammādiṭṭhipuraikkhārauti sammādiṭṭhiyā bhāvitāya bhāvitau bhavati ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| kaina kāraṇaina? sammādiṭṭhitau hi sammāsaṅkappau pabhavati, sammāsaṅkappatau sammāvācā pabhavati, sammāvācātau sammākammantau pabhavati, sammākammantatau sammāājīvau pabhavati, sammāājīvatau sammāvāyāmau pabhavati, sammāvāyāmatau sammāsati pabhavati, sammāsatitau sammāsamādhi pabhavati, sammāsamādhitau sammāvimutti pabhavati, sammāvimuttitau sammāvimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ pabhavati| ayaṃ anupādisaisau puggalau anupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātu|

niyuttau samāraupanau hārasampātau|

taināha  āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau –

‘‘sauḷasa hārā paṭhamaṃ, disalaucanatau disā vilaukaitvā|

saṅkhipiya aṅkusaina hi, nayaihi tīhi niddisai sutta’’nti||

niyuttau hārasampātau|




nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ

79. tattha katamaṃ nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ? pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāyaca bhavataṇhāya ca, tattha avij‍jānīvaraṇaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanaṃ| avij‍jānīvaraṇā sattā avij‍jāsaṃyuttā avij‍jāya saṃyuttā (sī॰ ka॰) avij‍jāpakkhaina vicaranti, tai vuc‍canti diṭṭhicaritāti| taṇhāsaṃyaujanā sattā taṇhāsaṃyuttā taṇhāpakkhaina vicaranti, tai vuc‍canti taṇhācaritāti| diṭṭhicaritā  itau bahiddhā pabbajitā attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| taṇhācaritā itau bahiddhā pabbajitā kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti|

tattha  kiṃkāraṇaṃ yaṃ diṭṭhicaritā itau bahiddhā pabbajitā attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti, taṇhācaritā itau bahiddhā pabbajitā kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti? itau bahiddhā natthi sac‍cavavatthānaṃ, kutau catusac‍cappakāsanā vā samathavipassanākausal‍laṃ vā upasamasukhappatti vā! tai upasamasukhassa anabhiñ‍ñā viparītacaitā aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘natthi sukhaina sukhaṃ, dukkhaina nāma sukhaṃ adhigantabba’’nti| yau kāmai paṭisaivati, sau laukaṃ vaḍḍhayati, yau laukaṃ vaḍḍhayati, sau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavatīti tai aivaṃsañ‍ñī aivaṃdiṭṭhī dukkhaina sukhaṃ patthayamānā kāmaisu puñ‍ñasañ‍ñī attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā ca viharanti kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttā ca, tai tadabhiñ‍ñā santā raugamaiva vaḍḍhayanti, gaṇḍamaiva vaḍḍhayanti, sal‍lamaiva vaḍḍhayanti, tai raugābhitun‍nā gaṇḍapaṭipīḷitā sal‍lānuviddhā nirayatiracchānayaunipaitāsuraisu ummuj‍janimuj‍jāni karauntā ugghātanigghātaṃ pac‍canubhauntā raugagaṇḍasal‍labhaisaj‍jaṃ na vindanti| tattha attakilamathānuyaugau kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau ca saṃkilaisau, samathavipassanā vaudānaṃ| attakilamathānuyaugau kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau ca raugau, samathavipassanā rauganigghātakabhaisaj‍jaṃ| attakilamathānuyaugau kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau ca gaṇḍau, samathavipassanā gaṇḍanigghātakabhaisaj‍jaṃ| attakilamathānuyaugau kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau ca sal‍lau, samathavipassanā sal‍luddharaṇabhaisaj‍jaṃ|

tattha saṃkilaisau dukkhaṃ, tadabhisaṅgau taṇhā samudayau, taṇhāniraudhau dukkhaniraudhau, samathavipassanā dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, imāni  cattāri sac‍cāni| dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, samudayau pahātabbau, maggau bhāvaitabbau, niraudhau sacchikātabbau|

80. tattha diṭṭhicaritā rūpaṃ attatau upagacchanti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau upagacchanti| taṇhācaritā rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti| attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ, vaidanāvantaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvantaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhāravantaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi|

tassā paṭipakkhau laukuttarā sammādiṭṭhi, anvāyikā sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi, ayaṃ ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| tai tayau khandhā sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau pañ‍ñākkhandhau| sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau ca samathau, pañ‍ñākkhandhau vipassanā| tattha sak‍kāyau dukkhaṃ, sak‍kāyasamudayau dukkhasamudayau, sak‍kāyaniraudhau  dukkhaniraudhau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, samudayau pahātabbau, maggau bhāvaitabbau, niraudhau sacchikātabbau|

tattha yai rūpaṃ attatau upagacchanti| vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau upagacchanti| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘ucchaidavādinau’’ti| yai rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti| attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| yai vaidanāvantaṃ…pai॰… yai sañ‍ñāvantaṃ…pai॰… yai saṅkhāravantaṃ…pai॰… yai viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ upagacchanti, attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| imai vuc‍canti ‘‘sassatavādinau’’ti, tattha ucchaidasassatavādā ubhau antā, ayaṃ saṃsārapavatti| tassa  paṭipakkhau majjhimā paṭipadā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, ayaṃ saṃsāranivatti| tattha pavatti dukkhaṃ, tadabhisaṅgau taṇhā samudayau, taṇhāniraudhau dukkhaniraudhau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau dukkhaniraudhagāminī  paṭipadā, imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, samudayau pahātabbau, maggau bhāvaitabbau, niraudhau sacchikātabbau|

tattha ucchaidasassataṃ samāsatau vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vitthāratau dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni, taisaṃ paṭipakkhau taicattālīsaṃ baudhipakkhiyā dhammā aṭṭha vimaukkhā dasa kasiṇāyatanāni| dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni mauhajālaṃ anādianidhanappavattaṃ| taicattālīsaṃ taitālīsaṃ (sī॰) baudhipakkhiyā dhammā ñāṇavajiraṃ mauhajālappadālanaṃ| tattha mauhau avij‍jā, jālaṃ bhavataṇhā, taina vuc‍cati ‘‘pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāya ca bhavataṇhāya cā’’ti|

81. tattha diṭṭhicaritau asmiṃ sāsanai pabbajitau sal‍laikhānusantatavutti bhavati sal‍laikhai tibbagāravau| taṇhācaritau asmiṃ sāsanai pabbajitau sikkhānusantatavutti bhavati sikkhāya tibbagāravau| diṭṭhicaritau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamantau dhammānusārī bhavati| taṇhācaritau sammattaniyāmaṃ auk‍kamantau saddhānusārī bhavati, diṭṭhicaritau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti| taṇhācaritau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti|

tattha kiṃkāraṇaṃ, yaṃ taṇhācaritau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyāti, tassa hi kāmā aparic‍cattā bhavanti, sau  kāmaihi vivaiciyamānau dukkhaina paṭinissarati  dandhañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānāti? yau panāyaṃ diṭṭhicaritau ayaṃ āditauyaiva kāmaihi anatthikau bhavati| sau tatau vivaiciyamānau khippañ‍ca paṭinissarati, khippañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānāti| dukkhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā ca khippābhiñ‍ñā ca| sukhāpi paṭipadā duvidhā dandhābhiñ‍ñā ca khippābhiñ‍ñā ca| sattāpi duvidhā mudindriyāpi tikkhindriyāpi| yai mudindriyā, tai dandhañ‍ca paṭinissaranti dandhañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānanti| yai tikkhindriyā, tai khippañ‍ca paṭinissaranti, khippañ‍ca dhammaṃ ājānanti, imā catassau paṭipadā| yai hi kaici niyyiṃsu vā niyyanti vā niyyissanti vā, tai imāhi aiva catūhi paṭipadāhi| aivaṃ ariyā catuk‍kamaggaṃ pañ‍ñāpainti abudhajanasaivitāya bālakantāya rattavāsiniyā nandiyā bhavataṇhāya avaṭṭanatthaṃ āvaṭṭanatthaṃ (sī॰ ka॰)| ayaṃ vuc‍cati nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa bhūmīti, taināha ‘‘taṇhañ‍ca avij‍jampi ca samathainā’’ti|

82. vaiyyākaraṇaisu  hi yai kusalākusalāti tai duvidhā upaparikkhitabbā – laukavaṭṭānusārī ca laukavivaṭṭānusārī ca| vaṭṭaṃ nāma saṃsārau| vivaṭṭaṃ nibbānaṃ| kammakilaisā haitu saṃsārassa| tattha kammaṃ caitanā caitasikañ‍ca niddisitabbaṃ| taṃ kathaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ? upacayaina sabbaipi kilaisā catūhi vipal‍lāsaihi niddisitabbā| tai kattha daṭṭhabbā? dasa vatthukai kilaisapuñ‍jai| katamāni  dasa vatthūni? cattārau āhārā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattārau yaugā, cattārau ganthā, cattārau āsavā, cattārau aughā, cattārau sal‍lā, catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau cattāri agatigamanāni| paṭhamai āhārai paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau, dutiyai āhārai dutiyau vipal‍lāsau, tatiyai āhārai tatiyau vipal‍lāsau, catutthai āhārai catutthau vipal‍lāsau| paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai paṭhamaṃ upādānaṃ| dutiyai vipal‍lāsai dutiyaṃ upādānaṃ, tatiyai vipal‍lāsai tatiyaṃ upādānaṃ, catutthai vipal‍lāsai catutthaṃ upādānaṃ| paṭhamai upādānai paṭhamau yaugau, dutiyai upādānai dutiyau yaugau, tatiyai upādānai tatiyau yaugau, catutthai upādānai catutthau yaugau| paṭhamai yaugai paṭhamau ganthau, dutiyai yaugai dutiyau ganthau, tatiyai yaugai tatiyau ganthau, catutthai yaugai catutthau ganthau, paṭhamai ganthai paṭhamau āsavau, dutiyai ganthai dutiyau āsavau, tatiyai ganthai tatiyau āsavau, catutthai ganthai catutthau āsavau| paṭhamai āsavai paṭhamau aughau, dutiyai āsavai dutiyau aughau, tatiyai āsavai tatiyau aughau, catutthai āsavai catutthau aughau| paṭhamai aughai paṭhamau sal‍lau, dutiyai aughai dutiyau sal‍lau, tatiyai aughai tatiyau sal‍lau, catutthai aughai catutthau sal‍lau| paṭhamai sal‍lai paṭhamā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, dutiyai sal‍lai dutiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, tatiyai sal‍lai tatiyā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, catutthai sal‍lai catutthī catutthā (sī॰) viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, paṭhamāyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyaṃ paṭhamaṃ agatigamanaṃ| dutiyāyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyaṃ  dutiyaṃ agatigamanaṃ| tatiyāyaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyaṃ tatiyaṃ agatigamanaṃ, catutthiyaṃ catutthāyaṃ (sī॰) viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyaṃ catutthaṃ agatigamanaṃ|

83. tattha  yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau phassau āhārau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca manausañ‍caitanāhārau yau ca viñ‍ñāṇāhārau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, yau ca ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau, yau ca ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha  yañ‍ca kāmupādānaṃ yañ‍ca bhavupādānaṃ, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yañ‍ca diṭṭhupādānaṃ yañ‍ca attavādupādānaṃ, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca kāmayaugau, yau ca bhavayaugau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca diṭṭhiyaugau, yau ca avij‍jāyaugau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca abhijjhākāyaganthau, yau ca byāpādau kāyaganthau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca parāmāsakāyaganthau, yau ca idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca kāmāsavau, yau ca bhavāsavau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca diṭṭhāsavau, yau ca avij‍jāsavau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca kāmaughau, yau ca bhavaughau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca diṭṭhaughau, yau ca avij‍jaughau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yau ca rāgasal‍lau, yau ca dausasal‍lau, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yau ca mānasal‍lau, yau ca mauhasal‍lau, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yā ca rūpūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, yā ca vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yā ca sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, yā ca saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| tattha yañ‍ca chandā agatigamanaṃ yañ‍ca dausā agatigamanaṃ, imai taṇhācaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā| yañ‍ca bhayā agatigamanaṃ, yañ‍ca mauhā agatigamanaṃ, imai diṭṭhicaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā|

84. tattha kabaḷīkārai āhārai ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, phassai āhārai ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, viñ‍ñāṇai āhārai ‘‘anic‍cai  nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau, manausañ‍caitanāya āhārai ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau| paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau kāmai upādiyati, idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmupādānaṃ; dutiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau anāgataṃ bhavaṃ upādiyati, idaṃ vuc‍cati bhavupādānaṃ; tatiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau saṃsārābhinandiniṃ diṭṭhiṃ upādiyati, idaṃ vuc‍cati diṭṭhupādānaṃ; catutthai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau attānaṃ kappiyaṃ upādiyati, idaṃ vuc‍cati attavādupādānaṃ|

kāmupādānaina kāmaihi saṃyuj‍jati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati kāmayaugau; bhavupādānaina bhavaihi saṃyuj‍jati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati bhavayaugau; diṭṭhupādānaina pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā saṃyuj‍jati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati diṭṭhiyaugau; attavādupādānaina avij‍jāya saṃyuj‍jati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati avij‍jāyaugau|

paṭhamai yaugai ṭhitau abhijjhāya kāyaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati abhijjhākāyaganthau; dutiyai yaugai ṭhitau byāpādaina kāyaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati byāpādakāyaganthau; tatiyai yaugai ṭhitau parāmāsaina kāyaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati parāmāsakāyaganthau; catutthai yaugai ṭhitau idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisaina kāyaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau|

tassa aivaṃganthitā kilaisā āsavanti| kutau ca vuc‍cati āsavantīti? anusayatau vā pariyuṭṭhānatau vā| tattha abhijjhākāyaganthaina kāmāsavau, byāpādakāyaganthaina bhavāsavau, parāmāsakāyaganthaina diṭṭhāsavau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthaina avij‍jāsavau|

tassa imai cattārau āsavā vaipul‍laṃ gatā aughā bhavanti| iti āsavavaipul‍lā aughavaipul‍laṃ| tattha kāmāsavaina kāmaughau, bhavāsavaina bhavaughau, diṭṭhāsavaina diṭṭhaughau, avij‍jāsavaina avij‍jaughau|

tassa imai cattārau aughā anusayasahagatā ajjhāsayaṃ anupaviṭṭhā hadayaṃ āhac‍ca tiṭṭhanti, taina vuc‍canti sal‍lāiti| tattha kāmaughaina rāgasal‍lau, bhavaughaina dausasal‍lau, diṭṭhaughaina mānasal‍lau, avij‍jaughaina mauhasal‍lau|

tassa  imaihi catūhi sal‍laihi pariyādin‍naṃ pariyādiṇṇaṃ (ka॰) viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catūsu dhammaisu saṇṭhahati rūpai vaidanāya sañ‍ñāya saṅkhāraisu| tattha rāgasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaina viñ‍ñāṇaina rūpūpagā  viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, dausasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaina viñ‍ñāṇaina vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, mānasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaina  viñ‍ñāṇaina sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, mauhasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaina viñ‍ñāṇaina saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti|

tassa imāhi catūhi viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīhi upatthaddhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catūhi dhammaihi agatiṃ gacchati chandā dausā bhayā mauhā| tattha rāgaina chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausaina dausāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayaina bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhaina mauhāgatiṃ gacchati| iti khau tañ‍ca kammaṃ imai ca kilaisā, aisa haitu saṃsārassa, aivaṃ sabbai kilaisā catūhi vipal‍lāsaihi niddisitabbā|

85. tattha imā catassau disā kabaḷīkārau āhārau ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, kāmupādānaṃ, kāmayaugau, abhijjhākāyaganthau, kāmāsavau, kāmaughau, rāgasal‍lau, rūpūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, chandā agatigamananti paṭhamā disā|

phassau āhārau, ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, bhavupādānaṃ, bhavayaugau, byāpādakāyaganthau, bhavāsavau, bhavaughau, dausasal‍lau, vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, dausā agatigamananti dutiyā disā|

viñ‍ñāṇāhārau ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, diṭṭhiyaugau parāmāsakāyaganthau, diṭṭhāsavau, diṭṭhaughau, mānasal‍lau, sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, bhayā agatigamananti tatiyā disā|

manausañ‍caitanāhārau ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau, attavādupādānaṃ, avij‍jāyaugau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau, avij‍jāsavau, avij‍jaughau, mauhasal‍lau, saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, mauhā agatigamananti catutthī disā|

tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau yau ca ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, kāmupādānaṃ, kāmayaugau, abhijjhākāyaganthau, kāmāsavau, kāmaughau, rāgasal‍lau, rūpūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti chandā agatigamananti, imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| imai rāgacaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā|

tattha  yau ca phassau āhārau yau ca ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, bhavupādānaṃ, bhavayaugau, byāpādakāyaganthau, bhavāsavau, bhavaughau, dausasal‍lau, vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, dausā agatigamananti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ, imai dausacaritassa puggalassa upak‍kilaisā|

tattha yau ca viñ‍ñāṇāhārau yau ca ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau, diṭṭhupādānaṃ, diṭṭhiyaugau, parāmāsakāyaganthau, diṭṭhāsavau, diṭṭhaughau, mānasal‍lau, sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, bhayā agatigamananti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| imai diṭṭhicaritassa mandassa upak‍kilaisā|

tattha yau ca manausañ‍caitanāhārau yau ca ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau, attavādupādānaṃ, avij‍jāyaugau, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau, avij‍jāsavau, avij‍jaughau, mauhasal‍lau, saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti, mauhā agatigamananti, imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| imai diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa udatthassa (sī॰ ka॰) upak‍kilaisā|

tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau yau ca phassau āhārau, imai appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchanti, viñ‍ñāṇāhārau suñ‍ñatāya, manausañ‍caitanāhārau animittaina, tattha yau ca ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, yau ca ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau, imai appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ abbhatthaṃ gacchanti| ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau suñ‍ñatāya, ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau animittaina| tattha kāmupādānañ‍ca bhavupādānañ‍ca appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti| diṭṭhupādānaṃ suñ‍ñatāya, attavādupādānaṃ animittaina| tattha kāmayaugau ca bhavayaugau ca appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, diṭṭhiyaugau suñ‍ñatāya, avij‍jāyaugau animittaina| tattha abhijjhākāyaganthau ca byāpādakāyaganthau ca appaṇihitaina  vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, parāmāsakāyaganthau  suñ‍ñatāya, idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthau animittaina|

tattha kāmāsavau ca bhavāsavau ca appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, diṭṭhāsavau suñ‍ñatāya, avij‍jāsavau animittaina| tattha kāmaughau ca bhavaughau ca appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, diṭṭhaughau suñ‍ñatāya, avij‍jaughau animittaina| tattha rāgasal‍lau ca dausasal‍lau ca appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, mānasal‍lau suñ‍ñatāya, mauhasal‍lau animittaina| tattha rūpūpagā ca viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti vaidanūpagā  ca viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pariñ‍ñaṃ gacchanti, sañ‍ñūpagā suñ‍ñatāya, saṅkhārūpagā animittaina|

tattha chandā ca agatigamanaṃ dausā ca agatigamanaṃ appaṇihitaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti, bhayā agatigamanaṃ suñ‍ñatāya, mauhā agatigamanaṃ animittaina vimaukkhamukhaina pahānaṃ gacchanti| iti sabbai laukavaṭṭānusārinau dhammā niyyanti| tai laukā tīhi vimaukkhamukhaihi|

86. tatridaṃ niyyānaṃ –

catassau paṭipadā, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri jhānāni, cattārau vihārā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, catassau samādhibhāvanā, cattārau sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, catassau appamāṇā|

paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ, catutthī paṭipadā catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ| catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ| paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamau vihārau, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyau vihārau, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyau vihārau, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ catutthau vihārau| paṭhamau vihārau paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ, dutiyau vihārau dutiyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ, tatiyau vihārau tatiyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ, catutthau vihārau catutthaṃ  sammappadhānaṃ| paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau, dutiyaṃ dutiyau, tatiyaṃ tatiyau, catutthaṃ sammappadhānaṃ catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau| paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau paṭhamaṃ  adhiṭṭhānaṃ, dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau dutiyaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ, tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau tatiyaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ, catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau catutthaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ| paṭhamaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ paṭhamā samādhibhāvanā, dutiyaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ dutiyā samādhibhāvanā, tatiyaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ tatiyā samādhibhāvanā, catutthaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ catutthī samādhibhāvanā| paṭhamā samādhibhāvanā paṭhamau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau, dutiyā samādhibhāvanā dutiyau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau, tatiyā samādhibhāvanā tatiyau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau, catutthī samādhibhāvanā catutthau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau| paṭhamau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau paṭhamaṃ appamāṇaṃ, dutiyau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau dutiyaṃ appamāṇaṃ, tatiyau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau tatiyaṃ appamāṇaṃ, catutthau sukhabhāgiyau dhammau catutthaṃ  appamāṇaṃ| paṭhamā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā bahulikatā (ka॰) paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, dutiyā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, tatiyā paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, catutthī paṭipadā bhāvitā bahulīkatā catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti| paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānau bhāvitau bahulīkatau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti, dutiyau satipaṭṭhānau bhāvitau bahulīkatau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti, tatiyau satipaṭṭhānau bhāvitau bahulīkatau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti, catutthau satipaṭṭhānau bhāvitau bahulīkatau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti|

paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ paṭhamaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ dutiyaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ tatiyaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ  catutthaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti| paṭhamau vihārau bhāvitau bahulīkatau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādaṃ paripūraiti, dutiyau vihārau bhāvitau bahulīkatau uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ paripūraiti, tatiyau vihārau bhāvitau bahulīkatau anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādaṃ paripūraiti, catutthau vihārau bhāvitau bahulīkatau uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiṃ asammausaṃ bhiyyaubhāvaṃ paripūraiti| paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ mānappahānaṃ  paripūraiti, dutiyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ ālayasamugghātaṃ paripūraiti, tatiyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ avij‍jāpahānaṃ paripūraiti, catutthaṃ sammappadhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ bhavūpasamaṃ paripūraiti| mānappahānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, ālayasamugghātau bhāvitau bahulīkatau cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, avij‍jāpahānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, bhavūpasamau bhāvitau bahulīkatau upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti| sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ chandasamādhiṃ paripūraiti, cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ vīriyasamādhiṃ paripūraiti, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ cittasamādhiṃ paripūraiti, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ bhāvitaṃ bahulīkataṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhiṃ paripūraiti| chandasamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, vīriyasamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau tapaṃ paripūraiti, cittasamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau buddhiṃ paripūraiti, vīmaṃsāsamādhi bhāvitau bahulīkatau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggaṃ paripūraiti| indriyasaṃvarau bhāvitau bahulīkatau maittaṃ paripūraiti, tapau bhāvitau bahulīkatau karuṇaṃ paripūraiti, buddhi bhāvitā bahulīkatā muditaṃ paripūraiti, sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau bhāvitau bahulīkatau upaikkhaṃ paripūraiti|

87. tattha imā catassau disā paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamau  vihārau paṭhamau sammappadhānau paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ chandasamādhi indriyasaṃvarau maittā iti paṭhamā  disā|

dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyau satipaṭṭhānau dutiyau vihārau dutiyau sammappadhānau dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau bhavādhiṭṭhānaṃ vīriyasamādhi tapau karuṇā iti dutiyā disā|

tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyau satipaṭṭhānau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyau vihārau tatiyau sammappadhānau tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ cittasamādhi buddhi muditā iti tatiyā disā|

catutthī paṭipadā catutthau satipaṭṭhānau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ catutthau vihārau catutthau sammappadhānau catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhi sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau upaikkhā iti catutthī disā|

tattha  paṭhamā paṭipadā paṭhamau satipaṭṭhānau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭhamau vihārau paṭhamau sammappadhānau paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaṃ chandasamādhi indriyasaṃvarau, maittā iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| idaṃ rāgacaritassa puggalassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ|

dutiyā paṭipadā dutiyau satipaṭṭhānau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyau vihārau dutiyau sammappadhānau dutiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ vīriyasamādhi tapau karuṇā iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| idaṃ dausacaritassa puggalassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ|

tatiyā paṭipadā tatiyau satipaṭṭhānau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyau vihārau tatiyau sammappadhānau tatiyau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ cittasamādhi buddhi muditā iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| idaṃ diṭṭhicaritassa mandassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ|

catutthī paṭipadā catutthau satipaṭṭhānau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ catutthau vihārau catutthau sammappadhānau catutthau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ vīmaṃsāsamādhi sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau upaikkhā  iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| idaṃ  diṭṭhicaritassa udattassa bhaisaj‍jaṃ|

tattha dukkhā ca paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā dukkhā ca paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, sukhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha kāyai kāyānupassitā satipaṭṭhānañ‍ca vaidanāsu vaidanānupassitā satipaṭṭhānañ‍ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, cittai cittānupassitā suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ| dhammaisu dhammānupassitā animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha paṭhamau ca vihārau dutiyau ca vihārau appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, tatiyau vihārau suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, catutthau vihārau animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

yattha  paṭhamañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, tatiyaṃ sammappadhānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, catutthaṃ sammappadhānaṃ animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha mānappahānañ‍ca ālayasamugghātau ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, avij‍jāpahānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, bhavūpasamau animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha sac‍cādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca cāgādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha chandasamādhi ca vīriyasamādhi ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, cittasamādhi suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, vīmaṃsāsamādhi animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha  indriyasaṃvarau ca tapau ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, buddhi suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

tattha  maittā ca karuṇā ca appaṇihitaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ, muditā suñ‍ñataṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ upaikkhā animittaṃ vimaukkhamukhaṃ|

taisaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ| cattārau āhārā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau paṭipadā…pai॰… cattārau vipal‍lāsā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| cattāri upādānāni taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattāri jhānāni| cattārau yaugā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattārau vihārā| cattārau ganthā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattārau sammappadhānā| cattārau āsavā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā| cattārau aughā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni| cattārau sal‍lā taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau samādhibhāvanā| catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau tāsaṃ paṭipakkhau cattārau sukhabhāgiyā dhammā| cattāri agatigamanāni taisaṃ paṭipakkhau catassau appamāṇā|

sīhā buddhā pac‍caikabuddhā sāvakā ca hatarāgadausamauhā, taisaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā byantīkiriyā ca| vik‍kīḷitaṃ indriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ vik‍kīḷitaṃ vipariyāsānadhiṭṭhānañ‍ca| indriyāni saddhammagaucarau vipariyāsā kilaisagaucarau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīhavik‍kīḷitassa ca nayassa disālaucanassa ca nayassa bhūmīti| taināha ‘‘yau naiti vipal‍lāsaihi saṃkilaisai’’ti| vaiyyākaraṇaisu hi yai ‘‘kusalākusalā’’ti ca|

tattha  yai dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai dvai  puggalā; yai sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai dvai puggalā| taisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, cattārau āhārā, cattārau vipal‍lāsā, cattāri upādānāni, cattārau yaugā, cattārau ganthā, cattārau āsavā, cattārau aughā, cattārau sal‍lā, catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau, cattāri agatigamanānīti| taisaṃ  catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudānaṃ, catassau paṭipadā, cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri jhānāni, cattārau vihārā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau acchariyā abbhutā dhammā, cattāri adhiṭṭhānāni, catassau samādhibhāvanā, cattārau sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, catassau appamāṇā iti|

88. tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti imai dvai puggalā| yai sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai dvai puggalā| tattha yau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū| yau sādhāraṇāya, ayaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñū| yau dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ naiyyau|

tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa samathaṃ upadisati, naiyyassa vipassanaṃ, samathavipassanaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa| tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa mudukaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ upadisati, tikkhaṃ naiyyassa, mudutikkhaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa, tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisayati, saṃkhittavitthāraina vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa, vitthāraina naiyyassa| tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa nissaraṇaṃ upadisati, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca upadisati, naiyyassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca upadisati| tattha bhagavā ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa adhipañ‍ñāsikkhaṃ pañ‍ñāpayati, adhicittaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa, adhisīlaṃ naiyyassa|

tattha yai dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai dvai puggalā| yai sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti| imai dvai puggalā| iti khau cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti ugghaṭitañ‍ñū vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyauti|

taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, tīṇi akusalamūlāni laubhau akusalamūlaṃ dausau akusalamūlaṃ mauhau akusalamūlaṃ, tīṇi  duc‍caritāni – kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ; tayau akusalavitak‍kā – kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau; tissau akusalasañ‍ñā – kāmasañ‍ñā byāpādasañ‍ñā vihiṃsāsañ‍ñā; tissau viparītasañ‍ñā – nic‍casañ‍ñā sukhasañ‍ñā attasañ‍ñā; tissau vaidanā – sukhā vaidanā dukkhā vaidanā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā; tissau dukkhatā – dukkhadukkhatā saṅkhāradukkhatā vipariṇāmadukkhatā; tayau aggī – rāgaggi dausaggi mauhaggi; tayau sal‍lā – rāgasal‍lau dausasal‍lau mauhasal‍lau; tissau jaṭā – rāgajaṭā dausajaṭā mauhajaṭā; tissau akusalūpaparikkhā – akusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ akusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ akusalaṃ manaukammaṃ| tissau vipattiyau – sīlavipatti diṭṭhivipatti ācāravipattīti| taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudānaṃ| tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ adausau kusalamūlaṃ amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| tīṇi sucaritāni – kāyasucaritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manausucaritaṃ| tayau kusalavitak‍kā – naikkhammavitak‍kau abyāpādavitak‍kau  avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tayau samādhī – savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi avitak‍kau vicāramattau samādhi avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi| tissau kusalasañ‍ñā – naikkhammasañ‍ñā abyāpādasañ‍ñā avihiṃsāsañ‍ñā| tissau aviparītasañ‍ñā – anic‍casañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā| tissau kusalūpaparikkhā – kusalaṃ kāyakammaṃ kusalaṃ vacīkammaṃ kusalaṃ manaukammaṃ| tīṇi saucaiyyāni – kāyasaucaiyyaṃ vacīsaucaiyyaṃ manausaucaiyyaṃ; tissau sampattiyau – sīlasampatti samādhisampatti pañ‍ñāsampatti| tissau sikkhā – adhisīlasikkhā adhicittasikkhā adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā; tayau khandhā – sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau pañ‍ñākkhandhau| tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni – suñ‍ñataṃ animittaṃ appaṇihitanti|

iti khau cattāri hutvā tīṇi bhavanti, tīṇi hutvā dvai bhavanti taṇhācaritau ca diṭṭhicaritau ca|

taisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, taṇhā ca avij‍jā ca ahirikañ‍ca anauttappañ‍ca assati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca  ayaunisau manasikārau ca kausaj‍jañ‍ca dauvacassañ‍ca ahaṃkārau ca mamaṃkārau ca assaddhā ca pamādau ca asaddhammassavanañ‍ca asaṃvarau ca abhijjhā ca byāpādau ca nīvaraṇañ‍ca saṃyaujanañ‍ca kaudhau ca upanāhau ca makkhau ca palāsau ca issā ca macchairañ‍ca māyā ca sāṭhaiyyañ‍ca sassatadiṭṭhi ca ucchaidadiṭṭhicāti|

taisaṃ  dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ idaṃ vaudānaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā ca hirī ca auttappañ‍ca sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau ca vīriyārambhau ca sauvacassañ‍ca dhammai ñāṇañ‍ca anvayai ñāṇañ‍ca khayai ñāṇañ‍ca anuppādai ñāṇañ‍ca saddhā ca appamādau ca saddhammassavanañ‍ca saṃvarau ca anabhijjhā ca abyāpādau ca rāgavirāgā ca caitauvimutti avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti abhisamayau ca appicchatā ca santuṭṭhi ca ak‍kaudhau ca anupanāhau ca amakkhau ca apalāsau ca issāpahānañ‍ca macchariyappahānañ‍ca vij‍jā ca vimutti ca saṅkhatārammaṇau ca vimaukkhau asaṅkhatārammaṇau ca vimaukkhau saupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātu anupādisaisā ca nibbānadhātūti|

ayaṃ vuc‍cati tipukkhalassa ca nayassa aṅkusassa ca nayassa bhūmīti| taināha ‘‘yau akusalai samūlaihi naitī’’ti ‘‘aulaukaitvā disalaucanainā’’ti ca|

niyuttaṃ nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ|



sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ

89. tattha  aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā? sāsanapaṭṭhānai| tattha katamaṃ sāsanapaṭṭhānaṃ? saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ  suttaṃ, vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ, taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, duc‍caritasaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, taṇhāvaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, diṭṭhivaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, duc‍caritavaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

tattha saṃkilaisau tividhau – taṇhāsaṃkilaisau diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau| tattha taṇhāsaṃkilaisau samathaina visujjhati, sau samathau samādhikkhandhau| diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau vipassanāya visujjhati, sā  vipassanā pañ‍ñākkhandhau| duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau sucaritaina visujjhati, taṃ sucaritaṃ sīlakkhandhau| tassa sīlai patiṭṭhitassa yadi āsatti uppaj‍jati bhavaisu, aivaṃ sāyaṃ samathavipassanā bhāvanāmayaṃ puñ‍ñakriyavatthu bhavati tatrūpapattiyā saṃvattati| imāni cattāri suttāni, sādhāraṇāni katāni aṭṭha bhavanti, tāniyaiva aṭṭha suttāni sādhāraṇāni katāni sauḷasa bhavanti|

imaihi sauḷasahi suttaihi bhin‍naihi navavidhaṃ suttaṃ bhin‍naṃ bhavati| gāthāya gāthā anuminitabbā, vaiyyākaraṇaina vaiyyākaraṇaṃ anuminitabbaṃ| suttaina suttaṃ anuminitabbaṃ|

90. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā, taṇhāchadanachāditā|

pamattabandhanā  pamattabandhunā (udā॰ 74) baddhā, macchāva kumināmukhai|

jarāmaraṇamanvainti, vacchau khīrapakauva khīrūpakauva (ka॰) passa udā॰ 64 mātara’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni| katamāni cattāri? chandāgatiṃ chandā agatiṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.17 gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri agatigamanāni| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau, athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā, manausaiṭṭhā manaumayā|

manasā cai paduṭṭhaina, bhāsati vā karauti vā|

tatau naṃ dukkhamanvaiti, cak‍kaṃva vahatau pada’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘middhī  passa dha॰ pa॰ 325 yadā hauti mahagghasau ca, niddāyitā samparivattasāyī|

mahāvarāhauva nivāpapuṭṭhau, punappunaṃ gabbhamupaiti mandau’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, tatuṭṭhāya taduṭṭhāya (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 240 tamaiva khādati|

aivaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ, sāni tāni (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 240 kammāni nayanti duggati’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘caurau  yathā sandhimukhai gahītau, sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai ca|

aivaṃ ayaṃ paic‍ca pajā parattha, sakammunā hañ‍ñati bajjhatai cā’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina vihiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau na labhatai labhai (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 131 sukha’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘gun‍naṃ  cai taramānānaṃ, jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti, naittai jimhaṃ gatai jimhagatai (sī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.70 sati||

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau cai adhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukkhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sukiccharūpāvatimai manussā, karaunti pāpaṃ upadhīsu rattā|

gacchanti tai bahujanasan‍nivāsaṃ, nirayaṃ avīciṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānaka’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘phalaṃ  vai passa a॰ ni॰ 4.68 kadaliṃ hanti, phalaṃ vaiḷuṃ phalaṃ naḷaṃ|

sak‍kārau kāpurisaṃ hanti, gabbhau assatariṃ yathā’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kaudhamakkhagaru bhikkhu, lābhasak‍kāragāravau lābhasak‍kārakāraṇā (sī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.43|

sukhaittai pūtibījaṃva, saddhammai na virūhatī’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

91. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ paduṭṭhacittaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi, (yathā khau ayaṃ puggalau iriyati, yañ‍ca paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau, yañ‍ca maggaṃ samārūḷhau)  ( ) natthi a॰ ni॰ 1.43-44; itivu॰ 20| imamhi cāyaṃ samayai kālaṃ karaiyya, yathābhataṃ nikkhittau, aivaṃ nirayai| taṃ kissa haitu? cittaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, paduṭṭhaṃ padausitaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 1.43; itivu॰ 20 passitabbaṃ, caitaupadausahaitu cittapadausahaitu (sī॰ ka॰) khau pana, bhikkhavai, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca, tatthaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati –

‘‘paduṭṭhacittaṃ ñatvāna, aikac‍caṃ idha puggalaṃ|

aitamatthañ‍ca byākāsi, buddhau satthā (sī॰ ka॰) bhikkhūna santikai|

imamhi  cāyaṃ samayai, kālaṃ kayirātha puggalau|

nirayaṃ upapaj‍jaiyya, cittaṃ hissa padūsitaṃ|

caitaupadausahaitu hi, sattā gacchanti duggatiṃ||

yathābhataṃ nikkhipaiyya, aivamaiva tathāvidhau|

kāyassa bhaidā duppañ‍ñau, nirayaṃ saupapaj‍jatī’’ti||

ayampi atthau vuttau bhagavatā iti mai sutanti|

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sacai bhāyatha dukkhassa, sacai vau dukkhamappiyaṃ|

mākattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, āvi āvī (sī॰) passa udā॰ 44 vā yadi vā rahau||

‘‘sacai  ca pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, karissatha karautha vā|

na vau dukkhā pamutyatthi, upaic‍capi palāyata’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘adhammaina dhanaṃ laddhā, musāvādaina cūbhayaṃ|

mamaiti bālā mañ‍ñanti, taṃ kathaṃ nu bhavissati||

‘‘antarāyā su bhavissanti, sambhatassa vinassati|

matā saggaṃ na gacchanti, nanu aittāvatā hatā’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kathaṃ khaṇati attānaṃ, kathaṃ mittaihi jīrati|

kathaṃ vivaṭṭatai dhammā, kathaṃ saggaṃ na gacchati||

‘‘laubhā  khaṇati attānaṃ, luddhau mittaihi jīrati|

laubhā vivaṭṭatai dhammā, laubhā saggaṃ na gacchatī’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘caranti bālā dummaidhā, amittainaiva attanā|

karauntā pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, yaṃ hauti kaṭukapphalaṃ kaṭakaṃ phalaṃ (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 66||

‘‘na  taṃ kammaṃ kataṃ sādhu, yaṃ katvā anutappati|

yassa assumukhau raudaṃ, vipākaṃ paṭisaivatī’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘duk‍karaṃ duttitikkhañ‍ca, abyattaina ca aviyattaina (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.17 sāmañ‍ñaṃ|

bahū hi tattha sambādhā, yattha bālau visīdati||

‘‘yau hi atthañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca, bhāsamānai tathāgatai|

manaṃ padausayai bālau, maughaṃ khau tassa jīvitaṃ||

‘‘aitañ‍cāhaṃ arahāmi, dukkhañ‍ca itau ca pāpiyataraṃ bhantai|

yau appamaiyyaisu tathāgataisu, cittaṃ padausaimi avītarāgau’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘appamaiyyaṃ  paminantau, kaudha vidvā vikappayai|

appamaiyyaṃ pamāyinaṃ pamāyantaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.179, nivutaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai akissava’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘purisassa hi jātassa, kuṭhārī kudhārī (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.180 jāyatai mukhai|

yāya chindati attānaṃ, bālau dubbhāsitaṃ bhaṇaṃ||

‘‘na  hi satthaṃ sunisitaṃ, visaṃ halāhalaṃ iva|

aivaṃ viraddhaṃ pātaiti, vācā dubbhāsitā yathā’’ti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

92.

 ‘‘yau nindiyaṃ pasaṃsati, taṃ vā nindati yau pasaṃsiyau|

vicināti mukhaina sau kaliṃ, kalinā taina sukhaṃ na vindati||

‘‘appamattau ayaṃ kali, yau akkhaisu dhanaparājayau|

sabbassāpi sahāpi attanā, ayamaiva mahantatarau mahattarau (ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.3; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.180 kali|

yau sugataisu manaṃ padausayai||

‘‘sataṃ sahassānaṃ nirabbudānaṃ, chattiṃsatī pañ‍ca ca abbudāni|

yamariyagarahī  nirayaṃ upaiti, vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya pāpaka’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yau laubhaguṇai anuyuttau, sau vacasā vacasā ca (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 668 paribhāsati añ‍ñai|

assaddhau kadariyau anariyau (sī॰ ka॰) avadañ‍ñū, macchari paisuṇiyaṃ anuyuttau||

‘‘mukhadugga  vibhūta anariya, bhūnahu pāpaka duk‍kaṭakāri|

purisanta kalī avajātaputta avajātakaputta (sī॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 669, mā bahubhāṇidha nairayikausi||

‘‘rajamākirasī ahitāya, santai garahasi kibbisakārī|

bahūni duc‍caritāni caritvā, gacchasi khau papataṃ ciraratta’’nti||

idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā  dhammā, manausaiṭṭhā manaumayā|

manasā cai pasan‍naina, bhāsati vā karauti vā|

tatau naṃ sukhamanvaiti, chāyāva anapāyinī’’ti anupāyinīti (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 2||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

93. mahānāmau sak‍kau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idaṃ, bhantai, kapilavatthu iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bāhujañ‍ñaṃ bahujanaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1018 ākiṇṇamanussaṃ sambādhabyūhaṃ, sau khau ahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ vā payirupāsitvā manaubhāvanīyai vā bhikkhū sāyanhasamayaṃ kapilavatthuṃ pavisantau bhantainapi hatthinā samāgacchāmi, bhantainapi assaina samāgacchāmi, bhantainapi rathaina samāgacchāmi, bhantainapi sakaṭaina samāgacchāmi, bhantainapi purisaina samāgacchāmi, tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, tasmiṃ samayai mussataiva bhagavantaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati dhammaṃ ārabbha sati, mussati saṅghaṃ ārabbha sati| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ hauti ‘imamhi cāhaṃ sāyanhasamayai kālaṃ karaiyyaṃ, kā mayhaṃ mamassa (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1018 gati, kau abhisamparāyau’’’ti|

‘‘mā  bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma, apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati, apāpikā apāpikā tai (sī॰) kālaṅkiriyā| catūhi khau, mahānāma, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau  ariyasāvakau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| katamaihi catūhi? idha, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ…pai॰… buddhau bhagavāti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi…pai॰… samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| saiyyathāpi, mahānāma, rukkhau pācīnanin‍nau pācīnapauṇau pācīnapabbhārau, sau mūlacchin‍nau mūlaihi chin‍nau (sī॰ ka॰) katamaina papataiyyā’’ti? ‘‘yaina, bhantai, nin‍nau yaina pauṇau yaina pabbhārau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, mahānāma, imaihi catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau nibbānanin‍nau hauti nibbānapauṇau nibbānapabbhārau| mā bhāyi, mahānāma, mā bhāyi, mahānāma, apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati, apāpikā kālaṅkiriyā’’ti|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sukhakāmāni bhūtāni, yau daṇḍaina na hiṃsati|

attanau sukhamaisānau, paic‍ca sau labhatai sukha’’nti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘gun‍nañ‍cai  taramānānaṃ, ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā ujuṃ gacchanti, naittai ujuṃ gatai sati||

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau sacai sau caiva (sī॰) passa (sī॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.70 dhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti dhammikau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

94. bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina sambahulā bhikkhū bhagavatau  cīvarakammaṃ karaunti ‘‘niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti| taina khau pana samayaina isidattapurāṇā thapatayau sākaitai sādhukai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1002) paṭivasanti kainaci daiva karaṇīyaina| assausuṃ khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau ‘‘sambahulā kira bhikkhū bhagavatau cīvarakammaṃ karaunti| niṭṭhitacīvarau bhagavā taimāsac‍cayaina cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’’ti|

atha  khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau maggai purisaṃ ṭhapaisuṃ ‘‘yadā tvaṃ ambhau purisa passaiyyāsi bhagavantaṃ āgacchantaṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ, atha amhākaṃ āraucaiyyāsī’’ti| dvīhatīhaṃ ṭhitau khau sau purisau addasa bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ, disvāna yaina isidattapurāṇā thapatayau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā isidattapurāṇai thapatayau aitadavauca ‘‘ayaṃ sau bhantai ayaṃ bhantai (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1002 bhagavā āgacchati arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau, yassadāni kālaṃ mañ‍ñathā’’ti|

atha khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu, upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā bhagavantaṃ piṭṭhitau piṭṭhitau anubandhiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| isidattapurāṇā thapatayau bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu, aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau isidattapurāṇā thapatayau bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘yadā mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘sāvatthiyā kausalaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana  mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘sāvatthiyā kausalaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘kāsīsu magadhaisu kāsīhi māgadhai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1002) cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti daumanassaṃ ‘dūrai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘kāsīsu magadhaisu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, anappakā nau tasmiṃ samayai anattamanatā hauti anappakaṃ daumanassaṃ ‘dūrai nau bhagavā’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘magadhaisu kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti, hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti| yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘magadhaisu kāsīsu cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’ti, hauti nau  tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘kausalaisu sāvatthiṃ sāvatthiyaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) cārikaṃ pak‍kamissatī’ti| hauti nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā hauti saumanassaṃ ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā bhavissatī’ti|

‘‘yadā pana mayaṃ bhantai bhagavantaṃ suṇauma ‘sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai’ti hauti anappakā nau tasmiṃ samayai attamanatā, hauti anappakaṃ saumanassaṃ ‘āsan‍nai nau bhagavā’’’ti|

‘‘tasmātiha, thapatayau, sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā, alañ‍ca pana vau, thapatayau, appamādāyā’’ti| ‘‘atthi khau nau, bhantai, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅkhātatarau cā’’ti? ‘‘katamau pana vau, thapatayau, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅkhātatarau cā’’ti?

‘‘idha mayaṃ, bhantai, yadā rājā pasainadi kausalau uyyānabhūmiṃ niyyātukāmau gantukāmau (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1002 hauti, yai tai rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa nāgā aupavayhā, tai kappaitvā yā tā rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa pajāpatiyau piyā manāpā, tā tāsaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) aikaṃ puratau aikaṃ pacchatau nisīdāpaima, tāsaṃ khau pana, bhantai, bhaginīnaṃ aivarūpau gandhau hauti| saiyyathāpi nāma gandhakaraṇḍakassa tāvadaiva  vivariyamānassa, yathā taṃ rājakañ‍ñānaṃ rājārahaina (sī॰ ka॰) gandhaina vibhūsitānaṃ| tāsaṃ khau pana, bhantai, bhaginīnaṃ aivarūpau kāyasamphassau hauti, saiyyathāpi nāma  tūlapicunau vā kappāhapicunau vā, yathā taṃ rājakañ‍ñānaṃ sukhaidhitānaṃ| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhantai, samayai nāgaupi rakkhitabbau hauti| tāpi bhaginiyau rakkhitabbā hauti| attāpi rakkhitabbā hauti| na khau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, abhijānāma tāsu bhagīnisu pāpakaṃ cittaṃ ñappādaintā, ayaṃ khau nau, bhantai, aitamhā sambādhā añ‍ñau sambādhau sambādhatarau caiva sambādhasaṅghātatarau cāti|

‘‘tasmātiha, thapatayau, sambādhau gharāvāsau rajāpathau, abbhaukāsau pabbaj‍jā| alañ‍ca pana vau, thapatayau, appamādāya| catūhi khau thapatayau, dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi? idha, thapatayau, sutavā ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ…pai॰… buddhau bhagavāti, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… vigatamalamacchairaina caitasā agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, muttacāgau payatapāṇi vaussaggaratau yācayaugau dānasaṃvibhāgaratau appaṭivibhattaṃ| imaihi khau, thapatayau, catūhi dhammaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau hauti avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau|

‘‘tumhai khau, thapatayau, buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatā itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ…pai॰… buddhau bhagavāti, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… yaṃ khau pana kiñ‍ci kulai daiyyadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ appaṭivibhattaṃ sīlavantaihi kalyāṇadhammaihi, taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, thapatayau, katividhā tai kausalaisu manussā yai tumhākaṃ samasamā yadidaṃ dānasaṃvibhāgaihī’’ti? ‘‘lābhā nau, bhantai, suladdhaṃ nau, bhantai, yaisaṃ nau bhagavā aivaṃ pajānātī’’ti|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

95.

 ‘‘aikapupphaṃ  cajitvāna yajitvāna (ka॰) passa thairagā॰ 96, sahassaṃ kappakauṭiyau|

daivai caiva manussai ca, saisaina parinibbutau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘assatthai  haritaubhāsai, saṃvirūḷhamhi pādapai|

aikaṃ buddhagataṃ buddhakataṃ (ka॰) passa thairagā॰ 217 sañ‍ñaṃ, alabhiṃtthaṃ alabhiṃhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) patissatau||

‘‘aj‍ja tiṃsaṃ tatau kappā, nābhijānāmi duggatiṃ|

tissau vij‍jā sacchikatā, tassā sañ‍ñāya vāsanā’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘piṇḍāya  kausalaṃ puraṃ, pāvisi aggapuggalau|

anukampakau puraibhattaṃ, taṇhānighātakau muni||

‘‘purisassa vaṭaṃsakau hatthai, sabbapupphaihilaṅkatau|

sau addasāsi sambuddhaṃ, bhikkhusaṅghapurakkhataṃ||

‘‘pavisantaṃ rājamaggaina, daivamānusapūjitaṃ|

haṭṭhau cittaṃ pasādaitvā, sambuddhamupasaṅkami||

‘‘sau taṃ vaṭaṃsakaṃ surabhiṃ, vaṇṇavantaṃ manauramaṃ|

sambuddhassupanāmaisi, pasan‍nau saihi pāṇibhi||

‘‘tatau aggisikhā vaṇṇā, buddhassa lapanantarā|

sahassaraṃsi vij‍juriva, auk‍kā nikkhami ānanā||

‘‘padakkhiṇaṃ karitvāna, sīsai ādic‍cabandhunau|

tikkhattuṃ parivaṭṭaitvā, muddhanantaradhāyatha||

‘‘idaṃ disvā acchariyaṃ, abbhutaṃ laumahaṃsanaṃ|

aikaṃsaṃ cīvaraṃ katvā, ānandau aitadabravi||

‘‘‘kau haitu sitakammassa, byākarauhi mahāmunai|

dhammālaukau bhavissati, kaṅkhaṃ vitara nau munai||

‘‘‘yassa  taṃ sabbadhammaisu, sadā ñāṇaṃ pavattati|

kaṅkhiṃ  vaimatikaṃ thairaṃ, ānandaṃ aitadabravi||

‘‘‘yau sau ānanda purisau, mayi cittaṃ pasādayi|

caturāsītikappāni, duggatiṃ na gamissati||

‘‘‘daivaisu daivasaubhaggaṃ, dibbaṃ raj‍jaṃ pasāsiya|

manujaisu manujindau, rājā raṭṭhai bhavissati||

‘‘‘sau carimaṃ pabbajitvā, sacchikatvāna sacchikatvā ca (ka॰) dhammataṃ|

pac‍caikabuddhau dhutarāgau, vaṭaṃsakau nāma bhavissati||

‘‘‘natthi cittai passa vi॰ va॰ 804 pasan‍namhi, appakā nāma dakkhiṇā|

tathāgatai vā sambuddhai, atha vā tassa sāvakai||

‘‘‘aivaṃ  acintiyā passa apa॰ thaira 1.1.82 buddhā, buddhadhammā acintiyā|

acintiyai pasan‍nānaṃ, vipākau hauti acintiyau’’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

96. ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aikac‍caṃ puggalaṃ pasan‍nacittaṃ aivaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi ‘‘(yathā khau ayaṃ puggalau iriyati, yañ‍ca paṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍nau, yañ‍ca maggaṃ samārūḷhau) ( ) natthi a॰ ni॰ 1.43-44; itivu॰ 21| imamhi cāyaṃ samayai kālaṃ karaiyya, yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| taṃ kissa haitu? cittaṃ hissa, bhikkhavai, pasan‍naṃ, caitaupasādahaitu cittappasādahaitu (sī॰ ka॰) khau pana aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jantī’’ti| aitamatthaṃ bhagavā avauca, tatthaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati –

‘‘pasan‍nacittaṃ ñatvāna, aikac‍caṃ idha puggalaṃ|

aitamatthañ‍ca byākāsi, buddhau satthā (sī॰ ka॰) passa itivu॰ 21 bhikkhūna santikai||

‘‘imamhi  cāyaṃ samayai, kālaṃ kayirātha puggalau|

saggamhi upapaj‍jaiyya, cittaṃ hissa pasāditaṃ||

‘‘caitaupasādahaitu hi, sattā gacchanti suggatiṃ|

yathābhataṃ nikkhipaiyya, aivamaivaṃ tathāvidhau|

kāyassa bhaidā sappañ‍ñau, saggaṃ sau upapaj‍jatī’’ti||

‘‘ayampi atthau vuttau bhagavatā iti mai suta’’nti|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘suvaṇṇacchadanaṃ  nāvaṃ, nāri āruyha tiṭṭhasi|

augāhasi augāhasai (sī॰ ka॰) passa vi॰ va॰ 53 paukkharaṇiṃ, padmaṃ chindasi pāṇinā||

‘‘kaina tai tādisau vaṇṇau, ānubhāvau juti ca tai|

uppaj‍janti ca tai bhaugā, yai kaici manasicchitā||

‘‘pucchitā daivatai saṃsa, kissa kammassidaṃ phalaṃ|

sā daivatā attamanā, daivarājaina pucchitā||

‘‘pañhaṃ  puṭṭhā viyākāsi, sak‍kassa iti mai sutaṃ|

addhānaṃ paṭipan‍nāhaṃ, disvā thūpaṃ manauramaṃ||

‘‘tattha cittaṃ pasādaisiṃ, kassapassa yasassinau|

paddhapupphaihi pūjaisiṃ, pasan‍nā saihi tassaiva|

kammassa phalaṃ vipākau, aitādisaṃ katapuñ‍ñā labhantī’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘dānakathā sīlakathā saggakathā puñ‍ñakathā puñ‍ñavipākakathā’’ti|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘apicāpi  paṃsuthūpaisu uddissakataisu dasabaladharānaṃ tatthapi kāraṃ katvā saggaisu narā pamaudantī’’ti|

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

97.

 ‘‘daivaputtasarīravaṇṇā, sabbai subhagasaṇṭhitī|

udakaina paṃsuṃ taimaitvā, thūpaṃ vaḍḍhaitha kassapaṃ||

‘‘ayaṃ sugattai sugatassa thūpau, mahaisinau dasabaladhammadhārinau|

tasmiṃ yasmiṃ (sī॰) imai daivamanujā pasan‍nā, kāraṃ karauntā jarāmaraṇā pamuc‍carai’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘uḷāraṃ vata taṃ āsi, yāhaṃ thūpaṃ mahaisinau|

uppalāni  ca cattāri, mālañ‍ca abhiraupayiṃ||

‘‘aj‍ja tiṃsaṃ tatau kappā, nābhijānāmi duggatiṃ|

vinipātaṃ na gacchāmi, thūpaṃ pūjaitva pūjaitvā (ka॰) satthunau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘bāttiṃsalakkhaṇadharassa, vijitavijayassa laukanāthassa|

satasahassaṃ kappai, muditau thūpaṃ apūjaisi||

‘‘yaṃ  mayā pasutaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, taina ca puñ‍ñaina daiva saubhaggaṃ|

raj‍jāni ca kāritāni, anāgantuna vinipātaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ cakkhu adantadamakassa, sāsanai paṇihitaṃ tathā|

cittaṃ taṃ mai sabbaṃ, laddhaṃ vimuttacittamhi vidhūtalatau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

98.

 ‘‘sāmākapatthaudanamattamaiva hi, pac‍caikabuddhamhi adāsi dakkhiṇaṃ|

vimuttacittai akhilai anāsavai, araṇavihārimhi asaṅgamānasai||

‘‘tasmiñ‍ca  aukappayi dhammamuttamaṃ, tasmiñ‍ca dhammai paṇidhaisiṃ mānasaṃ|

aivaṃ vihārīhi mai saṅgamau siyā, bhavai kudāsupi ca mā apaikkhavā||

‘‘tassaiva kammassa vipākatau ahaṃ, sahassakkhattuṃ kurusūpapaj‍jatha kurūsūpapaj‍jatha (sī॰)|

dīghāyukaisu amamaisu pāṇisu, visaisagāmīsu ahīnagāmisu||

‘‘tassaiva kammassa vipākatau ahaṃ, sahassakkhattuṃ tidasaupapaj‍jatha|

vicitramālābharaṇānulaipisu, visiṭṭhakāyūpagatau yasassisu||

‘‘tassaiva kammassa vipākatau ahaṃ, vimuttacittau akhilau anāsavau|

imaihi mai antimadaihadhāribhi, samāgamau  āsihi tāhi tāsihi||

‘‘pac‍cakkhaṃ  khvimaṃ avaca tathāgatau jinau, samijjhatai sīlavatau yadicchati|

yathā yathā mai manasā vicintitaṃ, tathā samiddhaṃ ayamantimau bhavau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘aikatiṃsamhi kappamhi jinau anaijau, anantadassī bhagavā sikhīti|

tassāpi rājā bhātā sikhiddhai sikhaṇḍi (sī॰), buddhai ca dhammai ca abhippasan‍nau||

‘‘parinibbutai laukavināyakamhi, thūpaṃ sakāsi vipulaṃ mahantaṃ|

samantatau gāvutikaṃ mahaisinau, daivātidaivassa naruttamassa||

‘‘tasmiṃ manussau balimābhihārī, paggayha jātisumanaṃ pahaṭṭhau|

vātaina pupphaṃ patitassa aikaṃ, tāhaṃ gahaitvāna tassaiva dāsi||

‘‘sau maṃ avaucābhipasan‍nacittau, tuyhamaiva aitaṃ pupphaṃ dadāmi|

tāhaṃ gahaitvā abhiraupayaisiṃ, punappunaṃ buddhamanussarantau||

‘‘aj‍ja  tiṃsaṃ tatau kappā, nābhijānāmi duggatiṃ|

vinipātañ‍ca na gacchāmi, thūpapūjāyidaṃ phala’’nti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kapilaṃ nāma nagaraṃ, suvibhattaṃ mahāpathaṃ|

ākiṇṇamiddhaṃ phītañ‍ca, brahmadattassa rājinau||

‘‘kummāsaṃ  vik‍kiṇiṃ tattha, pañ‍cālānaṃ puruttamai|

sauhaṃ  addasiṃ sambuddhaṃ, upariṭṭhaṃ yasassinaṃ||

‘‘haṭṭhau cittaṃ pasādaitvā, nimantaisiṃ naruttamaṃ|

ariṭṭhaṃ dhuvabhattaina, yaṃ mai gaihamhi vij‍jatha||

‘‘tatau ca kattikau puṇṇau kattikā puṇṇā (ka॰), puṇṇamāsī upaṭṭhitā|

navaṃ dussayugaṃ gayha, ariṭṭhassaupanāmayiṃ||

‘‘pasan‍nacittaṃ ñatvāna, paṭiggaṇhi naruttamau|

anukampakau kāruṇikau, taṇhānighātakau muni||

‘‘tāhaṃ kammaṃ karitvāna, kalyāṇaṃ buddhavaṇṇitaṃ|

daivai caiva manussai ca, sandhāvitvā tatau cutau||

‘‘bārāṇasiyaṃ nagarai, saiṭṭhissa aikaputtakau|

aḍḍhai kulasmiṃ uppaj‍jiṃ, pāṇaihi ca piyatarau||

‘‘tatau ca viñ‍ñutaṃ pattau, daivaputtaina cauditau|

pāsādā aurūhitvāna, sambuddhamupasaṅkamiṃ||

‘‘sau mai dhammamadaisayi, anukampāya gautamau|

dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ, dukkhassa ca atik‍kamaṃ||

‘‘ariyaṃ  aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ|

cattāri ariyasac‍cāni, muni dhammamadaisayi||

‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā, vihariṃ sāsanai ratau|

samathaṃ paṭivijjhāhaṃ, rattindivamatanditau||

‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca bahiddhā ca, yai mai vij‍jiṃsu vijjhiṃsu (sī॰) āsavā|

sabbai āsuṃ samucchin‍nā, na ca uppaj‍jarai puna||

‘‘pariyantakataṃ dukkhaṃ, carimauyaṃ samussayau|

jātimaraṇasaṃsārau, natthidāni punabbhavau’’ti||

idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

99. tattha  katamaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘uddhaṃ adhau sabbadhi vippamuttau, ayaṃ ahasmīti ayamahamasmīti (sī॰) passa udā॰ 61 anānupassī|

aivaṃ vimuttau udatāri aughaṃ, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sīlavatau, ānanda, na caitanā caitanāya (a॰ ni॰ 11.2) karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai avippaṭisārau jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ sīlavatau avippaṭisārau jāyaiyya| avippaṭisārinā, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ avippaṭisārinau pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyaiyya| pamuditaina, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai pīti jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ pamuditassa pīti jāyaiyya| pītimanassa, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai kāyau passambhaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ pītimanassa kāyau passambhaiyya| passaddhakāyassa ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kintāhaṃ sukhaṃ vaidiyaiyya’nti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyaiyya| sukhinau ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai samādhi jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ sukhinau samādhi jāyaiyya| samāhitassa ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kintāhaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya’nti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānaiyya| yathābhūtaṃ pajānatā, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai nibbidā jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau nibbindaiyya| nibbindantaina, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai virāgau jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ nibbindantau viraj‍jaiyya| viraj‍jantaina ānanda na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai vimutti jāyaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ viraj‍jantau vimuc‍caiyya| vimuttaina, ānanda, na caitanā karaṇīyā ‘kinti mai vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyā’ti| dhammatā aisā, ānanda, yaṃ vimuttassa vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti|

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

100.

 ‘‘yadā  havai pātubhavanti dhammā, ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā, yatau pajānāti sahaitudhamma’’nti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yadā havai pātubhavanti dhammā, ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

athassa kaṅkhā vapayanti sabbā, yatau khayaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ avaidī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kiṃnu passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.243 kujjhasi mā kujjhi, ak‍kaudhau tissa tai varaṃ|

kaudhamānamakkhavinayatthaṃ hi, tissa brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kadāhaṃ nandaṃ passaiyyaṃ, ārañ‍ñaṃ arañ‍ñaṃ (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.242 paṃsukūlikaṃ|

añ‍ñātuñchaina yāpaintaṃ, kāmaisu anapaikkhina’’nti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kiṃsu chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kiṃsu chaitvā na saucati|

kissassu kissassa (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.187 aikadhammassa, vadhaṃ raucaisi gautamāti||

‘‘kaudhaṃ  chaitvā sukhaṃ saiti, kaudhaṃ chaitvā na saucati|

kaudhassa visamūlassa, madhuraggassa brāhmaṇa|

vadhaṃ ariyā pasaṃsanti, taṃ hi chaitvā na saucatī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘kiṃsu hanai uppatitaṃ, kiṃsu jātaṃ vinaudayai|

kiñ‍cassu pajahai dhīrau, kissābhisamayau sukhau||

‘‘kaudhaṃ  hanai uppatitaṃ, rāgaṃ jātaṃ vinaudayai|

avij‍jaṃ pajahai dhīrau, sac‍cābhisamayau sukhau’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

101.

 ‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva dayhamānaiva (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.21; thairagā॰ 39 passitabbaṃ matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai||

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, ḍayhamānauva matthakai|

sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sabbai khayantā nicayā, patanantā samussayā|

sabbaisaṃ maraṇamāgamma, sabbaisaṃ jīvitamaddhuvaṃ|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai maraṇaṃ (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.100 paikkhamānau, puñ‍ñāni kayirātha sukhāvahāni||

‘‘sabbai khayantā nicayā, patanantā samussayā|

sabbaisaṃ maraṇamāgamma, sabbaisaṃ jīvitamaddhuvaṃ|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ maraṇai paikkhamānau, laukāmisaṃ pajahai santipaikkhau’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sukhaṃ  sayanti munayau, na tai saucanti māvidha|

yaisaṃ jhānarataṃ cittaṃ, pañ‍ñavā susamāhitau|

āraddhavīriyau pahitattau, aughaṃ tarati duttaraṃ||

‘‘viratau kāmasañ‍ñāya, sabbasaṃyaujanātītau sabbasaṃyaujanātigau (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.96|

nandibhavaparikkhīṇau nandīrāgaparikkhīṇau (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.96, sau gambhīrai na sīdatī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘saddahānau arahataṃ, dhammaṃ nibbānapattiyā|

sussūsaṃ labhatai pañ‍ñaṃ, appamattau vicakkhaṇau||

patirūpakārī  dhuravā, uṭṭhātā vindatai dhanaṃ|

sac‍caina kittiṃ pappauti, dadaṃ mittāni ganthati|

asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ, aivaṃ savai (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.246 paic‍ca na saucatī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sabbaganthapahīnassa, vippamuttassa tai satau|

samaṇassa na taṃ sādhu, yadañ‍ñamanusāsasīti||

‘‘yaina kainaci vaṇṇaina, saṃvāsau sak‍ka jāyati|

na taṃ arahati sappañ‍ñau, manasā anukampituṃ ananukampitaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.236||

‘‘manasā cai pasan‍naina, yadañ‍ñamanusāsati|

na taina hauti saṃyuttau, yānukampā anuddayā’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

102.

 ‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca kutaunidānā, aratī ratī arati rati (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.237; su॰ ni॰ 273 passitabbaṃ laumahaṃsau kutaujā|

kutau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajanti||

‘‘rāgau  ca dausau ca itaunidānā, aratī ratī laumahaṃsau itaujā|

itau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajanti||

‘‘snaihajā attasambhūtā, nigraudhassaiva khandhajā|

puthu visattā kāmaisu, māluvāva vitatā vanai||

‘‘yai naṃ pajānanti yataunidānaṃ, tai naṃ vinaudainti suṇauhi yakkha|

tai duttaraṃ aughamimaṃ taranti, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāyā’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘duk‍karaṃ  bhagavā suduk‍karaṃ bhagavā’’ti|

‘‘duk‍karaṃ vāpi karaunti, [kāmadāti bhagavā]

saikkhā sīlasamāhitā|

ṭhitattā anagāriyupaitassa, tuṭṭhi hauti sukhāvahā’’ti||

‘‘dul‍labhā dul‍labhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.87 bhagavā yadidaṃ tuṭṭhī’’ti|

‘‘dul‍labhaṃ vāpi labhanti, [kāmadāti bhagavā]

cittavūpasamai ratā|

yaisaṃ divā ca rattau ca, bhāvanāya ratau manau’’ti||

‘‘dussamādahaṃ bhagavā yadidaṃ citta’’nti|

‘‘dussamādahaṃ vāpi samādahanti, [kāmadāti bhagavā]

indriyūpasamai ratā|

tai chaitvā mac‍cunau jālaṃ, ariyā gacchanti kāmadā’’ti||

‘‘duggamau bhagavā visamau maggau’’ti|

‘‘duggamai  visamai vāpi, ariyā gacchanti kāmada kāmadā (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.87|

anariyā visamai maggai, papatanti avaṃsirā|

ariyānaṃ samau maggau, ariyā hi visamai samā’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

103.

 ‘‘idaṃ hi passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.101 taṃ jaitavanaṃ, isisaṅghanisaivitaṃ|

āvutthaṃ dhammarājaina, pītisañ‍jananaṃ mama||

‘‘kammaṃ vij‍jā ca dhammau ca, sīlaṃ jīvitamuttamaṃ|

aitaina mac‍cā sujjhanti, na gauttaina dhanaina vā||

‘‘tasmā hi paṇḍitau pausau, sampassaṃ atthamattanau|

yaunisau vicinai dhammaṃ, aivaṃ tattha visujjhati||

‘‘sāriputtauva  pañ‍ñāya, sīlaina upasamaina ca|

yaupi pāraṅgatau bhikkhu, aitāvaparamau siyā’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘atītaṃ nānvāgamaiyya, nappaṭikaṅkhai anāgataṃ|

yadatītaṃ pahīnaṃ pahīṇaṃ (sī॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.272 taṃ, appattañ‍ca anāgataṃ||

‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nañ‍ca  yau dhammaṃ, tattha tattha vipassati|

asaṃhīraṃ asaṃkuppaṃ, taṃ vidvā manubrūhayai||

‘‘aj‍jaiva kic‍camātappaṃ kic‍caṃ ātappaṃ (sī॰), kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

na hi nau saṅgaraṃ taina, mahāsainaina mac‍cunā||

‘‘aivaṃ vihāriṃ ātāpiṃ, ahaurattamatanditaṃ|

taṃ vai ‘‘bhaddaikarattau’’ti, santau ācikkhatai munī’’ti||

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, sacchikātabbāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi, bhikkhavai, dhammā cakkhunā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā, atthi dhammā satiyā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā, atthi dhammā kāyaina pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā, atthi dhammā pañ‍ñāya vaiditabbā, pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā cakkhunā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā? dibbacakkhu suvisuddhaṃ atik‍kantamānusakaṃ cakkhunā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbaṃ|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā satiyā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā? pubbainivāsānussati satiyā pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā kāyaina pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā? iddhividhā niraudhā kāyaina pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabbā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammā pañ‍ñāya vaiditabbā, pañ‍ñāya sacchikātabbā? āsavānaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya vaiditabbaṃ, pañ‍ñāya ca sacchikātabba’’nti|

idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

104. tattha  katamaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘yassa sailūpamaṃ cittaṃ, ṭhitaṃ nānupakampati|

virattaṃ rajanīyaisu, kaupanaiyyai na kuppati|

yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ, kutau naṃ taṃ (udā॰ 34) dukkhamaissatī’’ti||

idaṃ  asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

āyasmatau ca sāriputtassa cārikādasamaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ kātabbanti|

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yau  brāhmaṇau bāhitapāpadhammau, nihuṃhuṅkau nihuhuṅkau (sī॰) passa udā॰ 4 nik‍kasāvau yatattau|

vaidantagū vūsitabrahmacariyau, dhammaina sau brahmavādaṃ vadaiyya|

yassussadā natthi kuhiñ‍ci laukai’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘bāhitvā pāpakai dhammai, yai caranti sadā satā|

khīṇasaṃyaujanā buddhā, tai vai laukasmi laukasmiṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 5 brāhmaṇā’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yattha āpau ca pathavī, taijau vāyau na gādhati|

na tattha suk‍kā jautanti, ādic‍cau nappakāsati|

na tattha candimā bhāti, tamau tattha na vij‍jati||

‘‘yadā ca attanāvaidi vaidī (sī॰) passa udā॰ 10, muni maunaina brāhmaṇau|

atha rūpā arūpā ca, sukhadukkhā pamuc‍catī’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yadā sakaisu passa udā॰ 7 dhammaisu, pāragū hauti brāhmaṇau|

atha aitaṃ pisācañ‍ca, pak‍kulañ‍cātivattatī’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘nābhinandati  āyantiṃ āyantiṃ nābhinandati (udā॰ 8), pak‍kamantiṃ na saucati|

saṅgā saṅgāmajiṃ muttaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇa’’nti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘na  udakaina sucī suci (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 9 hauti, bahvaittha nhāyatī nahāyati (sī॰) janau|

yamhi sac‍cañ‍ca dhammau ca, sau sucī sau ca brāhmaṇau’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘yadā  havai pātubhavanti dhammā, ātāpinau jhāyatau brāhmaṇassa|

vidhūpayaṃ tiṭṭhati mārasainaṃ, sūriyauva aubhāsayamantalikkha’’nti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘santindriyaṃ passatha iriyamānaṃ, taivij‍japattaṃ apahānadhammaṃ|

sabbāni yaugāni upātivattau, akiñ‍canau iriyati paṃsukūlikau||

‘‘taṃ daivatā sambahulā uḷārā, brahmavimānaṃ upasaṅkamitvā|

ājāniyaṃ jātibalaṃ nisaidhaṃ, nidha namassanti pasan‍nacittā||

‘‘namau tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai nābhijānāma, kiṃ tvaṃ nissāya jhāyasī’’ti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘sahāyā vatimai bhikkhū, cirarattaṃ samaitikā|

samaiti naisaṃ saddhammau, dhammai buddhappavaiditai’’||

‘‘suvinītā  kappinaina, dhammai ariyappavaiditai|

dhārainti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti savāhana’’nti (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.246||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘nayidaṃ sithilamārabbha, nayidaṃ appaina thāmasā|

nibbānaṃ adhigantabbaṃ, sabbadukkhappamaucanaṃ sabbagantapamaucanaṃ (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.238||

‘‘ayañ‍ca  daharau bhikkhu, ayamuttamapurisau|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

‘‘dubbaṇṇakau lūkhacīvarau, maugharājā sadā satau|

khīṇāsavau visaṃyuttau, katakic‍cau anāsavau||

‘‘taivij‍jau  iddhippattau ca, caitaupariyakauvidau caitaupariyāyakauvidau (sī॰)|

dhāraiti antimaṃ daihaṃ, jaitvā māraṃ savāhini’’nti||

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

105. ‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau sammāsambuddhauti vuc‍cati| bhikkhupi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāvimuttau rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhārānaṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau sammāsambuddhauti vuc‍cati| bhikkhupi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāvimuttau viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tatra khau, bhikkhavai, kau visaisau kau adhippayāsau adhippāyau (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.58 kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pañ‍ñāvimuttaina bhikkhunāti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…

‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā, anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā, maggañ‍ñū maggavidū maggakauvidau, maggānugā ca, bhikkhavai, aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchāsaman‍nāgatā| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau, ayaṃ adhippayāsau, idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pañ‍ñāvimuttaina bhikkhunā’’ti|

idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

106. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘chan‍namativassati  passa udā॰ 45, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati|

tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassatī’’ti||

‘‘chan‍namativassatī’’ti  saṃkilaisau, ‘‘vivaṭaṃ nātivassatī’’ti vāsanā, ‘‘tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassatī’’ti ayaṃ saṃkilaisau ca vāsanā ca| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

‘‘cattāraumai, mahārāja bhikkhavai (a॰ ni॰ 4.85), puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai cattārau? tamau tamaparāyaṇau tamau jautiparāyaṇau jauti tamaparāyaṇau jauti jautiparāyaṇau’’ti| tattha yau ca puggalau jauti tamaparāyaṇau yau ca puggalau tamau tamaparāyaṇau, imai dvai puggalā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā, yau ca puggalau tamau jautiparāyaṇau yau ca puggalau jauti jautiparāyaṇau, imai dvai puggalā vāsanābhāgiyā| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, yadāyasaṃ dārujapabbajañ‍ca dārujaṃ pabbajañ‍ca (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.121)|

sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā’’ti|

ayaṃ saṃkilaisau||

‘‘aitaṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, auhārinaṃ sithilaṃ duppamuñ‍caṃ|

aitampi chaitvāna paribbajanti, anapaikkhinau kāmasukhaṃ pahāyā’’ti||

ayaṃ nibbaidhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

107. ‘‘yañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, caitaiti, yañ‍ca pakappaiti, yañ‍ca anusaiti| ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa hauti, tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai  āyatiṃ āyati (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.38 punabbhavābhinibbatti hauti, āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṃ āyati (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.38 jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘nau cai, bhikkhavai, caitaiti, nau cai pakappaiti, atha cai anusaiti| ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇai sati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.38 hauti, tasmiṃ patiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai virūḷhai āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti hauti, āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā sati āyatiṃ  jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, nau caiva ca (sī॰ ka॰) caitaiti, nau ca pakappaiti, nau ca anusaiti| ārammaṇamaitaṃ na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā, ārammaṇai asati patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti, tasmiṃ appatiṭṭhitai viñ‍ñāṇai avirūḷhai āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbatti na hauti, āyatiṃ punabbhavābhinibbattiyā asati āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti, ayaṃ nibbaidhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

108. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘‘samuddau samuddau’ti khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau bhāsati, naisau, bhikkhavai, ariyassa vinayai samuddau, mahā aisau bhikkhavai, udakarāsi mahāudakaṇṇavau| cakkhu, bhikkhavai, purisassa samuddau, tassa rūpamayau vaigau| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘yau  taṃ rūpamayaṃ vaigaṃ sahati ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atari atāri (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.228 cakkhusamuddaṃ saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagahaṃ sagāhaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.228) sarakkhasaṃ  tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau’’ti| ayaṃ asaikkhau|

‘‘‘sautaṃ, bhikkhavai…pai॰… ghānaṃ…pai॰… jivhā…pai॰… kāyau…pai॰… manau, bhikkhavai, purisassa samuddau tassa dhammamayau vaigauti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘yau taṃ dhammamayaṃ vaigaṃ sahati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, atari manausamuddaṃ saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sagahaṃ sarakkhasaṃ tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau’’ti| ayaṃ asaikkhau| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau, athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yau imaṃ samuddaṃ sagahaṃ sarakkhasaṃ,

saūmiṃ sāvaṭṭaṃ sabhayaṃ duttaraṃ ac‍catari|

sa vaidantagū vusitabrahmacariyau, laukantagū pāragatauti vuc‍catī’’ti||

ayaṃ  asaikkhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

‘‘chayimai, bhikkhavai, baḷisā laukasmiṃ anayāya sattānaṃ byābādhāya vadhāya (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.230) pāṇīnaṃ| katamai cha? santi, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilitabaḷisau gilabaḷisau (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.230 mārassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau, byasanaṃ āpan‍nau, yathākāmaṃ karaṇīyau pāpimatau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā…pai॰… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, tañ‍cai bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati  ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu gilitabaḷisau mārassa anayaṃ āpan‍nau, byasanaṃ āpan‍nau, yathākāmaṃ karaṇīyau yathākāmakaraṇīyau (sī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.230 pāpimatau’’ti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘santi  ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷisau mārassa, abhaidi baḷisaṃ, paribhaidi baḷisaṃ, na anayaṃ āpan‍nau, na byasanaṃ āpan‍nau, na yathākāmaṃ karaṇīyau pāpimatau|

‘‘santi ca, bhikkhavai, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, tañ‍cai bhikkhu nābhinandati nābhivadati, nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu na gilitabaḷisau mārassa, abhaidi baḷisaṃ, paribhaidi baḷisaṃ, na anayaṃ āpan‍nau, na byasanaṃ āpan‍nau, na yathākāmaṃ karaṇīyau pāpimatau’’ti| ayaṃ asaikkhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

109. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘ayaṃ laukau santāpajātau, phassaparaitau raugaṃ vadati attatau attanau (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 30|

yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñati mañ‍ñanti (sī॰ ka॰), tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā||

‘‘añ‍ñathābhāvī  bhavasattau laukau, bhavaparaitau bhavamaivābhinandati|

yadabhinandati taṃ bhayaṃ|

yassa bhāyati taṃ dukkha’’nti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau||

‘‘bhavavippahānāya khau panidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti| ayaṃ nibbaidhau|

‘‘yai  hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina bhavassa vippamaukkhamāhaṃsu, sabbai tai ‘avippamuttā bhavasmā’ti vadāmi| yai vā pana kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vibhavaina bhavassa nissaraṇamāhaṃsu, sabbai tai ‘anissaṭā bhavasmā’ti vadāmi| upadhiṃ upadhi (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 30 hi paṭic‍ca dukkhamidaṃ sambhautī’’ti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘sabbupādānakkhayā  natthi dukkhassa sambhavau’’ti| ayaṃ nibbaidhau|

‘‘laukamimaṃ passa, puthū avij‍jāya paraitā bhūtā bhūtaratā, bhavā aparimuttā, yai hi kaici bhavā sabbadhi sabbatthatāya, sabbai tai bhavā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā’’ti| ayaṃ saṃkilaisau|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, sammappañ‍ñāya passatau|

bhavataṇhā pahīyati, vibhavaṃ nābhinandati|

sabbasau taṇhānaṃ khayā, asaisavirāganiraudhau nibbāna’’nti|

ayaṃ nibbaidhau||

‘‘tassa nibbutassa bhikkhunau, anupādā punabbhavau na hauti|

abhibhūtau mārau vijitasaṅgāmau, upac‍cagā sabbabhavāni tādī’’ti||

ayaṃ asaikkhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai passa a॰ ni॰ 4.5, puggalā| katamai cattārau? anusautagāmī paṭisautagāmī ṭhitattau tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau’’ti| tattha yauyaṃ puggalau anusautagāmī, ayaṃ puggalau saṃkilaisabhāgiyau| tattha yauyaṃ puggalau paṭisautagāmī yau ca ṭhitattau, imai dvai puggalā  nibbaidhabhāgiyā| tattha  yauyaṃ puggalau tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau, ayaṃ asaikkhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

110. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

chaḷābhijātikau atthi puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, atthi puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, atthi puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ ac‍cantadiṭṭhaṃ antaṃ niṭṭhaṃ (sī॰) nibbānaṃ ārādhaiti, atthi  puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, atthi puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, atthi puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ ac‍cantadiṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhaiti|

tattha yau ca puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, yau ca puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau kaṇhaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, imai dvai puggalā saṃkilaisabhāgiyā|

tattha yau ca puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, yau ca puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau suk‍kaṃ dhammaṃ abhijāyati, imai dvai puggalā vāsanābhāgiyā|

tattha yau ca puggalau kaṇhau kaṇhābhijātikau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ ac‍cantadiṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhaiti, yau ca puggalau suk‍kau suk‍kābhijātikau akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ ac‍cantadiṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ ārādhaiti, imai dvai puggalā nibbaidhabhāgiyā, idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai passa a॰ ni॰ 4.232-233, kammāni| katamāni cattāri? atthi  kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ, atthi kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ, atthi kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ, atthi kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammuttamaṃ kammasaiṭṭhaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati’’|

tattha  yañ‍ca kammaṃ kaṇhaṃ kaṇhavipākaṃ, yañ‍ca kammaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ kaṇhasuk‍kavipākaṃ, ayaṃ saṃkilaisau| yañ‍ca kammaṃ suk‍kaṃ suk‍kavipākaṃ, ayaṃ vāsanā| yañ‍ca kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ akaṇhaasuk‍kavipākaṃ kammuttamaṃ kammasaiṭṭhaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati, ayaṃ nibbaidhau| idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

111. tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘laddhāna mānusattaṃ dvai, kic‍caṃ akic‍camaiva ca|

sukic‍caṃ caiva puñ‍ñāni, saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vā’’ti||

‘‘sukic‍caṃ  caiva puñ‍ñānī’’ti vāsanā| ‘‘saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vā’’ti nibbaidhau|

‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṃ vajanti katapuñ‍ñā|

saṃyaujanappahānā, jarāmaraṇā vippamuc‍cantī’’ti||

‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṃ vajanti katapuñ‍ñā’’ti vāsanā| ‘‘saṃyaujanappahānā jarāmaraṇā vippamuc‍cantī’’ti nibbaidhau| idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

‘‘dvaimāni, bhikkhavai, padhānāni passa a॰ ni॰ 2.2| katamāni dvai? yau ca agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitaisu cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paric‍cajati, yau ca agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitaisu sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’’nti| tattha  yau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitaisu cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paric‍cajati, ayaṃ vāsanā|

yau agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitaisu sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ, ayaṃ nibbaidhau| idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ|

tattha taṇhāsaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ taṇhāpakkhainaiva niddisitabbaṃ tīhi taṇhāhi – kāmataṇhāya bhavataṇhāya vibhavataṇhāya| yaina yaina vā pana vatthunā ajjhausitā, taina tainaiva niddisitabbaṃ, tassā vitthārau chattiṃsataṇhājāliniyāvicaritāni|

tattha  diṭṭhisaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ diṭṭhipakkhainaiva niddisitabbaṃ ucchaidasassataina, yaina yaina vā pana vatthunā diṭṭhivasaina abhinivisati ‘‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti, taina tainaiva niddisitabbaṃ, tassā vitthārau dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatāni|

tattha duc‍caritasaṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ caitanāya caitasikakammaina niddisitabbaṃ tīhi duc‍caritaihi – kāyaduc‍caritaina vacīduc‍caritaina manauduc‍caritaina, tassa vitthārau dasaakusalakammapathā|

tattha  taṇhāvaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ samathaina niddisitabbaṃ, diṭṭhivaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ vipassanā niddisitabbaṃ, duc‍caritavaudānabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ sucaritaina niddisitabbaṃ| tīṇi akusalamūlāni| taṃ kissa haitu? saṃsārassa nibbattiyā| tathā nibbattai saṃsārai kāyaduc‍caritaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ manausucaritaṃ iminā asubhaina kammavipākaina idaṃ bālalakkhaṇaṃ nibbattatīti| idaṃ  saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

iminā subhaina kammavipākaina idaṃ mahāpurisalakkhaṇaṃ nibbattatīti| idaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|
