tattha saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ catūhi kilaisabhūmīhi niddisitabbaṃ – anusayabhūmiyā pariyuṭṭhānabhūmiyā saṃyaujanabhūmiyā upādānabhūmiyā| sānusayassa pariyuṭṭhānaṃ jāyati, pariyuṭṭhitau saṃyuj‍jati, saṃyuj‍jantau upādiyati, upādānapac‍cayā bhavau, bhavapac‍cayā jāti, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti, aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| imāhi catūhi kilaisabhūmīhi sabbai kilaisā saṅgahaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchanti, idaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ tīhi sucaritaihi niddisitabbaṃ, nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ catūhi sac‍caihi niddisitabbaṃ, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ tīhi dhammaihi niddisitabbaṃ – buddhadhammaihi pac‍caikabuddhadhammaihi sāvakabhūmiyā| jhāyivisayai niddisitabbanti|

112. tattha katamai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā? laukiyaṃ laukuttaraṃ laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca, sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca, ñāṇaṃ ñaiyyaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca, dassanaṃ  bhāvanā dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca, sakavacanaṃ paravacanaṃ sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca, visaj‍janīyaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca, kammaṃ vipākau kammañ‍ca vipākau ca, kusalaṃ akusalaṃ kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca, anuñ‍ñātaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca, thavau cāti|

tattha  katamaṃ laukiyaṃ?

‘‘na hi pāpaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, saj‍jukhīraṃva muc‍cati|

ḍahantaṃ dahantaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 71 bālamanvaiti, bhasmacchan‍nauva bhasmāchan‍nauva (ka॰) pāvakauti||

idaṃ  laukiyaṃ|

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni sabbaṃ…pai॰… nihīyatai tassa yasau kāḷapakkhaiva candimā’’ti| idaṃ laukiyaṃ|

‘‘aṭṭhimai, bhikkhavai, laukadhammā passa a॰ ni॰ 8.6| katamai aṭṭha? lābhau alābhau, yasau ayasau, nindā pasaṃsā, sukhaṃ dukkhaṃ| imai khau, bhikkhavai, aṭṭha laukadhammā’’ti| idaṃ laukiyaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ laukuttaraṃ?

‘‘yassindriyāni samathaṅgatāni samathaṃ gatāni (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 94| assā yathā sārathinā sudantā|

pahīnamānassa anāsavassa, daivāpi tassa pihayanti tādinau’’ti||

idaṃ laukuttaraṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni laukuttarāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ satindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cindriyāni laukuttarānī’’ti| idaṃ laukuttaraṃ|

tattha katamaṃ laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca?

‘‘laddhāna mānusattaṃ dvai, kic‍caṃ akic‍camaiva cā’’ti dvai gāthā| yaṃ iha ‘‘sukic‍caṃ caiva  puñ‍ñānī’’ti ca ‘‘puñ‍ñāni karitvāna, saggā saggaṃ vajanti katapuñ‍ñā’’ti ca| idaṃ laukiyaṃ|

yaṃ iha ‘‘saṃyaujanavippahānaṃ vā’’ti ca ‘‘saṃyaujanappahānā, jarāmaraṇā vippamuc‍cantī’’ti ca, idaṃ laukuttaraṃ| idaṃ laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai  cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai sati nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti, nāmarūpassa avak‍kantiyā sati punabbhavau hauti, punabbhavai sati jāti hauti, jātiyā sati jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.55, mahārukkhau, tassa yāni caiva mūlāni adhaugamāni yāni ca tiriyaṃ gamāni, sabbāni tāni uddhaṃ aujaṃ abhiharanti| aivaṃ hi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau tadāhārau tadupādānau ciraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai āhārai sati nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti sabbaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hautī’’ti| idaṃ laukiyaṃ|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai cai, bhikkhavai, āhārai asati nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti na hauti, nāmarūpassa avak‍kantiyā asati punabbhavau na hauti, punabbhavai asati jāti na hauti, jātiyā asati jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā nirujjhanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau atha purisau āgacchaiyya kuddālapiṭakaṃ kudālapiṭakaṃ (ka॰) ādāya, sau taṃ rukkhaṃ mūlai chindaiyya, mūlai mūlaṃ (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.55) chaitvā palikhaṇaiyya, palikhaṇitvā mūlāni uddharaiyya antamasau usīranāḷimattānipi| sau taṃ rukkhaṃ khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chindaiyya, khaṇḍākhaṇḍikaṃ chinditvā chitvā (sī॰ ka॰) phālaiyya, phālaitvā sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karaiyya, sakalikaṃ sakalikaṃ karitvā vātātapai visausaiyya, vātātapai visausaitvā agginā ḍahaiyya, agginā ḍahaitvā masiṃ karaiyya, masiṃ karitvā mahāvātai vā auphunaiyya, nadiyā vā sīghasautāya pavāhaiyya, aivaṃ hi sau, bhikkhavai, mahārukkhau ucchin‍namūlau assa tālāvatthukatau anabhāvaṃkatau anabhāvaṃgatau (sī॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai āhārai asati nāmarūpassa  avak‍kanti na hauti, nāmarūpassa avak‍kantiyā asati sabbaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti| idaṃ laukuttaraṃ| idaṃ laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca|

113. tattha  katamaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

‘‘sabbā disā anuparigamma caitasā, naivajjhagā piyataramattanā k‍vaci|

aivaṃ piyau puthu attā paraisaṃ, tasmā na hiṃsai paramattakāmau’’ti paraṃ attakāmauti (sī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.119; udā॰ 41 passitabbaṃ||

idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

‘‘yai kaici bhūtā bhavissanti yai vāpi ca (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 42, sabbai gamissanti pahāya daihaṃ|

taṃ sabbajāniṃ kusalau viditvā, ātāpiyau ātāpī sau (sī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 42 brahmacariyaṃ caraiyyā’’ti||

idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

‘‘sattahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ api vivaiciyamānaina paṇāmiyamānaina galai pisanamaj‍jamānaina galaipi pamaj‍jamānaina (sī॰) yāvajīvaṃ na vijahitabbaṃ| katamaihi sattahi? piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca vattā ca vacanakkhamau ca gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā hauti, nau ca aṭṭhānai na ca aṭṭhānai (sī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 7.37 niyaujaiti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, sattahi…pai॰… na vijahitabbaṃ| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau| athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘piyau garu bhāvanīyau, vattā ca vacanakkhamau|

gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, na caṭṭhānai niyaujakau|

taṃ mittaṃ mittakāmaina, yāvajīvampi saiviya’’nti||

idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

‘‘yañ‍ca kāmasukhaṃ laukai, yañ‍cidaṃ diviyaṃ sukhaṃ|

taṇhakkhayasukhassaitai taṇhakkhayā sukhassaitai (sī॰) passa udā॰ 12, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasi’’nti||

idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

‘‘susukhaṃ  passa thairagā॰ 227 vata nibbānaṃ, sammāsambuddhadaisitaṃ|

asaukaṃ virajaṃ khaimaṃ, yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatī’’ti||

idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca

‘‘mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hantvā, rājānau dvai ca khattiyai|

raṭṭhaṃ sānucaraṃ hantvā’’ti idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ||

‘‘anīghau yāti brāhmaṇau’’ti| idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca||

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, iddhipādā passa iddhipādasaṃyuttai| katamai cattārau? chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau, vīriya…pai॰… citta| vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau iddhipādau’’ti| idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

sau kāyaipi cittaṃ samaudahati, cittaipi kāyaṃ samaudahati, kāyai sukhasañ‍ñañ‍ca lahusañ‍ñañ‍ca auk‍kamitvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ, idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca|

114. tattha katamaṃ ñāṇaṃ?

‘‘yaṃ taṃ laukuttaraṃ ñāṇaṃ, sabbañ‍ñū yaina vuc‍cati|

na tassa parihānatthi, sabbakālai pavattatī’’ti||

idaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍ñā  hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, yāyaṃ nibbānagāminī nibbaidhagāminī (itivu॰ 41)|

yāya sammā pajānāti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhaya’’nti||

idaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ ñaiyyaṃ?

‘‘kittayissāmi  tai vau (sī॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1072 santiṃ, [dhautakāti bhagavā,]

diṭṭhai dhammai anītihaṃ|

yaṃ viditvā satau caraṃ, tarai laukai visattikaṃ||

‘‘tañ‍cāhaṃ  abhinandāmi, mahaisi santimuttamaṃ|

yaṃ viditvā satau caraṃ, tarai laukai visattikaṃ||

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci sampajānāsi, [dhautakāti bhagavā]

uddhaṃ adhau tiriyañ‍cāpi majjhai|

aitaṃ viditvā saṅgauti laukai,

bhavābhavāya mākāsi taṇha’’nti||

idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

‘‘catun‍naṃ, bhikkhavai, ariyasac‍cānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍ca…pai॰… tayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhasamudayaṃ dukkhasamudayau (sī॰ ka॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 2.155 ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhaṃ dukkhaniraudhau (sī॰ ka॰) ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasac‍caṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ| ucchin‍nā bhavataṇhā, khīṇā bhavanaitti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā, idaṃ vatvāna sugatau, athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ, yathābhūtaṃ adassanā|

saṃsitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ (sī॰) dīghamaddhānaṃ, tāsu tāsvaiva jātisu||

‘‘tāni aitāni diṭṭhāni, bhavanaitti samūhatā|

ucchin‍naṃ mūlaṃ dukkhassa, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti||

idaṃ  ñaiyyaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca? rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti| idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

aivaṃ  jānaṃ aivaṃ passaṃ ariyasāvakau ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’nti passati, ‘‘vaidanā anic‍cā’’ti passati, ‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhārai…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍ca’’nti passatīti| idaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

sau parimuc‍cati rūpaina, parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmīti| idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca|

‘‘sabbai  saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ| ‘‘yadā pañ‍ñāya passatī’’ti idaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘atha nibbindati dukkhai aisa maggau visuddhiyā’’ti idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca|

‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ| ‘‘yadā pañ‍ñāya passatī’’ti idaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘atha nibbindati dukkhai aisa maggau visuddhiyā’’ti idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca|

‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ| ‘‘yadā pañ‍ñāya passatī’’ti idaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘atha nibbindati dukkhai aisa maggau visuddhiyā’’ti idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca|

‘‘yai  hi kaici, sauṇa passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.49, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anic‍caina rūpaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti| kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā| anic‍cāya vaidanāya…pai॰… anic‍cāya sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… anic‍caihi saṅkhāraihi…pai॰… anic‍caina viñ‍ñāṇaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti, ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti, kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā’’ti| idaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anic‍caina rūpaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti, ‘sadisauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti, ‘hīnauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti, kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa dassanā| anic‍cāya vaidanāya…pai॰… anic‍cāya sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… anic‍caihi saṅkhāraihi…pai॰… anic‍caina viñ‍ñāṇaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti, ‘sadisauhamasmī’tipi  na samanupassanti, ‘hīnauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti, kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa dassanāti| idaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca|

tattha katamaṃ dassanaṃ?

115.

 ‘‘yai ariyasac‍cāni vibhāvayanti, gambhīrapañ‍ñaina sudaisitāni|

kiñ‍cāpi tai haunti bhusaṃ pamattā bhusappamattā (sī॰) passa khu॰ pā॰ 609, na tai bhavaṃ aṭṭhamamādiyantī’’ti||

idaṃ dassanaṃ|

‘‘yathindakhīlau  pathavissitau siyā, catubbhi vātaihi asampakampiyau|

tathūpamaṃ  sappurisaṃ vadāmi, yau ariyasac‍cāni avaic‍ca passatī’’ti||

idaṃ dassanaṃ|

‘‘catūhi, bhikkhavai, sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya ‘khīṇanirayaumhi, khīṇatiracchānayauni, khīṇapaittivisayau, khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau, sattakkhattuparamaṃ sattakkhattuparamau (sī॰) daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’ti| katamaihi catūhi? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa tathāgatai saddhā niviṭṭhā patiṭṭhitā virūḷhā mūlajātā asaṃhāriyā samaṇaina vā brāhmaṇaina vā daivaina vā māraina vā brahmunā vā kainaci vā laukasmiṃ saha dhammaina, dhammai khau pana niṭṭhaṃ gatau hauti, svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhi, yadidaṃ madanimmadanau…pai॰… niraudhau nibbānaṃ, saha dhammiyā khau panassa haunti iṭṭhā kantā piyā manāpā gihī caiva pabbajitā ca| ariyakantaihi khau pana sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasaṭṭhaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya ‘khīṇanirayaumhi, khīṇatiracchānayauni, khīṇapaittivisayau, khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau  niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau, sattakkhattuparamaṃ daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissāmī’’’ti|

idaṃ  dassanaṃ|

tattha  katamā bhāvanā?

‘‘yassindriyāni bhāvitāni subhāvitāni (sī॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 512, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

nibbijjha imaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ, kālaṃ kaṅkhati bhāvitau sadantau’’ti||

ayaṃ bhāvanā|

‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, dhammapadāni passa a॰ ni॰ 4.29| katamāni cattāri? anabhijjhā dhammapadaṃ, abyāpādau dhammapadaṃ, sammāsati dhammapadaṃ, sammāsamādhi dhammapadaṃ, imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri dhammapadānī’’ti| ayaṃ bhāvanā|

tattha katamaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca? ‘‘pañ‍ca chindai pañ‍ca jahai’’ti idaṃ dassanaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍ca cuttari bhāvayai| pañ‍ca saṅgātigau bhikkhu, aughatiṇṇauti vuc‍catī’’ti ayaṃ bhāvanā| idaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, indriyāni passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.493| katamāni tīṇi, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anabhisamaitassa dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa abhisamayāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, anabhisamaitassa dukkhasamudayassa  ariyasac‍cassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhassa…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ariyasac‍cassa abhisamayāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriya’’nti| idaṃ dassanaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñindriyaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti| idaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñātāvindriya’’nti| ayaṃ bhāvanā|

idaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca|

116. tattha katamaṃ sakavacanaṃ?

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudāpanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsana’’nti||

idaṃ sakavacanaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālanimittāni bālāpadānāni, yaihi bālaṃ bālauti parai sañ‍jānanti| katamāni tīṇi? bālau, bhikkhavai, duc‍cintitacintī ca hauti, dubbhāsitabhāsī ca hauti, duk‍kaṭakammakārī duk‍katakammakārī (sī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.246; a॰ ni॰ 3.3 passitabbaṃ ca hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni bālanimittāni bālāpadānāni|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa paṇḍitalakkhaṇāni paṇḍitanimittāni paṇḍitāpadānāni, yaihi paṇḍitaṃ paṇḍitauti parai sañ‍jānanti| katamāni tīṇi? paṇḍitau, bhikkhavai, sucintitacintī ca hauti, subhāsitabhāsī ca hauti, sukatakammakārī ca hauti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi paṇḍitassa paṇḍitalakkhaṇāni paṇḍitanimittāni paṇḍitāpadānānī’’ti|

idaṃ sakavacanaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ paravacanaṃ?

‘‘pathavīsamau natthi vitthatau, nin‍nau pātālasamau na vij‍jati|

mairusamau natthi un‍natau, cak‍kavattisadisau natthi paurisau’’ti||

idaṃ paravacanaṃ|

‘‘‘hautu, daivānaminda, subhāsitaina jayauti| hautu, vaipacitti subhāsitaina jayauti| bhaṇa, vaipacitti, gātha’nti| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘bhiyyau  bālā pabhij‍jaiyyuṃ pakujjhaiyyuṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.251, nau cassa paṭisaidhakau|

tasmā bhusaina daṇḍaina, dhīrau bālaṃ nisaidhayai’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, vaipacittinā asurindaina gāthāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca ‘bhaṇa, daivānaminda, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘aitadaiva ahaṃ mañ‍ñai, bālassa paṭisaidhanaṃ|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammatī’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthāya daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau vaipacittiṃ asurindaṃ aitadavauca ‘bhaṇa, vaipacitti, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, vaipacitti asurindau imaṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi –

‘‘aitadaiva  titikkhāya, vaj‍jaṃ passāmi vāsava|

yadā naṃ mañ‍ñati mañ‍ñatī (sī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.251 bālau, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

ajjhāruhati dummaidhau, gauva bhiyyau palāyina’’nti||

‘‘bhāsitāya khau pana, bhikkhavai, vaipacittinā asurindaina gāthāya asurā anumaudiṃsu, daivā tuṇhī ahaisuṃ| atha khau vaipacitti asurindau sak‍kaṃ daivānamindaṃ aitadavauca ‘bhaṇa, daivānaminda, gātha’nti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau imā gāthāyau abhāsi –

‘‘kāmaṃ mañ‍ñatu vā mā vā, bhayā myāyaṃ titikkhati|

sadatthaparamā atthā, khantā bhiyyau na vij‍jati||

‘‘yau  havai balavā santau, dubbalassa titikkhati|

tamāhu paramaṃ khantiṃ, nic‍caṃ khamati dubbalau||

‘‘abalaṃ taṃ balaṃ āhu, yassa bālabalaṃ balaṃ|

balassa dhammaguttassa, paṭivattā na vij‍jati||

‘‘tassaiva taina pāpiyau, yau kuddhaṃ paṭikujjhati|

kuddhaṃ appaṭikujjhantau, saṅgāmaṃ jaiti duj‍jayaṃ||

‘‘ubhin‍namatthaṃ  carati, attanau ca parassa ca|

paraṃ saṅkupitaṃ ñatvā, yau satau upasammati||

‘‘ubhin‍naṃ tikicchantānaṃ, attanau ca parassa ca|

janā mañ‍ñanti bālauti, yai dhammassa akauvidā’’ti||

‘‘bhāsitāsu khau pana, bhikkhavai, sak‍kaina daivānamindaina gāthāsu daivā anumaudiṃsu, asurā tuṇhī ahaisu’’nti| idaṃ paravacanaṃ|

117. tattha katamaṃ sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca?

yañ‍ca pattaṃ yañ‍ca pattabbaṃ ubhayamaitaṃ rajānukiṇṇaṃ āturassānusikkhatau| yai ca sikkhāsārā sīlaṃ vataṃ jīvitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ upaṭṭhānasārā, ayamaikau antau| yai ca aivaṃvādinau aivaṃdiṭṭhinau ‘‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’’ti, ayaṃ dutiyau  antau| ic‍caitai ubhau antā kaṭasivaḍḍhanā kaṭasiyau diṭṭhiṃ vaḍḍhainti| aitai ubhau antai anabhiñ‍ñāya aulīyanti aikai atidhāvanti aikaiti| idaṃ paravacanaṃ|

yai ca khau tai ubhau antai abhiñ‍ñāya tatra ca na ahaisuṃ, taina ca amañ‍ñiṃsu, vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāyāti| idaṃ sakavacanaṃ| ayaṃ udānau sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca|

rājā pasainadi passainadi (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.113 kausalau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – idha mayhaṃ, bhantai, rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa  aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi ‘‘kaisaṃ nu khau piyau attā, kaisaṃ appiyau attā’’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi ‘‘yai ca khau kaici kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti, taisaṃ appiyau attā| kiñ‍cāpi tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘piyau nau attā’ti, atha khau taisaṃ appiyau attā| taṃ kissa haitu? yaṃ hi appiyau appiyassa karaiyya, taṃ tai attanāva attanau karaunti, tasmā taisaṃ appiyau attā| yai ca khau kaici kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti, vācāya sucaritaṃ caranti, manasā sucaritaṃ caranti, taisaṃ piyau attā| kiñ‍cāpi tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘appiyau nau attā’ti, atha khau taisaṃ piyau attā| taṃ kissa haitu? yaṃ hi piyau piyassa karaiyya| taṃ tai attanāva attanau karaunti| tasmā taisaṃ piyau attā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, mahārāja, aivamaitaṃ, mahārāja, yai hi kaici, mahārāja, kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti, manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti tasmā taisaṃ appiyau attā| kiñ‍cāpi tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘piyau nau attā’ti, atha khau taisaṃ appiyau attā| taṃ kissa haitu? yaṃ hi, mahārāja, appiyau appiyassa karaiyya, taṃ tai attanāva attanau karaunti, tasmā taisaṃ appiyau attā| yai ca khau kaici mahārāja kāyaina sucaritaṃ caranti, vācāya sucaritaṃ caranti, manasā sucaritaṃ caranti, taisaṃ piyau attā| kiñ‍cāpi tai aivaṃ vadaiyyuṃ ‘appiyau nau attā’ti, atha khau taisaṃ piyau attā| taṃ  kissa haitu? yaṃ hi, mahārāja, piyau piyassa karaiyya, taṃ tai attanāva attanau karaunti, tasmā taisaṃ piyau attāti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… satthā –

‘‘attānañ‍cai piyaṃ jañ‍ñā, na naṃ pāpaina saṃyujai|

na hi taṃ sulabhaṃ hauti, sukhaṃ duk‍kaṭakārinā||

‘‘antakainādhipan‍nassa maraṇainābhibhūtassa (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.115, jahatau mānusaṃ bhavaṃ|

kiṃ hi tassa sakaṃ hauti, kiñ‍ca ādāya gacchati|

kiñ‍cassa anugaṃ hauti, chāyāva anapāyinī||

‘‘ubhau puñ‍ñañ‍ca pāpañ‍ca, yaṃ mac‍cau kurutai idha|

tañhi tassa sakaṃ hauti, taṃva ādāya gacchati|

taṃvassa anugaṃ hauti, chāyāva anapāyinī||

‘‘tasmā  karaiyya kalyāṇaṃ, nicayaṃ samparāyikaṃ|

puñ‍ñāni paralaukasmiṃ, patiṭṭhā haunti pāṇina’’nti||

idaṃ suttaṃ paravacanaṃ| anugīti sakavacanaṃ| idaṃ sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca|

118. tattha katamaṃ visaj‍janīyaṃ?

pañhai pucchitai idaṃ abhiñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, idaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, idaṃ pahātabbaṃ, idaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ, idaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ, imai dhammā aivaṃgahitā idaṃ phalaṃ nibbattayanti| taisaṃ  aivaṃgahitānaṃ ayamatthau iti idaṃ visaj‍janīyaṃ| ‘‘uḷārau buddhau bhagavā’’ti buddhauḷārataṃ dhammasvākkhātataṃ saṅghasuppaṭipattiñ‍ca aikaṃsainaiva niddisai| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti aikaṃsainaiva niddisai| yaṃ vā panañ‍ñampi aivaṃ jātiyaṃ| idaṃ visaj‍janīyaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ?

‘‘ākaṅkhatau tai naradammasārathi naradammasārathī (sī॰), daivā manussā manasā vicintitaṃ|

sabbai na jañ‍ñā kasiṇāpi pāṇinau, santaṃ samādhiṃ araṇaṃ nisaivatau|

kintaṃ bhagavā ākaṅkhatī’’ti||

idaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ|

aittakau bhagavā sīlakkhandhai samādhikkhandhai pañ‍ñākkhandhai vimuttikkhandhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai iriyāyaṃ pabhāvai hitaisitāyaṃ karuṇāyaṃ iddhiyanti| idaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ|

‘‘tathāgatassa, bhikkhavai, arahatau sammāsambuddhassa laukai uppādā tiṇṇaṃ ratanānaṃ uppādau buddharatanassa dhammaratanassa saṅgharatanassa’’| kiṃ pamāṇāni tīṇi ratanānīti? idaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ|

buddhavisayau avisaj‍janīyau| puggalaparauparañ‍ñutā avisaj‍janīyā| ‘‘pubbā, bhikkhavai, kauṭi  na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sakiṃ nirayaṃ sakiṃ tiracchānayauniṃ sakiṃ paittivisayaṃ sakiṃ asurayauniṃ sakiṃ daivai sakiṃ manussai sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ’’| katamā pubbā kauṭīti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| na pañ‍ñāyatīti sāvakānaṃ ñāṇavaikal‍laina| duvidhā buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ daisanā attūpanāyikā ca parūpanāyikā ca| na pañ‍ñāyatīti parūpanāyikā| natthi buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ avijānanāti appajānanāti (sī॰) attūpanāyikā| yathā bhagavā kaukālikaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ aivamāha –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsatikhārikau kausalakau tilavāhau…pai॰… na tvaiva aikau abbudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi bhikkhu, vīsati abbudā nirayā, aivamaikau nirabbudau nirayau nirabbudanirayau (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.181)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati nirabbudā nirayā, aivamaikau ababau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ababā nirayā, aivamaikau aṭaṭau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati aṭaṭā nirayā, aivamaikau ahahau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati ahahā nirayā, aivamaikau kumudau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati kumudā nirayā, aivamaikau saugandhikau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati saugandhikā nirayā, aivamaikau uppalakau nirayau uppalanirayau (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.181)| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati uppalakā nirayā, aivamaikau puṇḍarīkau nirayau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhu, vīsati puṇḍarīkā nirayā, aivamaikau padumau nirayau| padumai pana, bhikkhu, nirayai kaukālikau bhikkhu upapan‍nau sāriputtamauggal‍lānaisu cittaṃ āghātaitvā’’ti| yaṃ vā pana kiñ‍ci bhagavā āha ‘‘ayaṃ appamaiyyau asaṅkhyaiyau’’ti| sabbaṃ taṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ|

119. tattha katamaṃ visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca, yadā sau upakau ājīvakau bhagavantaṃ āha ‘‘kuhiṃ, āvusau gautama, gamissasī’’ti| bhagavā āha –

‘‘bārāṇasiṃ gamissāmi, ahaṃ taṃ amatadundubhiṃ|

dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattaituṃ, laukai appaṭivattiya’’nti||

upakau ājīvakau āha ‘‘‘jinau’ti khau āvusau, bhau gautama, paṭijānāsī’’ti| bhagavā āha –

‘‘mādisā  vai jinā jinā vai mādisā (sī॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 2.341 haunti, yai pattā āsavakkhayaṃ|

jitā mai pāpakā dhammā, tasmāhaṃ upakā jinau’’ti||

kathaṃ jinau kaina jinauti visaj‍janīyaṃ| katamau jinauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| katamau āsavakkhayau, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayauti visaj‍janīyaṃ| kittakau āsavakkhayauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ  visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca|

atthi  tathāgatauti visaj‍janīyaṃ| atthi rūpanti visaj‍janīyaṃ| rūpaṃ tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| rūpavā tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| rūpai tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| tathāgatai rūpanti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| aivaṃ atthi vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… saṅkhārā…pai॰… atthi viñ‍ñāṇanti visaj‍janīyaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇavā tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇai tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| tathāgatai viñ‍ñāṇanti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| añ‍ñatra rūpaina tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| añ‍ñatra vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhāraihi…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaina tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| ayaṃ sau tathāgatau arūpakau…pai॰… avaidanakau…pai॰… asañ‍ñakau…pai॰… asaṅkhārakau…pai॰… aviñ‍ñāṇakauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca|

passati bhagavā dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai aivaṃ sabbaṃ…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānātīti visaj‍janīyaṃ| katamai sattā, katamau tathāgatauti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca|

atthi tathāgatauti visaj‍janīyaṃ| atthi tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti avisaj‍janīyaṃ| idaṃ visaj‍janīyañ‍ca avisaj‍janīyañ‍ca|

120. tattha katamaṃ kammaṃ?

‘‘antakainādhipan‍nassa, jahatau mānusaṃ bhavaṃ|

kiṃ hi tassa sakaṃ hauti, kiñ‍ca ādāya gacchati|

kiñ‍cassa anugaṃ hauti, chāyāva anapāyinī||

‘‘ubhau puñ‍ñañ‍ca pāpañ‍ca, yaṃ mac‍cau kurutai idha|

tañhi tassa sakaṃ hauti, taṃva tañ‍ca (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.115 ādāya gacchati|

taṃvassa anugaṃ hauti, chāyāva anapāyinī’’ti||

idaṃ kammaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ  vā chamāyaṃ chamāya (sī॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.248 vā saimānaṃ yānissa pubbai pāpakāni kammāni katāni kāyaina duc‍caritāni vācāya duc‍caritāni manasā duc‍caritāni, tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti  abhippalambanti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahataṃ pabbatakūṭānaṃ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṃ pathaviyaṃ aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ yānissa pubbai pāpakāni kammāni katāni kāyaina duc‍caritāni vācāya duc‍caritāni manasā duc‍caritāni, tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, bālassa aivaṃ hauti ‘akataṃ vata mai kalyāṇaṃ, akataṃ kusalaṃ, akataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ| kataṃ pāpaṃ, kataṃ luddaṃ, kataṃ kibbisaṃ, yāvatā bhau akatakalyāṇānaṃ akatakusalānaṃ akatabhīruttāṇānaṃ katapāpānaṃ kataluddānaṃ katakibbisānaṃ gati, taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti, sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ yānissa pubbai kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyaina sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni, tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, mahataṃ pabbatakūṭānaṃ chāyā sāyanhasamayaṃ pathaviyaṃ aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitaṃ pīṭhasamārūḷhaṃ vā mañ‍casamārūḷhaṃ vā chamāyaṃ vā saimānaṃ yānissa pubbai kalyāṇāni kammāni katāni kāyaina sucaritāni vācāya sucaritāni manasā sucaritāni, tānissa tamhi samayai aulambanti ajjhaulambanti abhippalambanti| tatra, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitassa ‘aivaṃ hauti akataṃ vata mai pāpaṃ, akataṃ luddaṃ, akataṃ kibbisaṃ| kataṃ kalyāṇaṃ, kataṃ kusalaṃ, kataṃ bhīruttāṇaṃ, yāvatā bhau akatapāpānaṃ  akataluddānaṃ akatakibbisānaṃ katakalyāṇānaṃ katakusalānaṃ katabhīruttāṇānaṃ gati, taṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchāmī’ti, sau na saucati na kilamati na paridaivati na urattāḷiṃ kandati na sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati, ‘kataṃ mai puñ‍ñaṃ, akataṃ pāpaṃ, yā bhavissati gati akatapāpassa akataluddassa akatakibbisassa katapuñ‍ñassa katakusalassa katabhīruttāṇassa, taṃ paic‍ca bhavai gatiṃ pac‍canubhavissāmī’ti vippaṭisārau na jāyati| avippaṭisārinau khau, bhikkhavai, itthiyā vā purisassa vā gihinau vā pabbajitassa vā bhaddakaṃ maraṇaṃ bhaddikā kālaṅkiriyāti vadāmī’’ti| idaṃ kammaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, duc‍caritāni| katamāni tīṇi, kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi duc‍caritāni| tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, sucaritāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyasucaritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manausucaritaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi sucaritāni| idaṃ kammaṃ|

tattha katamau vipākau?

‘‘lābhā vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau, bhikkhavai, paṭiladdhau brahmacariyavāsāya| diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai, cha phassāyatanikā nāma nirayā| tattha yaṃ kiñ‍ci cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati aniṭṭharūpaṃyaiva passati, nau iṭṭharūpaṃ| akantarūpaṃyaiva passati, nau kantarūpaṃ| amanāparūpaṃyaiva passati, nau manāparūpaṃ|

yaṃ kiñ‍ci sautaina saddaṃ suṇāti…pai॰… ghānaina…pai॰… jivhāya…pai॰… kāyaina…pai॰… yaṃ kiñ‍ci manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti aniṭṭhadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau iṭṭhadhammaṃ| akantadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau kantadhammaṃ| amanāpadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau manāpadhammaṃ| lābhā vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau, bhikkhavai, paṭiladdhau  brahmacariyavāsāya|

‘‘diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai, cha phassāyatanikā nāma saggā| tattha yaṃ kiñ‍ci cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati iṭṭharūpaṃyaiva passati, nau aniṭṭharūpaṃ| kantarūpaṃyaiva passati, nau akantarūpaṃ| manāparūpaṃyaiva passati, nau amanāparūpaṃ| yaṃ kiñ‍ci sautaina saddaṃ suṇāti…pai॰… ghānaina  …pai॰… jivhāya…pai॰… kāyaina…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ vijānāti iṭṭhadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau aniṭṭhadhammaṃ| kantadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau akantadhammaṃ| manāpadhammaṃyaiva vijānāti, nau amanāpadhammaṃ| lābhā vau, bhikkhavai, suladdhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, khaṇau vau, bhikkhavai, paṭiladdhau brahmacariyavāsāyā’’ti| ayaṃ vipākau|

‘‘saṭṭhivassasahassāni, paripuṇṇāni sabbasau|

nirayai pac‍camānānaṃ pac‍camānassa (ka॰) passa pai॰ va॰ 802, kadā antau bhavissati||

‘‘natthi antau kutau antau, na antau paṭidissati patidissati (sī॰) jā॰ 1.4.55|

tadā hi pakataṃ pāpaṃ, tuyhaṃ mayhañ‍ca mārisā’’ti||

ayaṃ vipākau|

121. tattha  katamaṃ kammañ‍ca vipākau ca?

‘‘adhammacārī hi narau pamattau, yahiṃ yahiṃ gacchati duggatiṃ yau|

sau naṃ adhammau caritau hanāti, sayaṃ gahītau yathā kaṇhasappau||

‘‘na hi passa thairagā॰ 304 dhammau adhammau ca, ubhau samavipākinau|

adhammau nirayaṃ naiti, dhammau pāpaiti suggati’’nti||

idaṃ kammañ‍ca vipākau ca|

‘‘mā, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñānaṃ bhāyittha, sukhassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, adhivacanaṃ iṭṭhassa kantassa piyassa manāpassa yadidaṃ  puñ‍ñāni| abhijānāmi khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, dīgharattaṃ katānaṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ iṭṭhaṃ dīgharattaṃ iṭṭhaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) passa itivu॰ 22 kantaṃ piyaṃ manāpaṃ vipākaṃ pac‍canubhūtaṃ, satta vassāni maittacittaṃ bhāvaitvā satta saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai na imaṃ na yimaṃ (itivu॰ 22) laukaṃ punarāgamāsiṃ| saṃvaṭṭamānai sudāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, kappai ābhassarūpagau haumi| vivaṭṭamānai kappai suñ‍ñaṃ brahmavimānaṃ upapaj‍jāmi| tatra sudāhaṃ sudaṃ (itivu॰ 22), bhikkhavai, brahmā haumi mahābrahmā abhibhū anabhibhūtau añ‍ñadatthudasau vasavattī| chattiṃsakkhattuṃ khau panāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau ahausiṃ daivānamindau, anaikasatakkhattuṃ rājā ahausiṃ cak‍kavattī cak‍kavatti (ka॰) dhammikau dhammarājā cāturantau vijitāvī janapadatthāvariyappattau sattaratanasaman‍nāgatau, kau pana vādau padaisaraj‍jassa? tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi ‘kissa nu khau mai idaṃ kammassa  phalaṃ, kissa kammassa vipākau, yaināhaṃ aitarahi aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi ‘tiṇṇaṃ khau mai idaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ, tiṇṇaṃ kammānaṃ vipākau| yaināhaṃ aitarahi aivaṃmahiddhikau aivaṃmahānubhāvau’ti| saiyyathidaṃ, dānassa damassa saṃyamassā’’ti| tattha yañ‍ca dānaṃ yau ca damau yau ca saṃyamau, idaṃ kammaṃ| yau tappac‍cayā vipākau pac‍canubhūtau, ayaṃ vipākau| tathā cūḷakammavibhaṅgau vattabbau|

yaṃ  subhassa māṇavassa taudaiyyaputtassa daisitaṃ| tattha yai dhammā appāyukadīghāyukatāya saṃvattanti bahvābādhaappābādhatāya appaisakkhamahaisakkhatāya dubbaṇṇasuvaṇṇatāya nīcakulikauc‍cakulikatāya appabhaugamahābhaugatāya duppañ‍ñapañ‍ñavantatāya ca  saṃvattanti, idaṃ kammaṃ| yā tattha appāyukadīghāyukatā…pai॰… duppañ‍ñapañ‍ñavantatā, ayaṃ vipākau| idaṃ kammañ‍ca vipākau ca|

122. tattha katamaṃ kusalaṃ?

‘‘vācānurakkhī manasā susaṃvutau, kāyaina ca nākusalaṃ kayirā akusalaṃ na kayirā (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 281|

aitai tayau kammapathai visaudhayai, ārādhayai maggamisippavaidita’’nti||

idaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘yassa kāyaina vācāya, manasā natthi duk‍kaṭaṃ|

saṃvutaṃ tīhi ṭhānaihi, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇa’’nti||

idaṃ kusalaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, kusalamūlāni| katamāni tīṇi? alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, adausau kusalamūlaṃ, amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi kusalamūlāni| idaṃ kusalaṃ| ‘‘vij‍jā, bhikkhavai vij‍jā ca khau bhikkhavai (saṃyuttanikāyai), pubbaṅgamā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ samāpattiyā anudaiva anvadaiva (sī॰ ka॰), syādikaṇḍai (mauggal‍lānai) 11 suttaṃ passitabbaṃ hirī auttappañ‍cā’’ti| idaṃ kusalaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ akusalaṃ?

‘‘yassa  ac‍canta dussīlyaṃ, māluvā sālamivautthataṃ|

karauti sau tathattānaṃ, yathā naṃ icchatī disau’’ti||

idaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘attanā hi kataṃ pāpaṃ, attajaṃ attasambhavaṃ|

abhimatthati abhimanthati (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 161 dummaidhaṃ, vajiraṃvasmamayaṃ maṇi’’nti||

idaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘dasa  kammapathai nisaiviya, akusalākusalaihi vivaj‍jitā|

garahā ca bhavanti daivatai, bālamatī nirayaisu pac‍carai’’ti||

idaṃ akusalaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, akusalamūlāni passa a॰ ni॰ 3.70, katamāni tīṇi? laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi akusalamūlāni’’| idaṃ akusalaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca?

‘‘yādisaṃ saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.256 vapatai bījaṃ, tādisaṃ haratai phalaṃ|

kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇaṃ, pāpakārī ca pāpaka’’nti||

tattha yaṃ āha ‘‘kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇa’’nti, idaṃ kusalaṃ| yaṃ āha ‘‘pāpakārī ca pāpaka’’nti, idaṃ akusalaṃ| idaṃ kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca|

‘‘subhaina kammaina vajanti suggatiṃ, apāyabhūmiṃ asubhaina kammunā|

khayā ca kammassa vimuttacaitasau, nibbanti tai jautirivindhanakkhayā’’||

tattha yaṃ āha ‘‘subhaina kammaina vajanti suggati’’nti, idaṃ kusalaṃ| yaṃ āha ‘‘apāyabhūmiṃ asubhaina kammunā’’ti, idaṃ akusalaṃ| idaṃ kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca|

123. tattha  katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ?

‘‘yathāpi bhamarau pupphaṃ, vaṇṇagandhamahaiṭhayaṃ vaṇṇagandhaṃ ahaiṭhayaṃ (sī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 49|

palaiti paḷaiti (ka॰) rasamādāya, aivaṃ gāmai munī carai’’ti||

idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi, idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, kāyakammavacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau kusalaina parisuddhājīvau| āraddhavīriyau khau pana hauti thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya sacchikiriyāya| pañ‍ñavā khau pana hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhūnaṃ tīṇi karaṇīyānī’’ti| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

‘‘dasayimai dasa imai (sī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 10.48, bhikkhavai, dhammā pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā| katamai dasa? ‘vaivaṇṇiyamhi ajjhupagatau’ti pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ…pai॰… imai khau bhikkhavai dasa dhammā pabbajitaina abhiṇhaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbā’’ti| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, karaṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyasucaritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manausucaritanti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi karaṇīyānī’’ti| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ?

‘‘natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi gausamitaṃ gauṇasamaṃ (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.13 dhanaṃ|

natthi sūriyasamā suriyasamā (sī॰) ābhā, samuddaparamā sarā’’ti||

bhagavā āha –

‘‘natthi  attasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi dhañ‍ñasamaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi pañ‍ñāsamā ābhā, vuṭṭhivaiparamā sarā’’ti||

aittha yaṃ purimakaṃ, idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavaiṛ akaraṇīyāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritanti| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, tīṇi akaraṇīyānī’’ti| idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ|

124. tattha  katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca?

‘‘kiṃsūdha bhītā janatā anaikā, maggau canaikāyatanau pavuttau canaikāyatanappavuttā (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.75)|

pucchāmi taṃ gautama bhūripañ‍ña, kismiṃ ṭhitau paralaukaṃ na bhāyaiti||

‘‘vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya sammā, kāyaina pāpāni akubbamānau|

bahvan‍napānaṃ gharamāvasantau, saddhau mudū saṃvibhāgī vadañ‍ñū|

aitaisu dhammaisu ṭhitau catūsu, dhammai ṭhitau paralaukaṃ na bhāyai’’ti||

tattha yaṃ āha ‘‘vācaṃ manañ‍ca paṇidhāya sammā’’ti, idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| ‘‘kāyaina pāpāni akubbamānau’’ti, idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ| ‘‘bahvan‍napānaṃ gharamāvasantau, saddhau mudū saṃvibhāgī vadañ‍ñū| aitaisu dhammaisu ṭhitau catūsu, dhammai ṭhitau paralaukaṃ na bhāyai’’ti, idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca|

‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudāpanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhānasāsanaṃ’’||

tattha yaṃ āha ‘‘sabbapāpassa akaraṇa’’nti, idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ, yaṃ āha ‘‘kusalassa upasampadā’’ti, idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca|

‘‘kāyasamācārampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| vacīsamācārampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| manausamācārampāhaṃ daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi…pai॰… pariyaisanampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi’’|

‘‘kāyasamācārampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| yathārūpañ‍ca khau kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpau kāyasamācārau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā kāyasamācāraṃ  ‘‘imaṃ idaṃ (ka॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 2.364 khau mai kāyasamācāraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’’ti, aivarūpau kāyasamācārau saivitabbau| ‘‘kāyasamācārampāhaṃ daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’’ti iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| ‘‘vacīsamācāraṃ…pai॰… ‘‘pariyaisanampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’’ti iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ, kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ| yathārūpañ‍ca khau pariyaisanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti, aivarūpā pariyaisanā na saivitabbā| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā pariyaisanaṃ ‘‘imaṃ khau mai pariyaisanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’’ti, aivarūpā pariyaisanā saivitabbā| ‘‘pariyaisanampāhaṃ, daivānaminda, duvidhaina vadāmi saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’’ti iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

tattha yaṃ āha ‘‘saivitabbampī’’ti, idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ| yaṃ āha ‘‘na saivitabbampī’’ti, idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca|

170. tattha  katamau thavau?

‘‘maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau passa dha॰ pa॰ 273 saiṭṭhau, sac‍cānaṃ caturau padā|

virāgau saiṭṭhau dhammānaṃ, dvipadānañ‍ca cakkhumā’’ti||

ayaṃ thavau|

‘‘tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, aggāni| katamāni tīṇi? yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattā apadā vā dvipadā vā catuppadā vā bahuppadā vā rūpinau vā arūpinau vā sañ‍ñinau vā asañ‍ñinau vā naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñinau vā, tathāgatau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati saiṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṃ arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, dhammānaṃ a॰ ni॰ 4.34; itivu॰ 81 passitabbaṃ paṇṇattisaṅkhatānaṃ vā asaṅkhatānaṃ vā, virāgau taisaṃ dhammānaṃ aggamakkhāyati saiṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṃ madanimmadanau…pai॰… niraudhau nibbānaṃ| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, saṅghānaṃ paṇṇatti gaṇānaṃ paṇṇatti mahājanasan‍nipātānaṃ paṇṇatti, tathāgatasāvakasaṅghau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati saiṭṭhamakkhāyati pavaramakkhāyati, yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā…pai॰… puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassāti|

‘‘sabbalaukuttarau  satthā, dhammau ca kusalakkhatau kusalamakkhatau (ka॰)|

gaṇau ca narasīhassa, tāni tīṇi vississarai||

‘‘samaṇapadumasañ‍cayau gaṇau, dhammavarau ca vidūnaṃ sak‍katau|

naravaradamakau ca cakkhumā, tāni tīṇi laukassa uttari||

‘‘satthā ca appaṭisamau, dhammau ca sabbau nirupadāhau|

ariyau ca gaṇavarau, tāni  khalu vississarai tīṇi||

‘‘sac‍canāmau jinau khaimau sabbābhibhū, sac‍cadhammau natthañ‍ñau tassa uttari|

ariyasaṅghau nic‍caṃ viñ‍ñūnaṃ pūjitau, tāni tīṇi laukassa uttari||

‘‘aikāyanaṃ jātikhayantadassī, maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina tariṃsu pubbai, tarissanti yai ca yai cāpi (sī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.384 taranti aughaṃ||

‘‘taṃ tādisaṃ daivamanussasaiṭṭhaṃ|

sattā namassanti visuddhipaikkhā’’ti||

ayaṃ thavauti|

tattha laukiyaṃ suttaṃ dvīhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina ca vāsanābhāgiyaina ca| laukuttaraṃpi suttaṃ tīhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ dassanabhāgiyaina ca bhāvanābhāgiyaina ca asaikkhabhāgiyaina ca| laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca| yasmiṃ suttai yaṃ yaṃ padaṃ dissati saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ vā vāsanābhāgiyaṃ vā, taina taina laukiyanti niddisitabbaṃ, dassanabhāgiyaṃ vā  bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ vā asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ vā yaṃ yaṃ padaṃ dissati taina taina laukuttaranti niddisitabbaṃ|

vāsanābhāgiyaṃ  suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyassa suttassa nigghātāya, dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ vāsanābhāgiyassa suttassa nigghātāya, bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ dassanabhāgiyassa suttassa paṭinissaggāya, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyassa suttassa paṭinissaggāya, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthaṃ|

laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ chabbīsatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ, tai tīhi suttaihi samanvaisitabbā dassanabhāgiyaina bhāvanābhāgiyaina asaikkhabhāgiyaina cāti|

tattha dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ pañ‍cahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ aikabījinā kaulaṃkaulaina sattakkhattuparamaina saddhānusārinā dhammānusārinā cāti, dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ| bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ dvādasahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍naina, sakadāgāminā, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍naina, anāgāminā, antarā parinibbāyinā, upahac‍ca parinibbāyinā, asaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyinā, uddhaṃsautaina akaniṭṭhagāminā, saddhāvimuttaina, diṭṭhippattaina, kāyasakkhinā cāti, bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi dvādasahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ| asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ navahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ saddhāvimuttaina, pañ‍ñāvimuttaina, suñ‍ñatavimuttaina, animittavimuttaina, appaṇihitavimuttaina, ubhataubhāgavimuttaina samasīsinā pac‍caikabuddhasammāsambuddhaihi cāti, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi navahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ| aivaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ imaihi chabbīsatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ|

laukiyaṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ aikūnavīsatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ| tai caritaihi niddiṭṭhā samanvaisitabbā kaici rāgacaritā, kaici dausacaritā, kaici mauhacaritā, kaici rāgacaritā ca dausacaritā ca, kaici rāgacaritā ca mauhacaritā ca, kaici dausacaritā ca mauhacaritā ca, kaici rāgacaritā ca dausacaritā ca mauhacaritā ca, rāgamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau, rāgamukhai ṭhitau dausacaritau, rāgamukhai ṭhitau mauhacaritau, rāgamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau ca dausacaritau ca mauhacaritau ca, dausamukhai ṭhitau dausacaritau, dausamukhai ṭhitau mauhacaritau, dausamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau, dausamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau ca dausacaritau  ca mauhacaritau  ca, mauhamukhai ṭhitau mauhacaritau, mauhamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau mauhamukhai ṭhitau dausacaritau, mauhamukhai ṭhitau rāgacaritau ca dausacaritau ca mauhacaritau cāti, laukiyaṃ suttaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ imaihi aikūnavīsatiyā puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ|

vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ sīlavantaihi niddisitabbaṃ, tai sīlavantau  pañ‍ca puggalā pakatisīlaṃ samādānasīlaṃ cittappasādau samathau vipassanā cāti, vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ imaihi pañ‍cahi puggalaihi niddisitabbaṃ|

laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ tīhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ dassanabhāgiyaina bhāvanābhāgiyaina asaikkhabhāgiyaina ca|

laukiyañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca ubhayaina niddisitabbaṃ, ñāṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya niddisitabbaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaina pañ‍ñābalaina adhipañ‍ñāsikkhāya dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgaina sammādiṭṭhiyā tīraṇāya santīraṇāya dhammai ñāṇaina anvayai ñāṇaina khayai ñāṇaina anuppādai ñāṇaina anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaina añ‍ñindriyaina añ‍ñātāvindriyaina cakkhunā vij‍jāya buddhiyā bhūriyā maidhāya, yaṃ yaṃ vā pana labbhati, taina taina pañ‍ñādhivacanaina niddisitabbaṃ|

ñaiyyaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naihi ajjhattikabāhiraihi hīnappaṇītaihi dūrasantikaihi saṅkhatāsaṅkhataihi kusalākusalābyākataihi saṅkhaipatau vā chahi ārammaṇaihi niddisitabbaṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca tadubhayaina niddisitabbaṃ, pañ‍ñāpi ārammaṇabhūtā ñaiyyaṃ, yaṃ kiñ‍ci ārammaṇabhūtaṃ ajjhattikaṃ vā bāhiraṃ vā, sabbaṃ taṃ saṅkhataina asaṅkhataina ca niddisitabbaṃ|

dassanaṃ bhāvanā dassanā bhāvanā (sī॰) sakavacanaṃ paravacanaṃ visaj‍janīyaṃ avisaj‍janīyaṃ kammaṃ vipākauti sabbattha tadubhayaṃ suttai yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ, tathā upadhārayitvā labbhamānatau niddisitabbaṃ, yaṃ vā pana kiñ‍ci bhagavā añ‍ñataravacanaṃ bhāsati, sabbaṃ taṃ yathāniddiṭṭhaṃ dhārayitabbaṃ|

duvidhau haitu yañ‍ca kammaṃ yai ca kilaisā, samudayau kilaisā| tattha  kilaisā saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina suttaina niddisitabbā| samudayau saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina  ca vāsanābhāgiyaina ca suttaina niddisitabbau| tattha kusalaṃ catūhi suttaihi niddisitabbaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaina dassanabhāgiyaina bhāvanābhāgiyaina asaikkhabhāgiyaina ca| akusalaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina suttaina niddisitabbaṃ| kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca tadubhayaina tadubhayaihi (sī॰) niddisitabbaṃ| anuñ‍ñātaṃ bhagavatau anuñ‍ñātāya niddisitabbaṃ, taṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ saṃvarau pahānaṃ bhāvanā sacchikiriyā kappiyānulaumauti, yaṃ dissati tāsu tāsu bhūmīsu, taṃ kappiyānulaumaina niddisitabbaṃ| paṭikkhittaṃ bhagavatā paṭikkhittakāraṇaina niddisitabbaṃ| anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca tadubhayaina niddisitabbaṃ| thavau pasaṃsāya niddisitabbau| sau pañ‍cavidhaina vaiditabbau bhagavatau dhammassa ariyasaṅghassa ariyadhammānaṃ sikkhāya laukiyaguṇasampattiyāti| aivaṃ thavau pañ‍cavidhaina niddisitabbau|

indriyabhūmi navahi padaihi niddisitabbā, kilaisabhūmi navahi padaihi niddisitabbā, aivamaitāni aṭṭhārasa padāni haunti nava padāni kusalāni nava padāni akusalānīti, tathāhi vuttaṃ ‘‘aṭṭhārasa mūlapadā kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbā, sāsanappaṭṭhānai’’ti| taināha āyasmā mahākac‍cāyanau –

‘‘navahi ca padaihi kusalā, navahi ca yuj‍janti akusalappakkhā|

aitai khalu mūlapadā, bhavanti aṭṭhārasa padānī’’ti||

niyuttaṃ sāsanappaṭṭhānaṃ|


aittāvatā  samattā naitti yā āyasmatā mahākac‍cāyanaina bhāsitā bhagavatā anumauditā mūlasaṅgītiyaṃ saṅgītāti|

naittippakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
